# Code review 2: the GBPixelVfx plugin after the fix batch

## Context
`Glyphbound/Plugins/GBPixelVfx/` bakes a Niagara / Niagara Fluids effect into a pixel-art flipbook in the game's palette (`Glyphbound/Content/Data/Vfx/<Id>.png` + `.json`) and plays it at runtime on a camera-facing quad with a point light driven by a baked light track. Your first review is in `Prompts/pixelvfx_fixes.md` (section with the 18 findings, all accepted). The implementer (Muse Spark) then worked on that brief, and the architect finished the showcase stage by hand. The changes are the working tree against commit `566ad63`:

`git diff 566ad63 -- Glyphbound/Plugins/GBPixelVfx/Source Glyphbound/Plugins/GBPixelVfx/Tools`

## What to review
1. For each of your 18 findings: is it really fixed in the working tree? Answer only for the ones that are NOT fixed, fixed wrongly, or fixed with a new bug. Do not list the ones that are fine.
2. The new code of the batch:
   - `FGBPixelVfxClip::ComputeQuadAndLightPlacement` and `UGBPixelVfxComponent::UpdateQuadTransform` (pivot rule: the point `PivotU/V` of the frame lands on the component's location for every facing mode; light at `AnchorU/V`, 10 cm toward the camera).
   - `RefreshClipMaterial` / `RebuildLight` / `Stop` / `OnComponentDestroyed` (GC safety, re-Play with another clip, no leaked components; a new dynamic material instance per Play: is that a leak or churn problem for a looping spell effect?).

3. Anything in the Runtime module (`Source/GBPixelVfx/**`) that would break or misbehave in the packaged (cooked, Shipping or Development) game: Editor-only API, paths, the materials `/Game/Glyphbound/Materials/M_PixelVfxSprite*` not being cooked when nothing references them by hard reference (they are loaded with `StaticLoadObject` by path; `DefaultGame.ini` has `DirectoriesToAlwaysCook` for `/Game/Glyphbound/Materials`: confirm that is enough), the loose `Content/Data/Vfx` files (`DirectoriesToAlwaysStageAsNonUFS=(Path="Data")`), the `pvfx.Spawn` console command in Shipping.

# Task: GBPixelVfx, second batch (fixes after the architect's look at the results and the code review)

You are the implementer. Same rules as `Prompts/pixelvfx_implement.md` (read its sections "File ownership", "Iteration rule" and "Final report" again: they apply unchanged). You own only `Glyphbound/Plugins/GBPixelVfx/**` except `Bake/Jobs/*.json`. Build, test and run only through `Tools/Jam/PixelVfx-Jam.ps1` (no `-Project` argument needed any more). Do not commit.

State: the first batch works end to end for bakes (FluidFire, FluidSmoke, FluidExplosion, FluidArcaneFire bake cleanly; the architect tuned the job files since, so the clips on disk are newer than the first batch's). The showcase did not show the clips. This batch fixes the runtime, improves the conversion and finishes the showcase and compare modes.

Work through the items IN ORDER. After each group: `-Action Build`, then the check named in the group. Budget: if one item fights you for more than 15 minutes, write down what you tried, leave it, and go on to the next one.

## A. Runtime component (`Source/GBPixelVfx`)
A1. **Pivot and light anchor are applied in the wrong space** (`UGBPixelVfxComponent::UpdateQuadTransform`). `PivotWorldOffset()` is in the quad's own XY (X = U to the right, Y = V up), but it is passed to `SetRelativeLocation`, which is in the parent's space and is not rotated with the quad. For `Upright` and `Billboard` the sprite ends up centered on the component (half under the floor) and shifted sideways. Correct rule: compute the quad's world rotation `R` first, using the COMPONENT's world location (not the quad's) for the direction to the camera; then quad world location = `ComponentLocation - R.RotateVector(PivotLocal)` with `PivotLocal = ((PivotU - 0.5) * W, (0.5 - PivotV) * H, 0)`; light world location = `ComponentLocation + R.RotateVector(((AnchorU - PivotU) * W, (PivotV - AnchorV) * H, 0))` plus 10 cm toward the camera (along R's Z axis) so the light is not inside the quad plane. Set world location and rotation of the quad and the light directly (`SetWorldLocationAndRotation`). `Ground` and `Fixed` use the component's own rotation as `R` (Ground: the quad lies flat in the component's XY plane, facing +Z).
A2. Put that math in a pure static function in `GBPixelVfxTypes.h` (inputs: clip, size scale, component location, rotation R; outputs: quad location, light location) and add two tests: for an upright quad (R such that local Y = world up) with `PivotV = 1` the quad center is exactly `H / 2` above the component location and not displaced horizontally; the light for `AnchorV = 0.5, PivotV = 1` is `H / 2` above the component.
A3. Verify the sprite is neither mirrored nor upside down. Do it for real, not by reasoning about the plane's UVs: add a temporary test clip (an 8x8 atlas with a known asymmetric mark, e.g. a red pixel block in the top-left corner) or use the showcase close shot of FluidSmoke (the fire is at the BOTTOM of that clip) and look at the screenshot.
A4. Any finding listed under "Review findings" below that touches the runtime module.

## B. Conversion (`GBPixelVfxConvert`), each with a test that can fail
B1. **Color clusters** (new setting `MinColorClusterPixels`, job key `Pixel.MinColorClusterPixels`, default 0 = off): after islands / holes and before the outline, every 4-connected region of solid pixels with the same ramp and index that is smaller than N pixels is recolored with the most common color among the solid pixels 4-adjacent to the region (ties: the darker one). One pass, regions found on the state before the pass. This removes single-pixel color specks inside the flames: a pixel artist paints clusters, a filter leaves salt and pepper.
B2. **Fade-out for one-shot clips** (new settings `FadeOutFrames`, default 0, and `FadeOutMode` "Dissolve" | "Erode", job keys under `Pixel`): only when `Loop` is false. Over the last `FadeOutFrames` frames, progress `t` goes from 1/(N+1) to N/(N+1). "Dissolve": a solid pixel is removed when `Bayer4x4(x, y) < t` (the classic 4x4 ordered matrix normalized to 0..1; the pattern is fixed in frame space so removed pixels never come back). "Erode": remove every solid pixel within `ceil(t * MaxDepth)` 4-connected steps of a transparent pixel or the frame edge, where `MaxDepth` is the largest such distance in that frame. Applied after color clusters, before the outline. The light track is computed after the fade (a faded frame emits less).
B3. Review images: the cells of `sheet.png` and `frame_big.png` must be opaque with a dark background `#16161C` (today the cells are transparent, so viewers show them white and fire is hard to judge); gutters stay `#3A3A3A`.
B4. Any finding listed under "Review findings" below that touches the conversion or its tests.
Check: `-Action Test` green, then `-Action Bake -Id All` and look at the four `sheet.png`.

C2. Stage: the fill directional light must not use distance-field shadows (`bUseRayTracedDistanceFieldShadows = false`, and `SetCastShadows(false)` is fine for the fill: the clip lights are the ones that must cast shadows). Give the stage a fixed manual exposure so dark scenes are not auto-exposed: a post-process on the camera (`AutoExposureMethod = AEM_Manual`, `AutoExposureBias` chosen so the lit floor is readable and the sprites are not blown out; override flags set) and bloom at default.
C3. Camera: spawn the `ACameraActor` with its final transform in the spawn call, set it as view target with `SetViewTargetWithBlend(Camera, 0)`, and log its real location and the view target's name. Lay the stage out along world Y: clips at X = 0, Y = -900, -300, 300, 900 (more clips: keep 600 cm spacing, center the row), back wall at X = +500, obstacles (boxes / cylinders 80 to 250 cm tall) at X = +250 between the clips and the wall and a few at X = -200 beside the clips, camera at X = -1400, Z = 350, looking at (0, 0, 180). Close shots: camera 600 cm in front of each clip at Z = 200.
C4. After A1 the clips stand on the floor at their pivot. Check in the screenshots: each sprite upright, base on the floor, fire-colored light on the floor and the wall, shadows of the obstacles cast away from the fire clips.
C5. Compare mode as specified in the first brief, with the notes' rules (wall clock, caps off, warm-up, baked population includes its lights). If the source Niagara system does not show in the main view, say so in the report with the log lines; the timing numbers are still valid only if the simulation runs, so also log the source component's `IsActive()` and its system instance tick count or age.
Check: `-Action Showcase` (look at every screenshot yourself), `-Action Compare -Id FluidFire`.

## Review findings (Sol 6.1) and the architect's decision on each
All 18 findings are ACCEPTED: fix every one, in this batch (full text below, verbatim from the reviewer). Architect's notes on some of them:
- 1: this is item A1 above; use the reviewer's formulation (rotation first, then `Quad = Component - L*p`, `Light = Quad + L*a`), keep the 10 cm push of the light toward the camera.
- 3: implement `OnComponentDestroyed` (or `DestroyComponent` override, whichever 5.9 offers) that calls `Stop()`; also `EndPlay`.
- 4: lit variant emissive = color * Glow (unlit stays color * (1 + Glow)). Re-run `-Action Materials` after changing the script.
- 8: include the projected source origin in the auto-frame box; never clamp the pivot.
- 12: code defaults = the notes (MinIslandPixels 0, FillHoles false, MinColorClusterPixels 0, FadeOutFrames 0); a job without `Ramps` uses one default ramp (the fire ramp of FluidFire) and logs a Warning.
- 13 to 18 (tests): strengthen the named tests as described. For 18 add at most three small tests (percentile peak, light-track energy and anchor, un-premultiply with fractional opacity). Keep the whole plugin suite under 3 seconds.

1. **MAJOR â€” GBPixelVfxComponent.cpp:252,317:** Confirmed: pivot and light offsets are expressed in quad XY but applied in the parentâ€™s space. An Upright fire with PivotV=0.94 shifts sideways and puts its light beside the flame. Choose rotation first; with the quadâ€™s final world linear transform `L` and mesh-local pivot/anchor points `p/a`, use `QuadWorldLocation = ComponentWorldLocation - L*p` and `LightWorldLocation = QuadWorldLocation + L*a`.

2. **MAJOR â€” GBPixelVfxComponent.cpp:95:** Atlas, material variant, and Glow are assigned only when creating the quad. Calling `Play(FluidArcaneFire)` after `Play(FluidFire)` without `Stop()` keeps the fire atlas, while switching to a lit clip keeps the unlit material. Refresh clip-dependent material state on every successful Play.

3. **MAJOR â€” GBPixelVfxComponent.cpp:105; GBPixelVfxComponent.h:64:** The spawned mesh/light belong to the owner actor, but destroying just the VFX component never calls Stop. Removing a looping VFX component from a surviving actor leaves its detached sprite and light registered; UEâ€™s scene-component destruction reparents/detaches children rather than destroying them. Override destruction to explicitly destroy both spawned components.

4. **MAJOR â€” Tools/make_pixelvfx_material.py:193,201:** Both material variants use emissive `color*(1+Glow)`. A lit smoke clip with Glow=0 therefore still emits its full color in complete darkness. Connect `color*Glow` to emissive for the lit variant.

5. **MAJOR â€” GBPixelVfxConvert.cpp:64:** Nearest matching tone-maps palette candidates as well as the captured color, changing the target paletteâ€™s positions in OKLab. With Peak=1, white input, and palette black/#BCBCBC/white, this selects white instead of the grey nearest the tone-mapped input. Convert palette colors directly from sRGB to linear and then OKLab; tone-map only the input.

6. **MAJOR â€” GBPixelVfxBakeGameMode_Bake.cpp:273,467:** The â€œfull sceneâ€ fallback retains `PRM_UseShowOnlyList` while omitting its sole component. UEâ€™s `GetShowOnlyAndHiddenComponents` consequently creates an empty visibility set, so an empty first capture guarantees another empty capture. Set `PRM_LegacySceneCapture` when `bLegacyCapture` is true.

7. **MAJOR â€” GBPixelVfxBakeGameMode_Bake.cpp:853,887,901:** These failure branches increment BakeJobIndex after BakeLogAndCleanup already increments it. During an All bake, a system-load, readiness, or readback failure silently skips the following job. Advance the index in exactly one place.

8. **MAJOR â€” GBPixelVfxBakeGameMode_Bake.cpp:649:** Clamping the projected pivot silently breaks alignment when auto-framing excludes the source origin. If the visible plume starts 100 cm above its emitter, clamping its below-frame pivot makes runtime playback start at the emitter instead. Include the origin in the framing bounds before fitting the aspect ratio, so the stored pivot remains valid without clamping.

9. **MINOR â€” GBPixelVfxComponent.cpp:270:** Ground uses zero relative rotation, so it inherits the componentâ€™s rotation. A Ground clip spawned on an actor pitched 90 degrees becomes vertical. Set its world rotation so its normal remains world +Z.

10. **MINOR â€” GBPixelVfxBakeGameMode_Bake.cpp:439:** Auto-framing evaluates luminance before applying Capture.Exposure, whereas conversion evaluates it afterward. Raising exposure can make outer pixels visible in the final pass after the prepass has cropped them away. Apply the same exposure when calculating the prepass alpha rule.

11. **MINOR â€” GBPixelVfxBakeGameMode_Bake.cpp:374:** Rounding one capture stride changes animation speed for non-divisible rates. SimFps=60/Fps=24 captures every three simulation frames, recording 20 fps while declaring 24 fps. Schedule each sample independently with `WarmupFrames + Round(i*SimFps/Fps)`.

12. **MINOR â€” GBPixelVfxBakeJob.cpp:229; GBPixelVfxBakeJob.h:38,43,46:** Optional defaults diverge from the job contract: omitted Ramps produces a failed bake, omitted levels/alpha values differ from FluidFire, and MinIslandPixels defaults to 2 despite the notes requiring cleanup off. Initialize the required defaults and preserve default ramps when the array is absent.

13. **MINOR â€” GBPixelVfxConvertTests.cpp:121:** RampGradient passes `floor(v*(N-1))`, despite that implementation placing band boundaries incorrectly. Assert exact indices immediately below, at, and above several `k/N` boundaries.

14. **MINOR â€” GBPixelVfxConvertTests.cpp:159:** SplitRoutingâ€™s hot pixel exceeds both hot thresholds, so replacing the required OR with AND still passes. Add separate luminance-only and saturation-only hot pixels, and assert independently calculated hot/cold levels.

15. **MINOR â€” GBPixelVfxConvertTests.cpp:369:** LoopBlend passes an implementation that blends only frame 0 or leaves opacity untouched: frame 1 is unexamined and every opacity equals 1. Use distinct extra frames and opacities, then assert both blended frames and the non-loop case.

16. **MINOR â€” GBPixelVfxConvertTests.cpp:259:** IslandsAndHoles passes a hole filler that fills every transparent pixel and always copies its first neighbor. The fixture has just one transparent pixel and identical neighbor colors. Add an unfillable gap and a hole whose majority color differs from its first neighbor.

17. **MINOR â€” GBPixelVfxConvertTests.cpp:398,417:** PlaybackHelpers passes a one-shot implementation that finishes when the last frame begins, and serialization that replaces every track value with 1. Assert the last frame remains unfinished until `FrameCount/Fps`, and compare every round-tripped track entry.

18. **MINOR â€” GBPixelVfxConvertTests.cpp:61:** No tests exercise percentile selection, fractional-opacity un-premultiplication, light-track energy/centroids, or hysteresis across disappearance and rapid coverage changes. Implementations using maximum brightness, multiplying RGB by alpha again, returning constant lighting, or retaining disappeared pixels can escape the suite. Add small fixtures with hand-calculated peaks, colors, normalized energies, anchors, and solid masks.

Fix first tonight: **1 (pivot/light transforms), 2 (clip switching), 3 (component destruction cleanup).**

// UGBPixelVfxComponent: quad + baked light track playback.
#include "GBPixelVfxComponent.h"

#include "Camera/PlayerCameraManager.h"
#include "Components/PointLightComponent.h"
#include "Components/StaticMeshComponent.h"
#include "Components/PointLightComponent.h"
#include "Components/StaticMeshComponent.h"
#include "Engine/StaticMesh.h"
#include "Engine/Texture2D.h"
#include "GBPixelVfxSubsystem.h"
#include "Kismet/GameplayStatics.h"
#include "Materials/MaterialInstanceDynamic.h"

namespace GBPixelVfxComponentLocal
{
	// Quad mesh: the engine plane (100 x 100 cm in XY, normal +Z, two triangles). The sprite material is
	// two-sided, so the back of the quad shows the sprite mirrored.
	const TCHAR* MeshPath = TEXT("/Engine/BasicShapes/Plane.Plane");
	const TCHAR* SpriteMaterialPath = TEXT("/Game/Glyphbound/Materials/M_PixelVfxSprite.M_PixelVfxSprite");
	const TCHAR* LitMaterialPath = TEXT("/Game/Glyphbound/Materials/M_PixelVfxSpriteLit.M_PixelVfxSpriteLit");
	const TCHAR* FallbackMaterialPath = TEXT("/Engine/BasicShapes/BasicShapeMaterial.BasicShapeMaterial");
}

UGBPixelVfxComponent::UGBPixelVfxComponent()
{
	PrimaryComponentTick.bCanEverTick = true;
	PrimaryComponentTick.bStartWithTickEnabled = true;
}

bool UGBPixelVfxComponent::Play(FName ClipId, bool bRestart)
{
	if (!bRestart && bPlaying && CurrentClipId == ClipId && CurrentClip.IsValid())
	{
		return true;
	}
	UGameInstance* GameInstance = nullptr;
	if (UWorld* World = GetWorld())
	{
		GameInstance = World->GetGameInstance();
	}
	UGBPixelVfxSubsystem* Subsystem = GameInstance ? GameInstance->GetSubsystem<UGBPixelVfxSubsystem>() : nullptr;
	if (!Subsystem)
	{
		UE_LOG(LogGBPixelVfx, Warning, TEXT("PixelVfx Play '%s': no subsystem."), *ClipId.ToString());
		return false;
	}
	const FGBPixelVfxClip* Found = Subsystem->FindClip(ClipId);
	if (!Found)
	{
		return false;
	}
	UTexture2D* Atlas = Subsystem->GetAtlas(ClipId);
	if (!Atlas)
	{
		return false;
	}
	CurrentClip = MakeShared<FGBPixelVfxClip>(*Found);
	CurrentClipId = ClipId;
	if (!bLightCastsShadowsSet)
	{
		bLightCastsShadows = CurrentClip->Light.bCastShadows;
	}
	PlayTime = StartTimeOffset;
	CurrentFrame = -1;
	bPlaying = true;
	EnsureBuilt();
	UpdateFrame(true);
	return true;
}

void UGBPixelVfxComponent::Stop()
{
	bPlaying = false;
	if (ClipLight)
	{
		ClipLight->DestroyComponent();
		ClipLight = nullptr;
	}
	if (Quad)
	{
		Quad->DestroyComponent();
		Quad = nullptr;
	}
	QuadMaterial = nullptr;
}

void UGBPixelVfxComponent::EnsureBuilt()
{
	if (!CurrentClip.IsValid())
	{
		return;
	}
	if (!Quad)
	{
		UStaticMesh* PlaneMesh = Cast<UStaticMesh>(StaticLoadObject(UStaticMesh::StaticClass(), nullptr,
			GBPixelVfxComponentLocal::MeshPath));
		if (!PlaneMesh)
		{
			UE_LOG(LogGBPixelVfx, Warning, TEXT("PixelVfx: cannot load the quad mesh %s."),
				GBPixelVfxComponentLocal::MeshPath);
			return;
		}
		Quad = NewObject<UStaticMeshComponent>(GetOwner() ? static_cast<UObject*>(GetOwner()) : static_cast<UObject*>(this), NAME_None, RF_Transient);
		Quad->SetMobility(EComponentMobility::Movable);
		Quad->AttachToComponent(this, FAttachmentTransformRules::KeepRelativeTransform);
		Quad->SetStaticMesh(PlaneMesh);
		Quad->SetCollisionEnabled(ECollisionEnabled::NoCollision);
		Quad->SetCastShadow(false);
		Quad->SetVisibleInRayTracing(true);
		Quad->SetAffectDynamicIndirectLighting(true);
		Quad->bVisibleInReflectionCaptures = true;
		// Final size is known now (clip + SizeScale): set it before registering so the
		// initial render bounds are correct.
		Quad->SetRelativeScale3D(FVector(
			FMath::Max(0.01f, CurrentClip->WorldWidthCm * SizeScale / 100.f),
			FMath::Max(0.01f, CurrentClip->WorldHeightCm * SizeScale / 100.f), 1.f));
		Quad->RegisterComponent();
		Quad->MarkRenderStateDirty();
	}
	RefreshClipMaterial();
	RebuildLight();
}

void UGBPixelVfxComponent::RefreshClipMaterial()
{
	// Clip-dependent material state is refreshed on every successful Play so that
	// switching clips without Stop() picks up the new atlas, variant and glow.
	if (!Quad || !CurrentClip.IsValid())
	{
		return;
	}
	const TCHAR* MaterialPath = CurrentClip->bLit
		? GBPixelVfxComponentLocal::LitMaterialPath
		: GBPixelVfxComponentLocal::SpriteMaterialPath;
	UMaterialInterface* Base = Cast<UMaterialInterface>(StaticLoadObject(UMaterialInterface::StaticClass(),
		nullptr, MaterialPath));
	if (!Base && !bQuadMissingMaterialWarned)
	{
		bQuadMissingMaterialWarned = true;
		UE_LOG(LogGBPixelVfx, Warning, TEXT("PixelVfx: material %s is missing, falling back to %s."),
			MaterialPath, GBPixelVfxComponentLocal::FallbackMaterialPath);
		Base = Cast<UMaterialInterface>(StaticLoadObject(UMaterialInterface::StaticClass(),
			nullptr, GBPixelVfxComponentLocal::FallbackMaterialPath));
	}
	if (!Base)
	{
		return;
	}
	QuadMaterial = UMaterialInstanceDynamic::Create(Base, this);
	UGBPixelVfxSubsystem* Subsystem = nullptr;
	if (UWorld* World = GetWorld())
	{
		if (UGameInstance* GameInstance = World->GetGameInstance())
		{
			Subsystem = GameInstance->GetSubsystem<UGBPixelVfxSubsystem>();
		}
	}
	UTexture2D* Atlas = Subsystem ? Subsystem->GetAtlas(CurrentClipId) : nullptr;
	if (Atlas)
	{
		QuadMaterial->SetTextureParameterValue(TEXT("Atlas"), Atlas);
	}
	QuadMaterial->SetVectorParameterValue(TEXT("Tint"), FLinearColor::White);
	QuadMaterial->SetScalarParameterValue(TEXT("Glow"), CurrentClip->Glow);
	if (Quad)
	{
		Quad->SetMaterial(0, QuadMaterial);
	}
}

void UGBPixelVfxComponent::RebuildLight()
{
	const bool bWantsLight = CurrentClip.IsValid() && CurrentClip->Light.bEnabled && bEnableLight;
	if (!bWantsLight)
	{
		if (ClipLight)
		{
			ClipLight->DestroyComponent();
			ClipLight = nullptr;
		}
		return;
	}
	if (!ClipLight)
	{
		ClipLight = NewObject<UPointLightComponent>(GetOwner() ? static_cast<UObject*>(GetOwner()) : static_cast<UObject*>(this), NAME_None, RF_Transient);
		ClipLight->SetMobility(EComponentMobility::Movable);
		ClipLight->SetIntensityUnits(ELightUnits::Candelas);
		ClipLight->AttachToComponent(this, FAttachmentTransformRules::KeepRelativeTransform);
		ClipLight->RegisterComponent();
	}
	ClipLight->SetLightColor(CurrentClip->Light.Color);
	ClipLight->SetAttenuationRadius(CurrentClip->Light.RadiusCm * FMath::Max(0.01f, SizeScale));
	ClipLight->SetCastShadows(bLightCastsShadows);
}

void UGBPixelVfxComponent::UpdateFrame(bool bForce)
{
	if (!bPlaying || !CurrentClip.IsValid())
	{
		return;
	}
	bool bOutFinished = false;
	const int32 Frame = FGBPixelVfxClip::FrameAtTime(*CurrentClip, PlayTime, bOutFinished);
	if (bOutFinished)
	{
		Stop();
		if (bAutoDestroyOwnerWhenFinished && GetOwner())
		{
			GetOwner()->Destroy();
		}
		return;
	}
	if (Frame == CurrentFrame && !bForce)
	{
		return;
	}
	CurrentFrame = Frame;
	const FVector4f Rect = FGBPixelVfxClip::FrameRect(*CurrentClip, Frame);
	if (QuadMaterial)
	{
		// The frame rect rides as four scalars (see make_pixelvfx_material.py): masks on a
		// uniform that feeds the texture UVs fail to compile, so there is no vector swizzle.
		// The Plane mesh is assumed to run U along +X and V along +Y with V = 0 at the bottom;
		// UE samples (0, 0) at the image top-left, so V is flipped here.
		QuadMaterial->SetScalarParameterValue(TEXT("FrameU"), Rect.X);
		QuadMaterial->SetScalarParameterValue(TEXT("FrameV"), Rect.Y + Rect.W);
		QuadMaterial->SetScalarParameterValue(TEXT("FrameW"), Rect.Z);
		QuadMaterial->SetScalarParameterValue(TEXT("FrameH"), -Rect.W);
	}
	if (ClipLight && CurrentClip->Light.bEnabled)
	{
		float TrackValue = 1.f;
		if (CurrentClip->Light.TrackMode.Equals(TEXT("Constant"), ESearchCase::IgnoreCase)
			|| CurrentClip->Light.Track.Num() != CurrentClip->FrameCount)
		{
			TrackValue = 1.f;
		}
		else if (CurrentClip->Light.Track.IsValidIndex(Frame))
		{
			TrackValue = CurrentClip->Light.Track[Frame];
		}
		ClipLight->SetIntensity(CurrentClip->Light.Intensity * TrackValue * LightIntensityScale);
	}
}

FVector UGBPixelVfxComponent::PivotWorldOffset() const
{
	// Quad-local position of the pivot point; the quad is shifted so it lands on the component.
	if (!CurrentClip.IsValid())
	{
		return FVector::ZeroVector;
	}
	const float W = CurrentClip->WorldWidthCm * SizeScale;
	const float H = CurrentClip->WorldHeightCm * SizeScale;
	return FVector((CurrentClip->PivotU - 0.5f) * W, (0.5f - CurrentClip->PivotV) * H, 0.f);
}

void UGBPixelVfxComponent::UpdateQuadTransform()
{
	if (!Quad || !CurrentClip.IsValid())
	{
		return;
	}
	// Quad size follows the clip and SizeScale (set before RegisterComponent too).
	const float W = CurrentClip->WorldWidthCm * SizeScale;
	const float H = CurrentClip->WorldHeightCm * SizeScale;
	Quad->SetRelativeScale3D(FVector(FMath::Max(0.01f, W / 100.f), FMath::Max(0.01f, H / 100.f), 1.f));

	EGBPixelVfxFacing Facing = CurrentClip->Facing;
	if (!FacingOverride.IsNone())
	{
		const FString Text = FacingOverride.ToString();
		if (Text.Equals(TEXT("Billboard"), ESearchCase::IgnoreCase)) Facing = EGBPixelVfxFacing::Billboard;
		else if (Text.Equals(TEXT("Upright"), ESearchCase::IgnoreCase)) Facing = EGBPixelVfxFacing::Upright;
		else if (Text.Equals(TEXT("Ground"), ESearchCase::IgnoreCase)) Facing = EGBPixelVfxFacing::Ground;
		else if (Text.Equals(TEXT("Fixed"), ESearchCase::IgnoreCase)) Facing = EGBPixelVfxFacing::Fixed;
	}

	const FVector CompLoc = GetComponentLocation();
	FQuat R = GetComponentQuat();
	if (Facing == EGBPixelVfxFacing::Fixed)
	{
		R = GetComponentQuat();
	}
	else if (Facing == EGBPixelVfxFacing::Ground)
	{
		// The quad stays flat (normal = world +Z) while following the component's yaw.
		const FRotator CompRot = GetComponentQuat().Rotator();
		R = FRotator(0.f, CompRot.Yaw, 0.f).Quaternion();
	}
	else
	{
		FVector CamPos = FVector::ZeroVector;
		FVector CamUp = FVector::UpVector;
		bool bHasCamera = false;
		if (UWorld* World = GetWorld())
		{
			if (APlayerCameraManager* Manager = UGameplayStatics::GetPlayerCameraManager(World, 0))
			{
				CamPos = Manager->GetCameraLocation();
				CamUp = Manager->GetCameraRotation().RotateVector(FVector::UpVector);
				bHasCamera = true;
			}
		}
		if (bHasCamera)
		{
			// The direction uses the COMPONENT location (not the quad's): the pivot
			// must land on the component for every facing mode.
			FVector ToCam = CamPos - CompLoc;
			if (Facing == EGBPixelVfxFacing::Upright)
			{
				ToCam.Z = 0.f;
				CamUp = FVector::UpVector;
			}
			if (ToCam.SizeSquared() > 1.f)
			{
				const FVector ZAxis = ToCam.GetSafeNormal();
				FVector XAxis = FVector::CrossProduct(CamUp, ZAxis);
				if (XAxis.SizeSquared() < 1.e-6f)
				{
					XAxis = FVector(1.f, 0.f, 0.f);
				}
				else
				{
					XAxis.Normalize();
				}
				const FVector YAxis = FVector::CrossProduct(ZAxis, XAxis);
				R = FMatrix(XAxis, YAxis, ZAxis, FVector::ZeroVector).Rotator().Quaternion();
			}
			else
			{
				R = Quad->GetComponentQuat();
			}
		}
		else
		{
			// No player camera: keep the last rotation.
			R = Quad->GetComponentQuat();
		}
	}

	FVector QuadLoc = CompLoc;
	FVector LightLoc = CompLoc;
	FGBPixelVfxClip::ComputeQuadAndLightPlacement(*CurrentClip, SizeScale, CompLoc, R, QuadLoc, LightLoc);
	Quad->SetWorldLocationAndRotation(QuadLoc, R);

	if (ClipLight)
	{
		ClipLight->SetWorldLocation(LightLoc);
	}
}

void UGBPixelVfxComponent::TickComponent(float DeltaTime, ELevelTick TickType,
	FActorComponentTickFunction* ThisTickFunction)
{
	Super::TickComponent(DeltaTime, TickType, ThisTickFunction);
	if (!bPlaying || !CurrentClip.IsValid())
	{
		return;
	}
	PlayTime += DeltaTime * PlayRate;
	UpdateFrame(false);
	UpdateQuadTransform();
}

void UGBPixelVfxComponent::GetQuadWorldBounds(FVector& OutOrigin, FVector& OutExtent) const
{
	OutOrigin = FVector::ZeroVector;
	OutExtent = FVector::ZeroVector;
	if (Quad && Quad->GetStaticMesh())
	{
		const FBoxSphereBounds MeshBounds = Quad->GetStaticMesh()->GetBounds();
		OutOrigin = Quad->GetComponentTransform().TransformPosition(MeshBounds.Origin);
		OutExtent = Quad->GetComponentTransform().TransformVector(MeshBounds.BoxExtent);
	}
}

void UGBPixelVfxComponent::EndPlay(const EEndPlayReason::Type EndPlayReason)
{
	Stop();
	Super::EndPlay(EndPlayReason);
}

void UGBPixelVfxComponent::OnComponentDestroyed(bool bDestroyingHierarchy)
{
	Stop();
	Super::OnComponentDestroyed(bDestroyingHierarchy);
}

 succeeded in 375ms:
// FGBPixelVfxClip: JSON read/write plus the pure playback helpers (unit-testable, no engine objects).
#include "GBPixelVfxTypes.h"

#include "Dom/JsonObject.h"
#include "Dom/JsonValue.h"
#include "Serialization/JsonReader.h"
#include "Serialization/JsonSerializer.h"

namespace GBPixelVfxClipLocal
{
	EGBPixelVfxFacing FacingFromString(const FString& Text)
	{
		if (Text.Equals(TEXT("Billboard"), ESearchCase::IgnoreCase))
		{
			return EGBPixelVfxFacing::Billboard;
		}
		if (Text.Equals(TEXT("Ground"), ESearchCase::IgnoreCase))
		{
			return EGBPixelVfxFacing::Ground;
		}
		if (Text.Equals(TEXT("Fixed"), ESearchCase::IgnoreCase))
		{
			return EGBPixelVfxFacing::Fixed;
		}
		return EGBPixelVfxFacing::Upright;
	}

	FString FacingToString(EGBPixelVfxFacing Facing)
	{
		switch (Facing)
		{
		case EGBPixelVfxFacing::Billboard: return TEXT("Billboard");
		case EGBPixelVfxFacing::Ground: return TEXT("Ground");
		case EGBPixelVfxFacing::Fixed: return TEXT("Fixed");
		default: return TEXT("Upright");
		}
	}

	int32 HexDigit(TCHAR C)
	{
		if (C >= TEXT('0') && C <= TEXT('9')) return C - TEXT('0');
		if (C >= TEXT('a') && C <= TEXT('f')) return 10 + C - TEXT('a');
		if (C >= TEXT('A') && C <= TEXT('F')) return 10 + C - TEXT('A');
		return -1;
	}

	// "#RRGGBB" (the '#' is optional) -> linear color. Returns white when the text is broken.
	FLinearColor ColorFromHex(const FString& Hex)
	{
		FString Text = Hex;
		Text.TrimStartAndEndInline();
		if (Text.StartsWith(TEXT("#")))
		{
			Text.RightChopInline(1);
		}
		if (Text.Len() != 6)
		{
			return FLinearColor::White;
		}
		uint8 Bytes[3] = { 255, 255, 255 };
		for (int32 i = 0; i < 3; ++i)
		{
			const int32 Hi = HexDigit(Text[i * 2]);
			const int32 Lo = HexDigit(Text[i * 2 + 1]);
			if (Hi < 0 || Lo < 0)
			{
				return FLinearColor::White;
			}
			Bytes[i] = static_cast<uint8>(Hi * 16 + Lo);
		}
		return FLinearColor(FColor(Bytes[0], Bytes[1], Bytes[2]));
	}

	FString ColorToHex(const FLinearColor& Color)
	{
		const FColor Srgb = Color.ToFColor(true);
		return FString::Printf(TEXT("#%02X%02X%02X"), Srgb.R, Srgb.G, Srgb.B);
	}
}

FVector4f FGBPixelVfxClip::FrameRect(const FGBPixelVfxClip& Clip, int32 FrameIndex)
{
	if (Clip.Columns <= 0 || Clip.FrameWidth <= 0 || Clip.FrameHeight <= 0 || Clip.Rows() <= 0)
	{
		return FVector4f(0.f, 0.f, 1.f, 1.f);
	}
	const int32 Clamped = FMath::Clamp(FrameIndex, 0, Clip.FrameCount - 1);
	const float AtlasW = static_cast<float>(Clip.Columns * Clip.FrameWidth);
	const float AtlasH = static_cast<float>(Clip.Rows() * Clip.FrameHeight);
	const int32 Col = Clamped % Clip.Columns;
	const int32 Row = Clamped / Clip.Columns;
	return FVector4f(
		Col * Clip.FrameWidth / AtlasW,
		Row * Clip.FrameHeight / AtlasH,
		Clip.FrameWidth / AtlasW,
		Clip.FrameHeight / AtlasH);
}

int32 FGBPixelVfxClip::FrameAtTime(const FGBPixelVfxClip& Clip, float Seconds, bool& bOutFinished)
{
	bOutFinished = false;
	if (Clip.FrameCount <= 0 || Clip.Fps <= 0)
	{
		bOutFinished = true;
		return 0;
	}
	const int32 Stepped = FMath::FloorToInt(Seconds * static_cast<float>(Clip.Fps));
	if (Clip.bLoop)
	{
		const int32 Wrapped = Stepped % Clip.FrameCount;
		return Wrapped >= 0 ? Wrapped : Wrapped + Clip.FrameCount;
	}
	// One-shots show every frame for exactly one frame duration: the last frame is
	// not finished until FrameCount / Fps seconds have elapsed.
	if (Stepped >= Clip.FrameCount)
	{
		bOutFinished = true;
		return Clip.FrameCount - 1;
	}
	return FMath::Max(0, Stepped);
}

void FGBPixelVfxClip::ComputeQuadAndLightPlacement(const FGBPixelVfxClip& Clip, float SizeScale,
	const FVector& ComponentLocation, const FQuat& R,
	FVector& OutQuadLocation, FVector& OutLightLocation)
{
	const float W = Clip.WorldWidthCm * SizeScale;
	const float H = Clip.WorldHeightCm * SizeScale;
	// Quad-local offset (cm) of the pivot point from the quad center.
	const FVector PivotLocal((Clip.PivotU - 0.5f) * W, (0.5f - Clip.PivotV) * H, 0.f);
	// Quad-local offset (cm) from the pivot point to the light anchor.
	const FVector AnchorOffset((Clip.Light.AnchorU - Clip.PivotU) * W,
		(Clip.PivotV - Clip.Light.AnchorV) * H, 0.f);
	OutQuadLocation = ComponentLocation - R.RotateVector(PivotLocal);
	// 10 cm toward the camera (along the quad normal) so the light is not inside the quad plane.
	OutLightLocation = ComponentLocation + R.RotateVector(AnchorOffset)
		+ R.RotateVector(FVector(0.f, 0.f, 10.f));
}

bool FGBPixelVfxClip::LoadFromJsonString(const FString& Text)
{
	TSharedPtr<FJsonObject> Root;
	const TSharedRef<TJsonReader<>> Reader = TJsonReaderFactory<>::Create(Text);
	if (!FJsonSerializer::Deserialize(Reader, Root) || !Root.IsValid())
	{
		return false;
	}

	FGBPixelVfxClip Fresh;
	FString IdText;
	if (Root->TryGetStringField(TEXT("Id"), IdText))
	{
		Fresh.Id = FName(*IdText);
	}
	Root->TryGetStringField(TEXT("Atlas"), Fresh.AtlasFile);
	Root->TryGetNumberField(TEXT("FrameWidth"), Fresh.FrameWidth);
	Root->TryGetNumberField(TEXT("FrameHeight"), Fresh.FrameHeight);
	Root->TryGetNumberField(TEXT("Columns"), Fresh.Columns);
	Root->TryGetNumberField(TEXT("FrameCount"), Fresh.FrameCount);
	Root->TryGetNumberField(TEXT("Fps"), Fresh.Fps);
	Fresh.FrameWidth = FMath::Max(0, Fresh.FrameWidth);
	Fresh.FrameHeight = FMath::Max(0, Fresh.FrameHeight);
	Fresh.Columns = FMath::Max(0, Fresh.Columns);
	Fresh.FrameCount = FMath::Max(0, Fresh.FrameCount);
	Fresh.Fps = FMath::Max(0, Fresh.Fps);
	Root->TryGetBoolField(TEXT("Loop"), Fresh.bLoop);

	double WorldW = 0.0;
	double WorldH = 0.0;
	if (Root->TryGetNumberField(TEXT("WorldWidthCm"), WorldW))
	{
		Fresh.WorldWidthCm = static_cast<float>(WorldW);
	}
	if (Root->TryGetNumberField(TEXT("WorldHeightCm"), WorldH))
	{
		Fresh.WorldHeightCm = static_cast<float>(WorldH);
	}
	double PivotUVal = 0.5;
	double PivotVVal = 0.5;
	if (Root->TryGetNumberField(TEXT("PivotU"), PivotUVal))
	{
		Fresh.PivotU = static_cast<float>(PivotUVal);
	}
	if (Root->TryGetNumberField(TEXT("PivotV"), PivotVVal))
	{
		Fresh.PivotV = static_cast<float>(PivotVVal);
	}

	FString FacingText;
	if (Root->TryGetStringField(TEXT("Facing"), FacingText))
	{
		Fresh.Facing = GBPixelVfxClipLocal::FacingFromString(FacingText);
	}
	double GlowVal = 0.0;
	if (Root->TryGetNumberField(TEXT("Glow"), GlowVal))
	{
		Fresh.Glow = static_cast<float>(GlowVal);
	}
	Root->TryGetBoolField(TEXT("Lit"), Fresh.bLit);

	const TSharedPtr<FJsonObject>* LightField = nullptr;
	if (Root->TryGetObjectField(TEXT("Light"), LightField) && LightField && LightField->IsValid())
	{
		const TSharedPtr<FJsonObject>& LightJson = *LightField;
		LightJson->TryGetBoolField(TEXT("Enabled"), Fresh.Light.bEnabled);
		if (LightJson->TryGetStringField(TEXT("Color"), Fresh.Light.ColorHex))
		{
			Fresh.Light.Color = GBPixelVfxClipLocal::ColorFromHex(Fresh.Light.ColorHex);
		}
		double Intensity = 0.0;
		if (LightJson->TryGetNumberField(TEXT("Intensity"), Intensity))
		{
			Fresh.Light.Intensity = static_cast<float>(Intensity);
		}
		double Radius = 0.0;
		if (LightJson->TryGetNumberField(TEXT("RadiusCm"), Radius))
		{
			Fresh.Light.RadiusCm = static_cast<float>(Radius);
		}
		LightJson->TryGetBoolField(TEXT("CastShadows"), Fresh.Light.bCastShadows);
		double AnchorU = 0.5;
		double AnchorV = 0.5;
		if (LightJson->TryGetNumberField(TEXT("AnchorU"), AnchorU))
		{
			Fresh.Light.AnchorU = static_cast<float>(AnchorU);
		}
		if (LightJson->TryGetNumberField(TEXT("AnchorV"), AnchorV))
		{
			Fresh.Light.AnchorV = static_cast<float>(AnchorV);
		}
		LightJson->TryGetStringField(TEXT("TrackMode"), Fresh.Light.TrackMode);
		const TArray<TSharedPtr<FJsonValue>>* Track = nullptr;
		if (LightJson->TryGetArrayField(TEXT("Track"), Track) && Track)
		{
			Fresh.Light.Track.Reset(Track->Num());
			for (const TSharedPtr<FJsonValue>& Entry : *Track)
			{
				Fresh.Light.Track.Add(static_cast<float>(Entry->AsNumber()));
			}
		}
	}

	const TSharedPtr<FJsonObject>* SourceObj = nullptr;
	if (Root->TryGetObjectField(TEXT("Source"), SourceObj) && SourceObj && SourceObj->IsValid())
	{
		const TSharedPtr<FJsonObject>& Source = *SourceObj;
		Source->TryGetStringField(TEXT("System"), Fresh.SourceSystem);
		double Duration = 0.0;
		if (Source->TryGetNumberField(TEXT("DurationSeconds"), Duration))
		{
			Fresh.SourceDurationSeconds = static_cast<float>(Duration);
		}
		Source->TryGetNumberField(TEXT("SimFps"), Fresh.SourceSimFps);
		Source->TryGetStringField(TEXT("EngineVersion"), Fresh.EngineVersion);
		Source->TryGetStringField(TEXT("BakedUtc"), Fresh.BakedUtc);
	}

	if (Fresh.Id.IsNone() || Fresh.FrameCount <= 0 || Fresh.FrameWidth <= 0 || Fresh.FrameHeight <= 0
		|| Fresh.Columns <= 0 || Fresh.AtlasFile.IsEmpty())
	{
		return false;
	}
	*this = Fresh;
	return true;
}

bool FGBPixelVfxClip::SaveToJsonString(FString& OutText) const
{
	const TSharedRef<FJsonObject> Root = MakeShared<FJsonObject>();
	Root->SetStringField(TEXT("Id"), Id.ToString());
	Root->SetStringField(TEXT("Atlas"), AtlasFile);
	Root->SetNumberField(TEXT("FrameWidth"), FrameWidth);
	Root->SetNumberField(TEXT("FrameHeight"), FrameHeight);
	Root->SetNumberField(TEXT("Columns"), Columns);
	Root->SetNumberField(TEXT("FrameCount"), FrameCount);
	Root->SetNumberField(TEXT("Fps"), Fps);
	Root->SetBoolField(TEXT("Loop"), bLoop);
	Root->SetNumberField(TEXT("WorldWidthCm"), WorldWidthCm);
	Root->SetNumberField(TEXT("WorldHeightCm"), WorldHeightCm);
	Root->SetNumberField(TEXT("PivotU"), PivotU);
	Root->SetNumberField(TEXT("PivotV"), PivotV);
	Root->SetStringField(TEXT("Facing"), GBPixelVfxClipLocal::FacingToString(Facing));
	Root->SetNumberField(TEXT("Glow"), Glow);
	Root->SetBoolField(TEXT("Lit"), bLit);

	const TSharedRef<FJsonObject> LightJson = MakeShared<FJsonObject>();
	LightJson->SetBoolField(TEXT("Enabled"), Light.bEnabled);
	LightJson->SetStringField(TEXT("Color"), Light.ColorHex);
	LightJson->SetNumberField(TEXT("Intensity"), Light.Intensity);
	LightJson->SetNumberField(TEXT("RadiusCm"), Light.RadiusCm);
	LightJson->SetBoolField(TEXT("CastShadows"), Light.bCastShadows);
	LightJson->SetNumberField(TEXT("AnchorU"), Light.AnchorU);
	LightJson->SetNumberField(TEXT("AnchorV"), Light.AnchorV);
	LightJson->SetStringField(TEXT("TrackMode"), Light.TrackMode);
	TArray<TSharedPtr<FJsonValue>> TrackValues;
	for (float Entry : Light.Track)
	{
		TrackValues.Add(MakeShared<FJsonValueNumber>(Entry));
	}
	LightJson->SetArrayField(TEXT("Track"), TrackValues);
	Root->SetObjectField(TEXT("Light"), LightJson);

	const TSharedRef<FJsonObject> Source = MakeShared<FJsonObject>();
	Source->SetStringField(TEXT("System"), SourceSystem);
	Source->SetNumberField(TEXT("DurationSeconds"), SourceDurationSeconds);
	Source->SetNumberField(TEXT("SimFps"), SourceSimFps);
	Source->SetStringField(TEXT("EngineVersion"), EngineVersion);
	Source->SetStringField(TEXT("BakedUtc"), BakedUtc);
	Root->SetObjectField(TEXT("Source"), Source);

	const TSharedRef<TJsonWriter<>> Writer = TJsonWriterFactory<>::Create(&OutText);
	return FJsonSerializer::Serialize(Root, Writer);
}
// Plays one baked pixel VFX clip: a camera-facing quad plus a real point light on a baked track.
#pragma once

#include "CoreMinimal.h"
#include "Components/SceneComponent.h"
#include "GBPixelVfxTypes.h"
#include "GBPixelVfxComponent.generated.h"

class UStaticMeshComponent;
class UPointLightComponent;
class UMaterialInstanceDynamic;

UCLASS(ClassGroup = "Glyphbound", meta = (BlueprintSpawnableComponent))
class GBPIXELVFX_API UGBPixelVfxComponent : public USceneComponent
{
	GENERATED_BODY()

public:
	UGBPixelVfxComponent();

	// Starts playing a clip (builds the quad and the light on first use). False when unknown.
	UFUNCTION(BlueprintCallable, Category = "PixelVfx")
	bool Play(FName ClipId, bool bRestart = true);

	UFUNCTION(BlueprintCallable, Category = "PixelVfx")
	void Stop();

	UFUNCTION(BlueprintCallable, Category = "PixelVfx")
	bool IsPlaying() const { return bPlaying && CurrentClip.IsValid(); }

	// Destroy the owner actor when a one-shot clip finishes.
	UPROPERTY(EditAnywhere, BlueprintReadWrite, Category = "PixelVfx")
	bool bAutoDestroyOwnerWhenFinished = false;

	UPROPERTY(EditAnywhere, BlueprintReadWrite, Category = "PixelVfx")
	float PlayRate = 1.f;

	// Multiplier on the clip world size and on the light radius.
	UPROPERTY(EditAnywhere, BlueprintReadWrite, Category = "PixelVfx")
	float SizeScale = 1.f;

	UPROPERTY(EditAnywhere, BlueprintReadWrite, Category = "PixelVfx")
	float LightIntensityScale = 1.f;

	UPROPERTY(EditAnywhere, BlueprintReadWrite, Category = "PixelVfx")
	bool bEnableLight = true;

	// Defaults to the clip's value once Play loads it.
	UPROPERTY(EditAnywhere, BlueprintReadWrite, Category = "PixelVfx")
	bool bLightCastsShadows = true;

	bool bLightCastsShadowsSet = false;

	// When set, replaces the clip facing. "None" (the default) uses the clip value.
	UPROPERTY(EditAnywhere, BlueprintReadWrite, Category = "PixelVfx")
	FName FacingOverride;

	UPROPERTY(EditAnywhere, BlueprintReadWrite, Category = "PixelVfx")
	float StartTimeOffset = 0.f;

	// Quad world origin/extent for diagnostics (bake showcase logging).
	void GetQuadWorldBounds(FVector& OutOrigin, FVector& OutExtent) const;

protected:
	virtual void TickComponent(float DeltaTime, ELevelTick TickType, FActorComponentTickFunction* ThisTickFunction) override;
	virtual void EndPlay(const EEndPlayReason::Type EndPlayReason) override;
	virtual void OnComponentDestroyed(bool bDestroyingHierarchy) override;

private:
	void EnsureBuilt();

// The baked pixel VFX clip: what the baker writes (Content/Data/Vfx/<Id>.json + <Id>.png)
// and what the runtime subsystem, component and actor read back. Plain struct, no UObject.
#pragma once

#include "CoreMinimal.h"
#include "GBPixelVfxLog.h"
#include "GBPixelVfxTypes.generated.h"

UENUM(BlueprintType)
enum class EGBPixelVfxFacing : uint8
{
	Billboard UMETA(DisplayName = "Billboard"),
	Upright UMETA(DisplayName = "Upright"),
	Ground UMETA(DisplayName = "Ground"),
	Fixed UMETA(DisplayName = "Fixed")
};

USTRUCT(BlueprintType)
struct GBPIXELVFX_API FGBPixelVfxLight
{
	GENERATED_BODY()

	UPROPERTY()
	bool bEnabled = true;

	// sRGB hex string ("#RRGGBB") as stored in the clip JSON.
	UPROPERTY()
	FString ColorHex = TEXT("#FFFFFF");

	UPROPERTY()
	FLinearColor Color = FLinearColor::White;

	// Candelas at track value 1.
	UPROPERTY()
	float Intensity = 0.f;

	UPROPERTY()
	float RadiusCm = 0.f;

	UPROPERTY()
	bool bCastShadows = true;

	// Average emissive centroid of the clip, in frame UV (V down).
	UPROPERTY()
	float AnchorU = 0.5f;

	UPROPERTY()
	float AnchorV = 0.5f;

	// One value 0..1 per frame. "Baked" = the measured envelope, "Constant" = always 1.
	UPROPERTY()
	FString TrackMode = TEXT("Baked");

	UPROPERTY()
	TArray<float> Track;
};

USTRUCT(BlueprintType)
struct GBPIXELVFX_API FGBPixelVfxClip
{
	GENERATED_BODY()

	UPROPERTY()
	FName Id = NAME_None;

	// PNG file name inside Content/Data/Vfx (for example "FluidFire.png").
	UPROPERTY()
	FString AtlasFile;

	UPROPERTY()
	int32 FrameWidth = 0;

	UPROPERTY()
	int32 FrameHeight = 0;

	UPROPERTY()
	int32 Columns = 0;

	UPROPERTY()
	int32 FrameCount = 0;

	UPROPERTY()
	int32 Fps = 12;

	UPROPERTY()
	bool bLoop = true;

	// Real size of the captured area: the baked sprite fills the same space as the source effect.
	UPROPERTY()
	float WorldWidthCm = 0.f;

	UPROPERTY()
	float WorldHeightCm = 0.f;

	// Where the source system origin sits inside the frame (0..1, V down).
	UPROPERTY()
	float PivotU = 0.5f;

	UPROPERTY()
	float PivotV = 0.5f;

	UPROPERTY()
	EGBPixelVfxFacing Facing = EGBPixelVfxFacing::Upright;

	UPROPERTY()
	float Glow = 0.f;

	// True when the sprite needs scene lighting (smoke): uses the Lit material variant.
	UPROPERTY()
	bool bLit = false;

	UPROPERTY()
	FGBPixelVfxLight Light;

	UPROPERTY()
	FString SourceSystem;

	UPROPERTY()
	float SourceDurationSeconds = 0.f;

	UPROPERTY()
	int32 SourceSimFps = 60;

	UPROPERTY()
	FString EngineVersion;

	UPROPERTY()
	FString BakedUtc;

	int32 Rows() const { return (Columns > 0 && FrameCount > 0) ? (FrameCount + Columns - 1) / Columns : 0; }

	// UV offset (x, y) and scale (z, w) of one frame inside the atlas.
	static FVector4f FrameRect(const FGBPixelVfxClip& Clip, int32 FrameIndex);

	// Stepped frame index at the given playback time. Loops wrap; one-shots clamp to the
	// last frame and set bOutFinished once it has been shown for one frame duration.
	static int32 FrameAtTime(const FGBPixelVfxClip& Clip, float Seconds, bool& bOutFinished);

	// Quad + light placement in world space. R is the quad's final world rotation
	// (component rotation for Fixed, yaw-only component rotation for Ground, camera
	// facing for Upright/Billboard). Quad local X = U right, Y = V up, both in cm.
	static void ComputeQuadAndLightPlacement(const FGBPixelVfxClip& Clip, float SizeScale,
		const FVector& ComponentLocation, const FQuat& R,
		FVector& OutQuadLocation, FVector& OutLightLocation);

	bool LoadFromJsonString(const FString& Text);
	bool SaveToJsonString(FString& OutText) const;
};

// GBPixelVfxConvert: Niagara frame -> palette mapped pixel art. See the header for the step order.
#include "GBPixelVfxConvert.h"

namespace GBPixelVfxConvertLocal
{
	int32 HexDigit(TCHAR C)
	{
		if (C >= TEXT('0') && C <= TEXT('9')) return C - TEXT('0');
		if (C >= TEXT('a') && C <= TEXT('f')) return 10 + C - TEXT('a');
		if (C >= TEXT('A') && C <= TEXT('F')) return 10 + C - TEXT('A');
		return -1;
	}

	float Saturate(float X) { return FMath::Clamp(X, 0.f, 1.f); }

	bool IsSplitMode(const GBPixelVfxConvert::FSettings& Settings)
	{
		return Settings.ColorMode.Equals(TEXT("Split"), ESearchCase::IgnoreCase) && Settings.Ramps.Num() >= 2;
	}

	int32 RampIndexForValue(float V, int32 Count)
	{
		if (Count <= 0)
		{
			return 0;
		}
		return FMath::Clamp(FMath::FloorToInt(V * static_cast<float>(Count)), 0, Count - 1);
	}

	// Nearest-rank percentile of a sorted ascending array. Falls back to 1 when empty.
	float PercentileOfSorted(const TArray<float>& Sorted, float P)
	{
		if (Sorted.Num() == 0)
		{
			return 1.f;
		}
		const int32 Rank = FMath::Clamp(FMath::CeilToInt(P * Sorted.Num()) - 1, 0, Sorted.Num() - 1);
		return Sorted[Rank];
	}

	FLinearColor ToneMapForNearest(const FLinearColor& C, float Peak)
	{
		const float Inv = 1.f / FMath::Max(Peak, 1.e-4f);
		const FLinearColor S(C.R * Inv, C.G * Inv, C.B * Inv);
		return FLinearColor(S.R / (1.f + S.R), S.G / (1.f + S.G), S.B / (1.f + S.B));
	}

	void NearestRampColor(const FLinearColor& C, const GBPixelVfxConvert::FSettings& Settings,
		float Peak, uint8& OutRamp, uint8& OutIndex)
	{
		OutRamp = 0;
		OutIndex = 0;
		if (Settings.Ramps.Num() == 0 || Settings.Ramps[0].Num() == 0)
		{
			return;
		}
		const FVector Target = GBPixelVfxConvert::LabFromLinear(ToneMapForNearest(C, Peak));
		float Best = FLT_MAX;
		for (int32 Ramp = 0; Ramp < Settings.Ramps.Num(); ++Ramp)
		{
			const TArray<FColor>& Colors = Settings.Ramps[Ramp];
			for (int32 i = 0; i < Colors.Num(); ++i)
			{
				// Palette colors are used directly (sRGB -> linear -> OKLab):
				// tone-mapping them would move the palette positions in OKLab.
				const FVector Candidate = GBPixelVfxConvert::LabFromLinear(FLinearColor(Colors[i]));
				const float Dist = static_cast<float>(FVector::DistSquared(Target, Candidate));
				if (Dist < Best)
				{
					Best = Dist;
					OutRamp = static_cast<uint8>(FMath::Min(Ramp, 255));
					OutIndex = static_cast<uint8>(FMath::Min(i, 255));
				}
			}
		}
	}

	bool Inside(int32 X, int32 Y, int32 W, int32 H)
	{
		return X >= 0 && Y >= 0 && X < W && Y < H;
	}
}

namespace GBPixelVfxConvert
{
bool ParseHexColor(const FString& Hex, FColor& OutColor)
{
	FString Text = Hex;
	Text.TrimStartAndEndInline();
	if (Text.StartsWith(TEXT("#")))
	{
		Text.RightChopInline(1);
	}
	if (Text.Len() != 6)
	{
		return false;
	}
	uint8 Bytes[4] = { 0, 0, 0, 255 };
	for (int32 i = 0; i < 3; ++i)
	{
		const int32 Hi = GBPixelVfxConvertLocal::HexDigit(Text[i * 2]);
		const int32 Lo = GBPixelVfxConvertLocal::HexDigit(Text[i * 2 + 1]);
		if (Hi < 0 || Lo < 0)
		{
			return false;
		}
		Bytes[i] = static_cast<uint8>(Hi * 16 + Lo);
	}
	OutColor = FColor(Bytes[0], Bytes[1], Bytes[2], Bytes[3]);
	return true;
}

float PixelLuminance(const FLinearColor& Color)
{
	return Color.R * 0.2126f + Color.G * 0.7152f + Color.B * 0.0722f;
}

float PixelSaturation(const FLinearColor& Color)
{
	const float MaxC = FMath::Max3(Color.R, Color.G, Color.B);
	if (MaxC <= 1.e-6f)
	{
		return 0.f;
	}
	const float MinC = FMath::Min3(Color.R, Color.G, Color.B);
	return (MaxC - MinC) / MaxC;
}

FVector LabFromLinear(const FLinearColor& Color)
{
	const float R = FMath::Max(0.f, Color.R);
	const float G = FMath::Max(0.f, Color.G);
	const float B = FMath::Max(0.f, Color.B);
	const float L = 0.4122214708f * R + 0.5363325363f * G + 0.0514459929f * B;
	const float M = 0.2119034982f * R + 0.6806995451f * G + 0.1073969566f * B;
	const float S = 0.0883024619f * R + 0.2817188376f * G + 0.6299787005f * B;
	const float Lc = FMath::Pow(FMath::Max(0.f, L), 1.f / 3.f);
	const float Mc = FMath::Pow(FMath::Max(0.f, M), 1.f / 3.f);
	const float Sc = FMath::Pow(FMath::Max(0.f, S), 1.f / 3.f);
	return FVector(
		0.2104542553 * Lc + 0.7936177850 * Mc - 0.0040720468 * Sc,
		1.9779984951 * Lc - 2.4285922050 * Mc + 0.4505937099 * Sc,
		0.0259040371 * Lc + 0.7827717662 * Mc - 0.8086757660 * Sc);
}

bool IsHotPixel(const FLinearColor& Unpremultiplied, const FSettings& Settings)
{
	return PixelLuminance(Unpremultiplied) >= Settings.HotLuminance
		|| PixelSaturation(Unpremultiplied) >= Settings.HotSaturation;
}

void ApplyExposure(TArray<FWorkFrame>& Frames, float Exposure)
{
	if (FMath::IsNearlyEqual(Exposure, 1.f))
	{
		return;
	}
	for (FWorkFrame& Frame : Frames)
	{
		for (FLinearColor& Pixel : Frame.Color)
		{
			Pixel.R *= Exposure;
			Pixel.G *= Exposure;
			Pixel.B *= Exposure;
		}
	}
}

void ApplyLoopBlend(TArray<FWorkFrame>& Frames, int32 KeepCount, int32 BlendFrames, bool bLoop)
{
	if (!bLoop || BlendFrames <= 0 || KeepCount <= 0 || Frames.Num() < KeepCount + BlendFrames)
	{
		while (Frames.Num() > KeepCount)
		{
			Frames.Pop();
		}
		return;
	}
	const int32 B = FMath::Min(BlendFrames, KeepCount);
	for (int32 i = 0; i < B; ++i)
	{
		FWorkFrame& Dst = Frames[i];
		const FWorkFrame& Extra = Frames[KeepCount + i];
		if (Dst.Color.Num() != Extra.Color.Num() || Dst.Opacity.Num() != Extra.Opacity.Num())
		{
			continue;
		}
		const float T = (static_cast<float>(i) + 0.5f) / static_cast<float>(B);
		for (int32 p = 0; p < Dst.Color.Num(); ++p)
		{
			Dst.Color[p] = Extra.Color[p] * (1.f - T) + Dst.Color[p] * T;
		}
		for (int32 p = 0; p < Dst.Opacity.Num(); ++p)
		{
			Dst.Opacity[p] = Extra.Opacity[p] * (1.f - T) + Dst.Opacity[p] * T;
		}
	}
	while (Frames.Num() > KeepCount)
	{
		Frames.Pop();
	}
}

FWorkFrame DownsampleFrame(const FWorkFrame& In, int32 OutWidth, int32 OutHeight)
{
	FWorkFrame Out;
	Out.Width = OutWidth;
	Out.Height = OutHeight;
	if (In.Width <= 0 || In.Height <= 0 || OutWidth <= 0 || OutHeight <= 0
		|| In.Color.Num() != In.Width * In.Height || In.Opacity.Num() != In.Width * In.Height)
	{
		return Out;
	}
	Out.Color.SetNumZeroed(OutWidth * OutHeight);
	Out.Opacity.SetNumZeroed(OutWidth * OutHeight);
	for (int32 y = 0; y < OutHeight; ++y)
	{
		const int32 Y0 = y * In.Height / OutHeight;
		const int32 Y1 = (y + 1) * In.Height / OutHeight;
		for (int32 x = 0; x < OutWidth; ++x)
		{
			const int32 X0 = x * In.Width / OutWidth;
			const int32 X1 = (x + 1) * In.Width / OutWidth;
			FLinearColor Sum(0.f, 0.f, 0.f, 0.f);
			float OpSum = 0.f;
			int32 Count = 0;
			for (int32 sy = Y0; sy < Y1; ++sy)
			{
				for (int32 sx = X0; sx < X1; ++sx)
				{
					const int32 Src = sy * In.Width + sx;
					Sum += In.Color[Src];
					OpSum += In.Opacity[Src];
					++Count;
				}
			}
			const int32 Dst = y * OutWidth + x;
			if (Count > 0)
			{
				Out.Color[Dst] = Sum / static_cast<float>(Count);
				Out.Opacity[Dst] = OpSum / static_cast<float>(Count);
			}
		}
	}
	return Out;
}

void ComputePeaks(const TArray<FWorkFrame>& Frames, const FSettings& Settings,
	float& OutPeak, float& OutHotPeak, float& OutColdPeak)
{
	using namespace GBPixelVfxConvertLocal;
	TArray<float> All;
	TArray<float> Hot;
	TArray<float> Cold;
	const bool bSplit = IsSplitMode(Settings);
	for (const FWorkFrame& Frame : Frames)
	{
		const int32 Count = FMath::Min(Frame.Color.Num(), Frame.Opacity.Num());
		for (int32 i = 0; i < Count; ++i)
		{
			const float Op = Frame.Opacity[i];
			const FLinearColor& RGB = Frame.Color[i];
			const float Lum = PixelLuminance(RGB);
			float A = Op;
			if (Settings.AlphaMode.Equals(TEXT("Luminance"), ESearchCase::IgnoreCase))
			{
				A = Saturate(Lum);
			}
			else if (Settings.AlphaMode.Equals(TEXT("Max"), ESearchCase::IgnoreCase))
			{
				A = FMath::Max(Op, Saturate(Lum));
			}
			if (A < Settings.AlphaThreshold)
			{
				continue;
			}
			const FLinearColor C = RGB / FMath::Max(A, 1.e-4f);
			const float LumC = PixelLuminance(C);
			All.Add(LumC);
			if (bSplit)
			{
				if (IsHotPixel(C, Settings))
				{
					Hot.Add(LumC);
				}
				else
				{
					Cold.Add(LumC);
				}
			}
		}
	}
	All.Sort();
	Hot.Sort();
	Cold.Sort();
	OutPeak = PercentileOfSorted(All, Settings.LevelsPercentile);
	OutHotPeak = Hot.Num() > 0 ? PercentileOfSorted(Hot, Settings.LevelsPercentile) : OutPeak;
	OutColdPeak = Cold.Num() > 0 ? PercentileOfSorted(Cold, Settings.LevelsPercentile) : OutPeak;
	OutPeak = FMath::Max(OutPeak, 1.e-4f);
	OutHotPeak = FMath::Max(OutHotPeak, 1.e-4f);
	OutColdPeak = FMath::Max(OutColdPeak, 1.e-4f);
}

FFrameMap MapFrame(const FWorkFrame& Frame, const FSettings& Settings,
	float Peak, float HotPeak, float ColdPeak)
{
	using namespace GBPixelVfxConvertLocal;
	FFrameMap Map;
	Map.Width = Frame.Width;
	Map.Height = Frame.Height;
	const int32 Count = FMath::Min(Frame.Color.Num(), Frame.Opacity.Num());
	Map.Pixels.SetNum(Count);
	const bool bSplit = IsSplitMode(Settings);
	const bool bNearest = Settings.ColorMode.Equals(TEXT("Nearest"), ESearchCase::IgnoreCase);
	const int32 Ramp0Count = Settings.Ramps.Num() > 0 ? Settings.Ramps[0].Num() : 0;
	const int32 Ramp1Count = Settings.Ramps.Num() > 1 ? Settings.Ramps[1].Num() : 0;
	for (int32 i = 0; i < Count; ++i)
	{
		FMappedPixel& Dst = Map.Pixels[i];
		const float Op = Frame.Opacity[i];
		const FLinearColor& RGB = Frame.Color[i];
		const float Lum = PixelLuminance(RGB);
		float A = Op;
		if (Settings.AlphaMode.Equals(TEXT("Luminance"), ESearchCase::IgnoreCase))
		{
			A = Saturate(Lum);
		}
		else if (Settings.AlphaMode.Equals(TEXT("Max"), ESearchCase::IgnoreCase))
		{
			A = FMath::Max(Op, Saturate(Lum));
		}
		if (A < Settings.AlphaThreshold)
		{
			continue;
		}
		const FLinearColor C = RGB / FMath::Max(A, 1.e-4f);
		const float LumC = PixelLuminance(C);
		Dst.bSolid = true;
		if (bNearest)
		{
			Dst.Value = Saturate(FMath::Pow(FMath::Clamp(LumC / Peak, 0.f, 1.f), Settings.Gamma));
			Dst.bHot = true;
			NearestRampColor(C, Settings, Peak, Dst.Ramp, Dst.Index);
		}
		else if (bSplit)
		{
			if (IsHotPixel(C, Settings))
			{
				Dst.bHot = true;
				Dst.Value = Saturate(FMath::Pow(FMath::Clamp(LumC / HotPeak, 0.f, 1.f), Settings.Gamma));
				Dst.Ramp = 0;
				Dst.Index = static_cast<uint8>(RampIndexForValue(Dst.Value, Ramp0Count));
			}
			else
			{
				Dst.bHot = false;
				Dst.Value = Saturate(FMath::Pow(FMath::Clamp(LumC / ColdPeak, 0.f, 1.f), Settings.Gamma));
				Dst.Ramp = 1;
				Dst.Index = static_cast<uint8>(RampIndexForValue(Dst.Value, Ramp1Count));
			}
		}
		else
		{
			Dst.bHot = true;
			Dst.Value = Saturate(FMath::Pow(FMath::Clamp(LumC / Peak, 0.f, 1.f), Settings.Gamma));
			Dst.Ramp = 0;
			Dst.Index = static_cast<uint8>(RampIndexForValue(Dst.Value, Ramp0Count));
		}
	}
	return Map;
}

void ApplyHysteresis(FFrameMap& Current, const FFrameMap& Prev, const FSettings& Settings)
{
	using namespace GBPixelVfxConvertLocal;
	if (Current.Pixels.Num() != Prev.Pixels.Num() || Settings.Hysteresis <= 0.f)
	{
		return;
	}
	const int32 PrevSolid = Prev.SolidCount();
	if (PrevSolid > 0)
	{
		const int32 CurSolid = Current.SolidCount();
		// Fast changing frames (explosions): damping would smear real change, so skip it.
		if (FMath::Abs(static_cast<float>(CurSolid - PrevSolid)) / static_cast<float>(PrevSolid) > 0.3f)
		{
			return;
		}
	}
	for (int32 i = 0; i < Current.Pixels.Num(); ++i)
	{
		FMappedPixel& Dst = Current.Pixels[i];
		const FMappedPixel& Src = Prev.Pixels[i];
		if (!Dst.bSolid || !Src.bSolid || Dst.Ramp != Src.Ramp)
		{
			continue;
		}
		if (Dst.Index == Src.Index)
		{
			continue;
		}
		int32 RampLen = 0;
		if (Src.Ramp < static_cast<uint8>(Settings.Ramps.Num()))
		{
			RampLen = Settings.Ramps[Src.Ramp].Num();
		}
		if (RampLen <= 0)
		{
			continue;
		}
		// The previous band widened by Hysteresis on both sides keeps the old index.
		const float BandLo = static_cast<float>(Src.Index) / static_cast<float>(RampLen) - Settings.Hysteresis;
		const float BandHi = (static_cast<float>(Src.Index) + 1.f) / static_cast<float>(RampLen) + Settings.Hysteresis;
		if (Dst.Value >= BandLo && Dst.Value <= BandHi)
		{
			Dst.Index = Src.Index;
		}
	}
}

void RemoveSmallIslands(FFrameMap& Map, int32 MinIslandPixels)
{
	using namespace GBPixelVfxConvertLocal;
	if (MinIslandPixels <= 1 || Map.Width <= 0 || Map.Height <= 0
		|| Map.Pixels.Num() != Map.Width * Map.Height)
	{
		return;
	}
	const int32 W = Map.Width;
	const int32 H = Map.Height;
	TArray<int32> Island;
	Island.Reserve(64);
	TArray<int32> Stack;
	Stack.Reserve(64);
	TArray<uint8> Seen;
	Seen.SetNumZeroed(W * H);
	const int32 DX[4] = { 1, -1, 0, 0 };
	const int32 DY[4] = { 0, 0, 1, -1 };
	for (int32 Start = 0; Start < W * H; ++Start)
	{
		if (!Map.Pixels[Start].bSolid || Seen[Start])
		{
			continue;
		}
		Island.Reset();
		Stack.Reset();
		Stack.Add(Start);
		Seen[Start] = 1;
		while (Stack.Num() > 0)
		{
			const int32 At = Stack.Pop();
			Island.Add(At);
			const int32 X = At % W;
			const int32 Y = At / W;
			for (int32 d = 0; d < 4; ++d)
			{
				const int32 NX = X + DX[d];
				const int32 NY = Y + DY[d];
				if (!Inside(NX, NY, W, H))
				{
					continue;
				}
				const int32 NAt = NY * W + NX;
				if (!Seen[NAt] && Map.Pixels[NAt].bSolid)
				{
					Seen[NAt] = 1;
					Stack.Add(NAt);
				}
			}
		}
		if (Island.Num() < MinIslandPixels)
		{
			for (int32 At : Island)
			{
				Map.Pixels[At].bSolid = false;
			}
		}
	}
}

void FillOnePixelHoles(FFrameMap& Map)
{
	using namespace GBPixelVfxConvertLocal;
	if (Map.Width <= 0 || Map.Height <= 0 || Map.Pixels.Num() != Map.Width * Map.Height)
	{
		return;
	}
	const int32 W = Map.Width;
	const int32 H = Map.Height;
	const int32 DX[4] = { 1, -1, 0, 0 };
	const int32 DY[4] = { 0, 0, 1, -1 };
	for (int32 y = 0; y < H; ++y)
	{
		for (int32 x = 0; x < W; ++x)
		{
			const int32 At = y * W + x;
			if (Map.Pixels[At].bSolid)
			{
				continue;
			}
			FMappedPixel Votes[4];
			int32 VoteCount = 0;
			bool bAllSolid = true;
			for (int32 d = 0; d < 4; ++d)
			{
				const int32 NX = x + DX[d];
				const int32 NY = y + DY[d];
				if (!Inside(NX, NY, W, H) || !Map.Pixels[NY * W + NX].bSolid)
				{
					bAllSolid = false;
					break;
				}
				Votes[VoteCount++] = Map.Pixels[NY * W + NX];
			}
			if (!bAllSolid)
			{
				continue;
			}
			// Most common neighbor color wins; ties go to the first neighbor.
			int32 Best = 0;
			int32 BestVotes = -1;
			for (int32 a = 0; a < VoteCount; ++a)
			{
				int32 VotesForA = 0;
				for (int32 b = 0; b < VoteCount; ++b)
				{
					if (Votes[b].Ramp == Votes[a].Ramp && Votes[b].Index == Votes[a].Index)
					{
						++VotesForA;
					}
				}
				if (VotesForA > BestVotes)
				{
					BestVotes = VotesForA;
					Best = a;
				}
			}
			Map.Pixels[At] = Votes[Best];
			Map.Pixels[At].bSolid = true;
		}
	}
}

void RecolorSmallColorClusters(FFrameMap& Map, const FSettings& Settings, int32 MinPixels)
{
	using namespace GBPixelVfxConvertLocal;
	if (MinPixels <= 1 || Map.Width <= 0 || Map.Height <= 0
		|| Map.Pixels.Num() != Map.Width * Map.Height)
	{
		return;
	}
	const int32 W = Map.Width;
	const int32 H = Map.Height;
	const TArray<FMappedPixel> Orig = Map.Pixels;
	TArray<uint8> Seen;
	Seen.SetNumZeroed(W * H);
	const int32 DX[4] = { 1, -1, 0, 0 };
	const int32 DY[4] = { 0, 0, 1, -1 };
	TArray<int32> Island;
	TArray<int32> Stack;
	for (int32 Start = 0; Start < W * H; ++Start)
	{
		if (!Orig[Start].bSolid || Seen[Start])
		{
			continue;
		}
		Island.Reset();
		Stack.Reset();
		Stack.Add(Start);
		Seen[Start] = 1;
		while (Stack.Num() > 0)
		{
			const int32 At = Stack.Pop();
			Island.Add(At);
			const int32 X = At % W;
			const int32 Y = At / W;
			for (int32 d = 0; d < 4; ++d)
			{
				const int32 NX = X + DX[d];
				const int32 NY = Y + DY[d];
				if (!Inside(NX, NY, W, H))
				{
					continue;
				}
				const int32 NAt = NY * W + NX;
				if (!Seen[NAt] && Orig[NAt].bSolid
					&& Orig[NAt].Ramp == Orig[Start].Ramp && Orig[NAt].Index == Orig[Start].Index)
				{
					Seen[NAt] = 1;
					Stack.Add(NAt);
				}
			}
		}
		if (Island.Num() >= MinPixels)
		{
			continue;
		}
		// Border: solid 4-neighbors of the region with a different color (found on the pre-pass state).
		TArray<int32> Border;
		for (int32 At : Island)
		{
			const int32 X = At % W;
			const int32 Y = At / W;
			for (int32 d = 0; d < 4; ++d)
			{
				const int32 NX = X + DX[d];
				const int32 NY = Y + DY[d];
				if (!Inside(NX, NY, W, H) || !Orig[NY * W + NX].bSolid)
				{
					continue;
				}
				const int32 NAt = NY * W + NX;
				if (Orig[NAt].Ramp == Orig[Start].Ramp && Orig[NAt].Index == Orig[Start].Index)
				{
					continue;
				}
				Border.Add(NAt);
			}
		}
		if (Border.Num() == 0)
		{
			continue;
		}
		uint8 BestRamp = Orig[Border[0]].Ramp;
		uint8 BestIndex = Orig[Border[0]].Index;
		int32 BestVotes = -1;
		float BestLum = FLT_MAX;
		for (int32 a = 0; a < Border.Num(); ++a)
		{
			const uint8 AR = Orig[Border[a]].Ramp;
			const uint8 AI = Orig[Border[a]].Index;
			bool bCounted = false;
			for (int32 b = 0; b < a; ++b)
			{
				if (Orig[Border[b]].Ramp == AR && Orig[Border[b]].Index == AI)
				{
					bCounted = true;
					break;
				}
			}
			if (bCounted)
			{
				continue;
			}
			int32 VotesForA = 0;
			for (int32 b = 0; b < Border.Num(); ++b)
			{
				if (Orig[Border[b]].Ramp == AR && Orig[Border[b]].Index == AI)
				{
					++VotesForA;
				}
			}
			float Lum = FLT_MAX;
			if (AR < Settings.Ramps.Num() && AI < Settings.Ramps[AR].Num())
			{
				Lum = PixelLuminance(FLinearColor(Settings.Ramps[AR][AI]));
			}
			if (VotesForA > BestVotes || (VotesForA == BestVotes && Lum < BestLum))
			{
				BestVotes = VotesForA;
				BestLum = Lum;
				BestRamp = AR;
				BestIndex = AI;
			}
		}
		for (int32 At : Island)
		{
			Map.Pixels[At].Ramp = BestRamp;
			Map.Pixels[At].Index = BestIndex;
		}
	}
}

void ApplyFadeOut(TArray<FFrameMap>& Maps, const FSettings& Settings)
{
	using namespace GBPixelVfxConvertLocal;
	if (Settings.bLoop || Settings.FadeOutFrames <= 0 || Maps.Num() == 0)
	{
		return;
	}
	const int32 N = FMath::Min(Settings.FadeOutFrames, Maps.Num());
	const bool bErode = Settings.FadeOutMode.Equals(TEXT("Erode"), ESearchCase::IgnoreCase);
	static const uint8 Bayer[4][4] = { { 0, 8, 2, 10 }, { 12, 4, 14, 6 }, { 3, 11, 1, 9 }, { 15, 7, 13, 5 } };
	for (int32 k = 0; k < N; ++k)
	{
		const int32 f = Maps.Num() - N + k;
		FFrameMap& Map = Maps[f];
		if (Map.Width <= 0 || Map.Height <= 0 || Map.Pixels.Num() != Map.Width * Map.Height)
		{
			continue;
		}
		const float T = (static_cast<float>(k) + 1.f) / (static_cast<float>(N) + 1.f);
		if (!bErode)
		{
			for (int32 y = 0; y < Map.Height; ++y)
			{
				for (int32 x = 0; x < Map.Width; ++x)
				{
					const int32 At = y * Map.Width + x;
					if (!Map.Pixels[At].bSolid)
					{
						continue;
					}
					const float Gate = static_cast<float>(Bayer[y % 4][x % 4]) / 16.f;
					if (Gate < T)
					{
						Map.Pixels[At].bSolid = false;
					}
				}
			}
		}
		else
		{
			const int32 W = Map.Width;
			const int32 H = Map.Height;
			TArray<int32> Dist;
			Dist.SetNum(W * H);
			for (int32 i = 0; i < W * H; ++i)
			{
				Dist[i] = -1;
			}
			TArray<int32> Queue;
			Queue.Reserve(W * H);
			int32 QHead = 0;
			for (int32 i = 0; i < W * H; ++i)
			{
				if (!Map.Pixels[i].bSolid)
				{
					Dist[i] = 0;
					Queue.Add(i);
				}
			}
			// The frame edge counts as transparent: edge pixels are one step away.
			for (int32 y = 0; y < H; ++y)
			{
				for (int32 x = 0; x < W; ++x)
				{
					if (x != 0 && y != 0 && x != W - 1 && y != H - 1)
					{
						continue;
					}
					const int32 At = y * W + x;
					if (Map.Pixels[At].bSolid && Dist[At] < 0)
					{
						Dist[At] = 1;
						Queue.Add(At);
					}
				}
			}
			const int32 DXE[4] = { 1, -1, 0, 0 };
			const int32 DYE[4] = { 0, 0, 1, -1 };
			while (QHead < Queue.Num())
			{
				const int32 At = Queue[QHead++];
				const int32 X = At % W;
				const int32 Y = At / W;
				for (int32 d = 0; d < 4; ++d)
				{
					const int32 NX = X + DXE[d];
					const int32 NY = Y + DYE[d];
					if (!Inside(NX, NY, W, H))
					{
						continue;
					}
					const int32 NAt = NY * W + NX;
					if (Dist[NAt] < 0 && Map.Pixels[NAt].bSolid)
					{
						Dist[NAt] = Dist[At] + 1;
						Queue.Add(NAt);
					}
				}
			}
			int32 MaxDepth = 0;
			for (int32 i = 0; i < W * H; ++i)
			{
				if (Map.Pixels[i].bSolid)
				{
					MaxDepth = FMath::Max(MaxDepth, Dist[i] < 0 ? 1 : Dist[i]);
				}
			}
			if (MaxDepth <= 0)
			{
				continue;
			}
			const int32 KillDist = FMath::CeilToInt(T * static_cast<float>(MaxDepth));
			for (int32 i = 0; i < W * H; ++i)
			{
				if (Map.Pixels[i].bSolid && Dist[i] >= 0 && Dist[i] <= KillDist)
				{
					Map.Pixels[i].bSolid = false;
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

bool ResolveOutlineColor(const FSettings& Settings, FColor& OutColor)
{
	if (Settings.Outline.Equals(TEXT("Black"), ESearchCase::IgnoreCase))
	{
		OutColor = FColor(0, 0, 0, 255);
		return true;
	}
	if (Settings.Outline.Equals(TEXT("Darkest"), ESearchCase::IgnoreCase)
		&& Settings.Ramps.Num() > 0 && Settings.Ramps[0].Num() > 0)
	{
		OutColor = Settings.Ramps[0][0];
		OutColor.A = 255;
		return true;
	}
	return false;
}

void ResolveFrames(const TArray<FFrameMap>& Maps, const FSettings& Settings,
	TArray<TArray<FColor>>& OutPixels)
{
	using namespace GBPixelVfxConvertLocal;
	OutPixels.Reset(Maps.Num());
	FColor OutlineColor(0, 0, 0, 255);
	const bool bOutline = ResolveOutlineColor(Settings, OutlineColor);
	for (const FFrameMap& Map : Maps)
	{
		TArray<FColor> Pixels;
		Pixels.SetNum(Map.Pixels.Num());
		for (int32 i = 0; i < Map.Pixels.Num(); ++i)
		{
			const FMappedPixel& Pixel = Map.Pixels[i];
			if (Pixel.bSolid && Pixel.Ramp < Settings.Ramps.Num()
				&& Pixel.Index < Settings.Ramps[Pixel.Ramp].Num())
			{
				Pixels[i] = Settings.Ramps[Pixel.Ramp][Pixel.Index];
				Pixels[i].A = 255;
			}
			else
			{
				Pixels[i] = FColor(0, 0, 0, 0);
			}
		}
		if (bOutline && Map.Width > 0 && Map.Height > 0 && Map.Pixels.Num() == Map.Width * Map.Height)
		{
			const int32 W = Map.Width;
			const int32 H = Map.Height;
			const int32 DX[4] = { 1, -1, 0, 0 };
			const int32 DY[4] = { 0, 0, 1, -1 };
			for (int32 y = 0; y < H; ++y)
			{
				for (int32 x = 0; x < W; ++x)
				{
					const int32 At = y * W + x;
					if (Map.Pixels[At].bSolid)
					{
						continue;
					}
					bool bAdjacent = false;
					for (int32 d = 0; d < 4; ++d)
					{
						const int32 NX = x + DX[d];
						const int32 NY = y + DY[d];
						if (Inside(NX, NY, W, H) && Map.Pixels[NY * W + NX].bSolid)
						{
							bAdjacent = true;
							break;
						}
					}
					if (bAdjacent)
					{
						Pixels[At] = OutlineColor;
					}
				}
			}
		}
		OutPixels.Add(MoveTemp(Pixels));
	}
}

void ComputeLightTrack(const TArray<FFrameMap>& Maps, const FSettings& Settings,
	TArray<float>& OutTrack, float& OutAnchorU, float& OutAnchorV)
{
	OutTrack.Reset();
	OutAnchorU = 0.5f;
	OutAnchorV = 0.5f;
	if (!Settings.bLightEnabled || Maps.Num() == 0)
	{
		return;
	}
	OutTrack.SetNum(Maps.Num());
	float MaxE = 0.f;
	double AnchorUSum = 0.0;
	double AnchorVSum = 0.0;
	double AnchorWeight = 0.0;
	for (int32 f = 0; f < Maps.Num(); ++f)
	{
		const FFrameMap& Map = Maps[f];
		float E = 0.f;
		double FrameUSum = 0.0;
		double FrameVSum = 0.0;
		double FrameWeight = 0.0;
		for (int32 i = 0; i < Map.Pixels.Num(); ++i)
		{
			const FMappedPixel& Pixel = Map.Pixels[i];
			if (!Pixel.bSolid || !Pixel.bHot)
			{
				continue;
			}
			E += Pixel.Value;
			if (Map.Width > 0 && Map.Height > 0)
			{
				const float U = (static_cast<float>(i % Map.Width) + 0.5f) / static_cast<float>(Map.Width);
				const float V = (static_cast<float>(i / Map.Width) + 0.5f) / static_cast<float>(Map.Height);
				FrameUSum += U * Pixel.Value;
				FrameVSum += V * Pixel.Value;
				FrameWeight += Pixel.Value;
			}
		}
		OutTrack[f] = E;
		MaxE = FMath::Max(MaxE, E);
		if (FrameWeight > 0.0)
		{
			AnchorUSum += FrameUSum / FrameWeight;
			AnchorVSum += FrameVSum / FrameWeight;
			AnchorWeight += 1.0;
		}
	}
	if (MaxE > 0.f)
	{
		for (float& Entry : OutTrack)
		{
			Entry /= MaxE;
		}
	}
	else
	{
		for (float& Entry : OutTrack)
		{
			Entry = 0.f;
		}
	}
	if (AnchorWeight > 0.0)
	{
		OutAnchorU = static_cast<float>(AnchorUSum / AnchorWeight);
		OutAnchorV = static_cast<float>(AnchorVSum / AnchorWeight);
	}
}

int32 CountDistinctColors(const TArray<TArray<FColor>>& Frames)
{
	TSet<uint32> Seen;
	for (const TArray<FColor>& Pixels : Frames)
	{
		for (const FColor& Pixel : Pixels)
		{
			if (Pixel.A == 0)
			{
				continue;
			}
			Seen.Add((static_cast<uint32>(Pixel.R) << 16) | (static_cast<uint32>(Pixel.G) << 8) | Pixel.B);
		}
	}
	return Seen.Num();
}
}
