// Pure logic tests for the pixel-art conversion. Every test must be able to fail:
// each one checks behavior that a plausible wrong implementation would break.
#include "GBPixelVfxConvert.h"

#include "GBPixelVfxTypes.h"
#include "Misc/AutomationTest.h"

namespace GBPixelVfxTestLocal
{
	using namespace GBPixelVfxConvert;

	FSettings MakeRampSettings()
	{
		FSettings Settings;
		Settings.FrameWidth = 8;
		Settings.FrameHeight = 1;
		Settings.ColorMode = TEXT("Ramp");
		Settings.AlphaMode = TEXT("Opacity");
		Settings.AlphaThreshold = 0.3f;
		Settings.LevelsPercentile = 1.f;
		Settings.Gamma = 1.f;
		TArray<FColor> Ramp;
		Ramp.Add(FColor(0, 0, 0));
		Ramp.Add(FColor(85, 85, 85));
		Ramp.Add(FColor(170, 170, 170));
		Ramp.Add(FColor(255, 255, 255));
		Settings.Ramps.Add(Ramp);
		return Settings;
	}

	// One row of grey pixels with full opacity, linear value X across the row.
	FWorkFrame MakeGreyRow(int32 Width, float Step)
	{
		FWorkFrame Frame;
		Frame.Width = Width;
		Frame.Height = 1;
		Frame.Color.SetNum(Width);
		Frame.Opacity.SetNum(Width);
		for (int32 i = 0; i < Width; ++i)
		{
			const float X = FMath::Min(1.f, static_cast<float>(i) * Step);
			Frame.Color[i] = FLinearColor(X, X, X);
			Frame.Opacity[i] = 1.f;
		}
		return Frame;
	}

	int32 RampIndexOf(const FSettings& Settings, const FColor& Color)
	{
		for (int32 i = 0; i < Settings.Ramps[0].Num(); ++i)
		{
			if (Settings.Ramps[0][i] == Color)
			{
				return i;
			}
		}
		return -1;
	}
}

IMPLEMENT_SIMPLE_AUTOMATION_TEST(FGBPixelVfxHardAlphaTest, "GBPixelVfx.Alpha.HardCutoff",
	EAutomationTestFlags::EditorContext | EAutomationTestFlags::EngineFilter)
bool FGBPixelVfxHardAlphaTest::RunTest(const FString& Parameters)
{
	using namespace GBPixelVfxConvert;
	using namespace GBPixelVfxTestLocal;
	FSettings Settings = MakeRampSettings();
	FWorkFrame Frame;
	Frame.Width = 4;
	Frame.Height = 1;
	Frame.Color.SetNum(4);
	Frame.Opacity.SetNum(4);
	// Bright white everywhere; only the opacity crosses the threshold between pixels 1 and 2.
	for (int32 i = 0; i < 4; ++i)
	{
		Frame.Color[i] = FLinearColor(4.f, 4.f, 4.f);
	}
	Frame.Opacity[0] = 0.f;
	Frame.Opacity[1] = 0.29f;
	Frame.Opacity[2] = 0.31f;
	Frame.Opacity[3] = 1.f;
	float Peak = 1.f;
	float Hot = 1.f;
	float Cold = 1.f;
	ComputePeaks({ Frame }, Settings, Peak, Hot, Cold);
	const FFrameMap Map = MapFrame(Frame, Settings, Peak, Hot, Cold);
	TArray<TArray<FColor>> Pixels;
	ResolveFrames({ Map }, Settings, Pixels);
	TestTrue(TEXT("pixel below the threshold is transparent"), Pixels[0][0].A == 0);
	TestTrue(TEXT("pixel just below the threshold is transparent"), Pixels[0][1].A == 0);
	TestTrue(TEXT("pixel just above the threshold is solid"), Pixels[0][2].A == 255);
	TestTrue(TEXT("pixel at full opacity is solid"), Pixels[0][3].A == 255);
	for (const TArray<FColor>& Row : Pixels)
	{
		for (const FColor& Pixel : Row)
		{
			TestTrue(TEXT("no partial alpha in the output"), Pixel.A == 0 || Pixel.A == 255);
		}
	}
	return true;
}

IMPLEMENT_SIMPLE_AUTOMATION_TEST(FGBPixelVfxRampOrderTest, "GBPixelVfx.Mapping.RampGradient",
	EAutomationTestFlags::EditorContext | EAutomationTestFlags::EngineFilter)
bool FGBPixelVfxRampOrderTest::RunTest(const FString& Parameters)
{
	using namespace GBPixelVfxConvert;
	using namespace GBPixelVfxTestLocal;
	FSettings Settings = MakeRampSettings();
	const FWorkFrame Frame = MakeGreyRow(8, 0.25f);
	float Peak = 1.f;
	float Hot = 1.f;
	float Cold = 1.f;
	ComputePeaks({ Frame }, Settings, Peak, Hot, Cold);
	const FFrameMap Map = MapFrame(Frame, Settings, Peak, Hot, Cold);
	TArray<TArray<FColor>> Pixels;
	ResolveFrames({ Map }, Settings, Pixels);
	int32 PrevIndex = -1;
	bool bSawFirst = false;
	bool bSawLast = false;
	for (int32 i = 0; i < 8; ++i)
	{
		const int32 Index = RampIndexOf(Settings, Pixels[0][i]);
		TestTrue(TEXT("every output color is one of the ramp colors"), Index >= 0);
		if (Index >= 0)
		{
			TestTrue(TEXT("ramp indices never decrease along a rising gradient"), Index >= PrevIndex);
			PrevIndex = Index;
			bSawFirst = bSawFirst || Index == 0;
			bSawLast = bSawLast || Index == Settings.Ramps[0].Num() - 1;
		}
	}
	TestTrue(TEXT("a full range gradient uses the first ramp color"), bSawFirst);
	TestTrue(TEXT("a full range gradient uses the last ramp color"), bSawLast);
	// Exact band boundaries for N = 4: k/N edges must map to the upper band, values
	// just below to the lower band (a floor(v*(N-1)) implementation would differ).
	auto IndexAtValue = [&Settings](float V) -> int32
	{
		FWorkFrame Frame;
		Frame.Width = 1;
		Frame.Height = 1;
		Frame.Color.SetNum(1);
		Frame.Opacity.SetNum(1);
		Frame.Color[0] = FLinearColor(V, V, V);
		Frame.Opacity[0] = 1.f;
		const FFrameMap Map = MapFrame(Frame, Settings, 1.f, 1.f, 1.f);
		FColor Resolved(0, 0, 0, 0);
		TArray<TArray<FColor>> Pixels;
		ResolveFrames({ Map }, Settings, Pixels);
		return RampIndexOf(Settings, Pixels[0][0]);
	};
	TestEqual(TEXT("just below 0.25 maps to band 0"), IndexAtValue(0.24f), 0);
	TestEqual(TEXT("exactly 0.25 maps to band 1"), IndexAtValue(0.25f), 1);
	TestEqual(TEXT("just below 0.5 maps to band 1"), IndexAtValue(0.49f), 1);
	TestEqual(TEXT("exactly 0.5 maps to band 2"), IndexAtValue(0.5f), 2);
	TestEqual(TEXT("just below 0.75 maps to band 2"), IndexAtValue(0.74f), 2);
	TestEqual(TEXT("exactly 0.75 maps to band 3"), IndexAtValue(0.75f), 3);
	return true;
}

IMPLEMENT_SIMPLE_AUTOMATION_TEST(FGBPixelVfxSplitSortTest, "GBPixelVfx.Mapping.SplitRouting",
	EAutomationTestFlags::EditorContext | EAutomationTestFlags::EngineFilter)
bool FGBPixelVfxSplitSortTest::RunTest(const FString& Parameters)
{
	using namespace GBPixelVfxConvert;
	using namespace GBPixelVfxTestLocal;
	FSettings Settings = MakeRampSettings();
	Settings.ColorMode = TEXT("Split");
	Settings.AlphaMode = TEXT("Opacity");
	Settings.HotLuminance = 0.55f;
	Settings.HotSaturation = 0.9f; // only luminance decides in this test
	TArray<FColor> ColdRamp;
	ColdRamp.Add(FColor(16, 16, 16));
	ColdRamp.Add(FColor(64, 64, 64));
	Settings.Ramps.Add(ColdRamp);
	FWorkFrame Frame;
	Frame.Width = 4;
	Frame.Height = 1;
	Frame.Color.SetNum(4);
	Frame.Opacity.SetNum(4);
	// Luminance-only hot: bright neutral grey (high luminance, zero saturation).
	Frame.Color[0] = FLinearColor(0.8f, 0.8f, 0.8f);
	// Saturation-only hot: dim but vivid (low luminance, high saturation).
	Frame.Color[1] = FLinearColor(0.4f, 0.02f, 0.02f);
	// Hot by both (bright saturated orange). Cold: dim neutral grey.
	Frame.Color[2] = FLinearColor(3.f, 0.9f, 0.1f);
	Frame.Color[3] = FLinearColor(0.2f, 0.2f, 0.2f);
	for (int32 i = 0; i < 4; ++i)
	{
		Frame.Opacity[i] = 1.f;
	}
	float Peak = 1.f;
	float Hot = 1.f;
	float Cold = 1.f;
	ComputePeaks({ Frame }, Settings, Peak, Hot, Cold);
	const FFrameMap Map = MapFrame(Frame, Settings, Peak, Hot, Cold);
	TestTrue(TEXT("luminance-only pixel is hot"), Map.Pixels[0].bHot);
	TestTrue(TEXT("luminance-only hot pixel uses ramp 0"), Map.Pixels[0].Ramp == 0);
	TestTrue(TEXT("saturation-only pixel is hot"), Map.Pixels[1].bHot);
	TestTrue(TEXT("saturation-only hot pixel uses ramp 0"), Map.Pixels[1].Ramp == 0);
	TestTrue(TEXT("bright saturated pixel is hot"), Map.Pixels[2].bHot);
	TestTrue(TEXT("hot pixel uses ramp 0"), Map.Pixels[2].Ramp == 0);
	TestFalse(TEXT("dim grey pixel is not hot"), Map.Pixels[3].bHot);
	TestTrue(TEXT("cold pixel uses ramp 1"), Map.Pixels[3].Ramp == 1);
	// Hot and cold levels are normalized independently: the hot peak (brightest hot
	// pixel) differs from the cold peak, and each pixel's value uses its own peak.
	TestTrue(TEXT("hot and cold peaks differ"), !FMath::IsNearlyEqual(Hot, Cold, 0.01f));
	const float HotLum0 = PixelLuminance(FLinearColor(0.8f, 0.8f, 0.8f));
	TestTrue(TEXT("luminance-only value uses the hot peak"),
		FMath::IsNearlyEqual(Map.Pixels[0].Value, FMath::Clamp(HotLum0 / Hot, 0.f, 1.f), 0.01f));
	const float ColdLum = PixelLuminance(FLinearColor(0.2f, 0.2f, 0.2f));
	TestTrue(TEXT("cold value uses the cold peak"),
		FMath::IsNearlyEqual(Map.Pixels[3].Value, FMath::Clamp(ColdLum / Cold, 0.f, 1.f), 0.01f));
	TArray<TArray<FColor>> Pixels;
	ResolveFrames({ Map }, Settings, Pixels);
	TestEqual(TEXT("hot pixel resolves to a ramp 0 color"), Pixels[0][2], Settings.Ramps[0][Map.Pixels[2].Index]);
	TestEqual(TEXT("cold pixel resolves to a ramp 1 color"), Pixels[0][3], Settings.Ramps[1][Map.Pixels[3].Index]);
	return true;
}

IMPLEMENT_SIMPLE_AUTOMATION_TEST(FGBPixelVfxHysteresisHoldTest, "GBPixelVfx.Temporal.HysteresisBand",
	EAutomationTestFlags::EditorContext | EAutomationTestFlags::EngineFilter)
bool FGBPixelVfxHysteresisHoldTest::RunTest(const FString& Parameters)
{
	using namespace GBPixelVfxConvert;
	using namespace GBPixelVfxTestLocal;
	FSettings Settings = MakeRampSettings();
	Settings.Hysteresis = 0.05f;
	Settings.LevelsPercentile = 1.f;
	// A value that sits in band 2 of 4 ([0.5, 0.75)) but wobbles across the lower edge by
	// less than the margin must keep one color; a second run wobbles far past it and must change.
	auto RunWobble = [&Settings](float Base, float Wobble) -> TArray<int32>
	{
		TArray<int32> Indices;
		FFrameMap Prev;
		bool bFirst = true;
		for (int32 f = 0; f < 6; ++f)
		{
			const float V = (f % 2 == 0) ? Base - Wobble : Base + Wobble;
			FWorkFrame Frame;
			Frame.Width = 1;
			Frame.Height = 1;
			Frame.Color.SetNum(1);
			Frame.Opacity.SetNum(1);
			Frame.Color[0] = FLinearColor(V, V, V);
			Frame.Opacity[0] = 1.f;
			// Fixed peak of 1 so V is the levels value directly.
			FFrameMap Map = MapFrame(Frame, Settings, 1.f, 1.f, 1.f);
			if (!bFirst)
			{
				ApplyHysteresis(Map, Prev, Settings);
			}
			bFirst = false;
			Prev = Map;
			Indices.Add(Map.Pixels[0].Index);
		}
		return Indices;
	};
	// Band 2 of 4 spans [0.5, 0.75); wobble 0.02 around 0.51 stays inside the widened band.
	const TArray<int32> Held = RunWobble(0.51f, 0.02f);
	bool bAllSame = true;
	for (int32 Index : Held)
	{
		bAllSame = bAllSame && Index == Held[0];
	}
	TestTrue(TEXT("a wobble inside the margin keeps one color over 6 frames"), bAllSame);
	// Wobble 0.3 around 0.6 crosses into far bands: the color must change at least once.
	const TArray<int32> Moved = RunWobble(0.6f, 0.3f);
	bool bChanged = false;
	for (int32 Index : Moved)
	{
		bChanged = bChanged || Index != Moved[0];
	}
	TestTrue(TEXT("a wobble far past the margin changes the color"), bChanged);
	return true;
}

IMPLEMENT_SIMPLE_AUTOMATION_TEST(FGBPixelVfxCleanupIslandsTest, "GBPixelVfx.Cleanup.IslandsAndHoles",
	EAutomationTestFlags::EditorContext | EAutomationTestFlags::EngineFilter)
bool FGBPixelVfxCleanupIslandsTest::RunTest(const FString& Parameters)
{
	using namespace GBPixelVfxConvert;
	// A 2-pixel island in a 6x6 frame.
	auto MakeIsland = []() -> FFrameMap
	{
		FFrameMap Map;
		Map.Width = 6;
		Map.Height = 6;
		Map.Pixels.SetNum(36);
		Map.Pixels[0].bSolid = true;
		Map.Pixels[1].bSolid = true;
		return Map;
	};
	FFrameMap Removed = MakeIsland();
	RemoveSmallIslands(Removed, 3);
	TestEqual(TEXT("a 2-pixel island is removed when the minimum is 3"), Removed.SolidCount(), 0);
	FFrameMap Kept = MakeIsland();
	RemoveSmallIslands(Kept, 2);
	TestEqual(TEXT("a 2-pixel island is kept when the minimum is 2"), Kept.SolidCount(), 2);

	// A single transparent hole fully surrounded by solid pixels is filled.
	FFrameMap Hole;
	Hole.Width = 3;
	Hole.Height = 3;
	Hole.Pixels.SetNum(9);
	for (int32 i = 0; i < 9; ++i)
	{
		Hole.Pixels[i].bSolid = true;
		Hole.Pixels[i].Ramp = 0;
		Hole.Pixels[i].Index = 2;
	}
	Hole.Pixels[4].bSolid = false;
	FillOnePixelHoles(Hole);
	TestTrue(TEXT("a one-pixel hole is filled"), Hole.Pixels[4].bSolid);
	TestEqual(TEXT("the filled hole takes the neighbor color"), Hole.Pixels[4].Index, 2);

	// Majority beats first neighbor: right neighbor (read first) is color 0, the other
	// three are color 5, so the hole must take color 5, not the first neighbor's color.
	FFrameMap Majority;
	Majority.Width = 3;
	Majority.Height = 3;
	Majority.Pixels.SetNum(9);
	for (int32 i = 0; i < 9; ++i)
	{
		Majority.Pixels[i].bSolid = true;
		Majority.Pixels[i].Ramp = 0;
		Majority.Pixels[i].Index = 5;
	}
	Majority.Pixels[5].Ramp = 0; // right of center (x+1): read first
	Majority.Pixels[5].Index = 0;
	Majority.Pixels[4].bSolid = false;
	FillOnePixelHoles(Majority);
	TestTrue(TEXT("a majority hole is filled"), Majority.Pixels[4].bSolid);
	TestEqual(TEXT("the filled hole takes the majority color, not the first neighbor"), Majority.Pixels[4].Index, 5);

	// An unfillable gap (a transparent pixel with a transparent neighbor) stays transparent,
	// so a filler that fills every transparent pixel fails here.
	FFrameMap Gap;
	Gap.Width = 3;
	Gap.Height = 3;
	Gap.Pixels.SetNum(9);
	for (int32 i = 0; i < 9; ++i)
	{
		Gap.Pixels[i].bSolid = true;
		Gap.Pixels[i].Ramp = 0;
		Gap.Pixels[i].Index = 2;
	}
	Gap.Pixels[4].bSolid = false;
	Gap.Pixels[7].bSolid = false; // below the hole: the hole has a transparent neighbor
	FillOnePixelHoles(Gap);
	TestFalse(TEXT("a gap with a transparent neighbor is not filled"), Gap.Pixels[4].bSolid);
	return true;
}

IMPLEMENT_SIMPLE_AUTOMATION_TEST(FGBPixelVfxOutlineShapeTest, "GBPixelVfx.Cleanup.OutlineRing",
	EAutomationTestFlags::EditorContext | EAutomationTestFlags::EngineFilter)
bool FGBPixelVfxOutlineShapeTest::RunTest(const FString& Parameters)
{
	using namespace GBPixelVfxConvert;
	using namespace GBPixelVfxTestLocal;
	FSettings Settings = MakeRampSettings();
	Settings.Outline = TEXT("Black");
	FFrameMap Map;
	Map.Width = 6;
	Map.Height = 6;
	Map.Pixels.SetNum(36);
	for (int32 y = 2; y < 4; ++y)
	{
		for (int32 x = 2; x < 4; ++x)
		{
			FMappedPixel& Pixel = Map.Pixels[y * 6 + x];
			Pixel.bSolid = true;
			Pixel.Ramp = 0;
			Pixel.Index = 1;
		}
	}
	TArray<TArray<FColor>> Pixels;
	ResolveFrames({ Map }, Settings, Pixels);
	const FColor Outline(0, 0, 0, 255);
	// The 8 4-adjacent pixels around the 2x2 block.
	const int32 Expected[8][2] = { {2,1},{3,1},{1,2},{4,2},{1,3},{4,3},{2,4},{3,4} };
	for (const auto& Cell : Expected)
	{
		TestEqual(TEXT("4-adjacent pixel is outlined"), Pixels[0][Cell[1] * 6 + Cell[0]], Outline);
	}
	TestEqual(TEXT("distant pixel stays transparent"), Pixels[0][0], FColor(0, 0, 0, 0));
	TestEqual(TEXT("solid pixel keeps its color"), Pixels[0][2 * 6 + 2], Settings.Ramps[0][1]);

	// A block in the corner must not draw outside the frame.
	FFrameMap Corner;
	Corner.Width = 6;
	Corner.Height = 6;
	Corner.Pixels.SetNum(36);
	Corner.Pixels[0].bSolid = true;
	Corner.Pixels[0].Ramp = 0;
	Corner.Pixels[0].Index = 1;
	Corner.Pixels[1].bSolid = true;
	Corner.Pixels[1].Ramp = 0;
	Corner.Pixels[1].Index = 1;
	Corner.Pixels[6].bSolid = true;
	Corner.Pixels[6].Ramp = 0;
	Corner.Pixels[6].Index = 1;
	Corner.Pixels[7].bSolid = true;
	Corner.Pixels[7].Ramp = 0;
	Corner.Pixels[7].Index = 1;
	TArray<TArray<FColor>> CornerPixels;
	ResolveFrames({ Corner }, Settings, CornerPixels);
	TestTrue(TEXT("corner block resolves without crashing"), CornerPixels[0].Num() == 36);
	int32 Outlined = 0;
	for (const FColor& Pixel : CornerPixels[0])
	{
		if (Pixel == Outline)
		{
			++Outlined;
		}
	}
	TestEqual(TEXT("a 2x2 block in the corner gets 4 in-frame outline pixels"), Outlined, 4);
	return true;
}

IMPLEMENT_SIMPLE_AUTOMATION_TEST(FGBPixelVfxLoopBlendFlowTest, "GBPixelVfx.Temporal.LoopBlend",
	EAutomationTestFlags::EditorContext | EAutomationTestFlags::EngineFilter)
bool FGBPixelVfxLoopBlendFlowTest::RunTest(const FString& Parameters)
{
	using namespace GBPixelVfxConvert;
	auto SolidFrame = [](float Bright, float Opacity) -> FWorkFrame
	{
		FWorkFrame Frame;
		Frame.Width = 2;
		Frame.Height = 2;
		Frame.Color.SetNum(4);
		Frame.Opacity.SetNum(4);
		for (int32 i = 0; i < 4; ++i)
		{
			Frame.Color[i] = FLinearColor(Bright, Bright, Bright);
			Frame.Opacity[i] = Opacity;
		}
		return Frame;
	};
	TArray<FWorkFrame> Frames;
	Frames.Add(SolidFrame(1.f, 1.f)); // frame 0: dim, opaque
	Frames.Add(SolidFrame(2.f, 0.5f)); // frame 1: mid, half coverage
	Frames.Add(SolidFrame(1.f, 1.f)); // frame 2: dim
	Frames.Add(SolidFrame(1.f, 1.f)); // frame 3: dim, outside the blend window
	Frames.Add(SolidFrame(9.f, 0.2f)); // extra 0: bright, thin
	Frames.Add(SolidFrame(5.f, 0.8f)); // extra 1: distinct from extra 0
	ApplyLoopBlend(Frames, 4, 2, true);
	TestEqual(TEXT("the extra frames are dropped"), Frames.Num(), 4);
	// Frame 0 blends 75% extra0 / 25% self on RGB and opacity.
	const float Frame0 = Frames[0].Color[0].R;
	TestTrue(TEXT("frame 0 mostly equals extra frame 0"), FMath::Abs(Frame0 - 7.f) < 0.01f);
	TestTrue(TEXT("frame 0 opacity blends too"), FMath::Abs(Frames[0].Opacity[0] - 0.4f) < 0.01f);
	// Frame 1 blends 25% extra1 / 75% self: an implementation that only blends frame 0 fails here.
	const float Frame1 = Frames[1].Color[0].R;
	TestTrue(TEXT("frame 1 blends extra frame 1"), FMath::Abs(Frame1 - 2.75f) < 0.01f);
	TestTrue(TEXT("frame 1 opacity blends too"), FMath::Abs(Frames[1].Opacity[0] - 0.575f) < 0.01f);
	// Frame 3 is past the blend window: the last frame of the clip is unchanged.
	const float Frame3 = Frames[3].Color[0].R;
	TestTrue(TEXT("the last frame is unchanged"), FMath::Abs(Frame3 - 1.f) < 0.01f);
	// Non-loop clips never blend: extras are dropped and kept frames are untouched.
	TArray<FWorkFrame> Flat;
	Flat.Add(SolidFrame(1.f, 1.f));
	Flat.Add(SolidFrame(2.f, 0.5f));
	Flat.Add(SolidFrame(9.f, 0.2f));
	ApplyLoopBlend(Flat, 2, 2, false);
	TestEqual(TEXT("non-loop drops the extras"), Flat.Num(), 2);
	TestTrue(TEXT("non-loop leaves frame 0 alone"), FMath::Abs(Flat[0].Color[0].R - 1.f) < 0.01f);
	TestTrue(TEXT("non-loop leaves frame 1 opacity alone"), FMath::Abs(Flat[1].Opacity[0] - 0.5f) < 0.01f);
	return true;
}

IMPLEMENT_SIMPLE_AUTOMATION_TEST(FGBPixelVfxClipPlaybackTest, "GBPixelVfx.Clip.PlaybackHelpers",
	EAutomationTestFlags::EditorContext | EAutomationTestFlags::EngineFilter)
bool FGBPixelVfxClipPlaybackTest::RunTest(const FString& Parameters)
{
	FGBPixelVfxClip Clip;
	Clip.Columns = 8;
	Clip.FrameWidth = 48;
	Clip.FrameHeight = 64;
	Clip.FrameCount = 24;
	Clip.Fps = 12;
	Clip.bLoop = true;
	// Frame 9 of an 8-column atlas: column 1, row 1 of a 384x192 atlas.
	const FVector4f Rect = FGBPixelVfxClip::FrameRect(Clip, 9);
	TestTrue(TEXT("frame 9 offset U is 1/8"), FMath::IsNearlyEqual(Rect.X, 0.125f));
	TestTrue(TEXT("frame 9 offset V is 1/3"), FMath::IsNearlyEqual(Rect.Y, 1.f / 3.f, 0.001f));
	TestTrue(TEXT("frame 9 scale U is 1/8"), FMath::IsNearlyEqual(Rect.Z, 0.125f));
	TestTrue(TEXT("frame 9 scale V is 1/3"), FMath::IsNearlyEqual(Rect.W, 1.f / 3.f, 0.001f));

	bool bFinished = false;
	TestEqual(TEXT("a looping clip wraps"), FGBPixelVfxClip::FrameAtTime(Clip, 2.5f, bFinished), 6);
	TestFalse(TEXT("a looping clip never finishes"), bFinished);
	Clip.bLoop = false;
	TestEqual(TEXT("a one-shot clamps to the last frame"), FGBPixelVfxClip::FrameAtTime(Clip, 99.f, bFinished), 23);
	TestTrue(TEXT("a one-shot finishes past its end"), bFinished);
	bFinished = true;
	TestEqual(TEXT("a one-shot shows frame 0 at time 0"), FGBPixelVfxClip::FrameAtTime(Clip, 0.f, bFinished), 0);
	TestFalse(TEXT("a one-shot is not finished at time 0"), bFinished);
	// The last frame is shown for a full frame duration: mid-last-frame is not finished.
	const float LastStart = (Clip.FrameCount - 1) / static_cast<float>(Clip.Fps);
	const float ClipEnd = Clip.FrameCount / static_cast<float>(Clip.Fps);
	bFinished = true;
	TestEqual(TEXT("a one-shot shows the last frame mid-frame"), FGBPixelVfxClip::FrameAtTime(Clip, LastStart + 0.5f / Clip.Fps, bFinished), 23);
	TestFalse(TEXT("a one-shot is not finished mid-last-frame"), bFinished);
	bFinished = false;
	TestEqual(TEXT("a one-shot clamps at the exact end"), FGBPixelVfxClip::FrameAtTime(Clip, ClipEnd, bFinished), 23);
	TestTrue(TEXT("a one-shot finishes at the exact end"), bFinished);

	// JSON round trip keeps the light track.
	Clip.Id = FName(TEXT("RoundTrip"));
	Clip.AtlasFile = TEXT("RoundTrip.png");
	Clip.Light.bEnabled = true;
	Clip.Light.Track = { 0.2f, 0.9f, 1.f };
	FString Json;
	TestTrue(TEXT("clip serializes to JSON"), Clip.SaveToJsonString(Json));
	FGBPixelVfxClip Back;
	TestTrue(TEXT("clip parses back from JSON"), Back.LoadFromJsonString(Json));
	TestEqual(TEXT("the light track survives the round trip"), Back.Light.Track.Num(), 3);
	if (Back.Light.Track.Num() == 3)
	{
		// Every entry must survive, not just the count and the peak.
		const float Expected[3] = { 0.2f, 0.9f, 1.f };
		for (int32 i = 0; i < 3; ++i)
		{
			TestTrue(TEXT("every track entry survives the round trip"),
				FMath::IsNearlyEqual(Back.Light.Track[i], Expected[i], 0.001f));
		}
	}
	TestTrue(TEXT("the id survives the round trip"), Back.Id == FName(TEXT("RoundTrip")));
	return true;
}

IMPLEMENT_SIMPLE_AUTOMATION_TEST(FGBPixelVfxQuadPivotTest, "GBPixelVfx.Clip.QuadPivotPlacement",
	EAutomationTestFlags::EditorContext | EAutomationTestFlags::EngineFilter)
bool FGBPixelVfxQuadPivotTest::RunTest(const FString& Parameters)
{
	FGBPixelVfxClip Clip;
	Clip.WorldWidthCm = 200.f;
	Clip.WorldHeightCm = 400.f;
	Clip.PivotU = 0.5f;
	Clip.PivotV = 1.f;
	Clip.Light.AnchorU = 0.5f;
	Clip.Light.AnchorV = 0.5f;
	// Upright quad: local X = world X, local Y = world Z (up), normal faces -X.
	const FQuat R(FMatrix(FVector(0.f, -1.f, 0.f), FVector(0.f, 0.f, 1.f), FVector(-1.f, 0.f, 0.f),
		FVector::ZeroVector).Rotator());
	const FVector Comp(100.f, 200.f, 0.f);
	FVector QuadLoc;
	FVector LightLoc;
	FGBPixelVfxClip::ComputeQuadAndLightPlacement(Clip, 1.f, Comp, R, QuadLoc, LightLoc);
	// PivotV = 1 (bottom of the frame): the quad center is H/2 above the component.
	TestTrue(TEXT("pivot bottom puts the quad center H/2 up"),
		FMath::IsNearlyEqual(QuadLoc.Z, 200.f, 0.01f));
	TestTrue(TEXT("pivot bottom does not shift sideways"),
		FMath::IsNearlyEqual(QuadLoc.X, Comp.X, 0.01f)
		&& FMath::IsNearlyEqual(QuadLoc.Y, Comp.Y, 0.01f));
	return true;
}

IMPLEMENT_SIMPLE_AUTOMATION_TEST(FGBPixelVfxLightAnchorTest, "GBPixelVfx.Clip.LightAnchorPlacement",
	EAutomationTestFlags::EditorContext | EAutomationTestFlags::EngineFilter)
bool FGBPixelVfxLightAnchorTest::RunTest(const FString& Parameters)
{
	FGBPixelVfxClip Clip;
	Clip.WorldWidthCm = 200.f;
	Clip.WorldHeightCm = 400.f;
	Clip.PivotU = 0.5f;
	Clip.PivotV = 1.f;
	Clip.Light.AnchorU = 0.5f;
	Clip.Light.AnchorV = 0.5f;
	// Same upright frame as the pivot test.
	const FQuat R(FMatrix(FVector(0.f, -1.f, 0.f), FVector(0.f, 0.f, 1.f), FVector(-1.f, 0.f, 0.f),
		FVector::ZeroVector).Rotator());
	const FVector Comp(100.f, 200.f, 0.f);
	FVector QuadLoc;
	FVector LightLoc;
	FGBPixelVfxClip::ComputeQuadAndLightPlacement(Clip, 1.f, Comp, R, QuadLoc, LightLoc);
	// AnchorV = 0.5 is H/2 above the pivot: the light sits H/2 above the component,
	// pushed 10 cm toward the camera (quad normal = -X here).
	TestTrue(TEXT("mid-frame anchor puts the light H/2 above the pivot"),
		FMath::IsNearlyEqual(LightLoc.Z, 200.f, 0.01f));
	TestTrue(TEXT("the light keeps the component Y"), FMath::IsNearlyEqual(LightLoc.Y, Comp.Y, 0.01f));
	TestTrue(TEXT("the light is pushed 10 cm toward the camera"),
		FMath::IsNearlyEqual(LightLoc.X, Comp.X - 10.f, 0.01f));
	return true;
}

IMPLEMENT_SIMPLE_AUTOMATION_TEST(FGBPixelVfxColorClusterTest, "GBPixelVfx.Cleanup.ColorClusters",
	EAutomationTestFlags::EditorContext | EAutomationTestFlags::EngineFilter)
bool FGBPixelVfxColorClusterTest::RunTest(const FString& Parameters)
{
	using namespace GBPixelVfxConvert;
	FSettings Settings = GBPixelVfxTestLocal::MakeRampSettings();
	// Two colors in ramp 0; the frame is all color 1 with a 2-pixel color-2 speck.
	FFrameMap Map;
	Map.Width = 6;
	Map.Height = 4;
	Map.Pixels.SetNum(24);
	for (int32 i = 0; i < 24; ++i)
	{
		Map.Pixels[i].bSolid = true;
		Map.Pixels[i].Ramp = 0;
		Map.Pixels[i].Index = 1;
		Map.Pixels[i].Value = 0.5f;
	}
	Map.Pixels[7].Index = 2;
	Map.Pixels[8].Index = 2;
	FFrameMap Cleaned = Map;
	RecolorSmallColorClusters(Cleaned, Settings, 3);
	TestEqual(TEXT("a 2-pixel speck is recolored when the minimum is 3"), Cleaned.SolidCount(), 24);
	TestEqual(TEXT("the speck takes the surrounding color"), Cleaned.Pixels[7].Index, 1);
	TestEqual(TEXT("the speck keeps its ramp"), Cleaned.Pixels[7].Ramp, 0);
	FFrameMap Kept = Map;
	RecolorSmallColorClusters(Kept, Settings, 2);
	TestEqual(TEXT("a 2-pixel region is kept when the minimum is 2"), Kept.Pixels[7].Index, 2);
	// Off: nothing changes.
	FFrameMap Off = Map;
	RecolorSmallColorClusters(Off, Settings, 0);
	TestEqual(TEXT("clusters off leaves the speck alone"), Off.Pixels[7].Index, 2);
	return true;
}

IMPLEMENT_SIMPLE_AUTOMATION_TEST(FGBPixelVfxFadeOutTest, "GBPixelVfx.Temporal.FadeOut",
	EAutomationTestFlags::EditorContext | EAutomationTestFlags::EngineFilter)
bool FGBPixelVfxFadeOutTest::RunTest(const FString& Parameters)
{
	using namespace GBPixelVfxConvert;
	auto SolidMap = []() -> FFrameMap
	{
		FFrameMap Map;
		Map.Width = 8;
		Map.Height = 8;
		Map.Pixels.SetNum(64);
		for (int32 i = 0; i < 64; ++i)
		{
			Map.Pixels[i].bSolid = true;
			Map.Pixels[i].Ramp = 0;
			Map.Pixels[i].Index = 1;
			Map.Pixels[i].Value = 1.f;
		}
		return Map;
	};
	// Dissolve: removals are monotonic (frame-space pattern) and grow with t.
	FSettings Dissolve;
	Dissolve.bLoop = false;
	Dissolve.FadeOutFrames = 2;
	Dissolve.FadeOutMode = TEXT("Dissolve");
	TArray<FFrameMap> DissolveMaps;
	DissolveMaps.Add(SolidMap());
	DissolveMaps.Add(SolidMap());
	DissolveMaps.Add(SolidMap());
	ApplyFadeOut(DissolveMaps, Dissolve);
	const int32 First = DissolveMaps[1].SolidCount();
	const int32 Last = DissolveMaps[2].SolidCount();
	TestTrue(TEXT("dissolve removes pixels over the fade"), First < 64 && Last < First);
	TestEqual(TEXT("dissolve leaves pre-fade frames alone"), DissolveMaps[0].SolidCount(), 64);
	// Removed pixels never come back: every solid pixel of the last frame is solid in the first faded frame.
	for (int32 i = 0; i < 64; ++i)
	{
		if (DissolveMaps[2].Pixels[i].bSolid)
		{
			TestTrue(TEXT("dissolve removals never return"), DissolveMaps[1].Pixels[i].bSolid);
		}
	}
	// Erode: a solid block shrinks from the transparent border inward.
	FSettings Erode;
	Erode.bLoop = false;
	Erode.FadeOutFrames = 1;
	Erode.FadeOutMode = TEXT("Erode");
	TArray<FFrameMap> ErodeMaps;
	FFrameMap Block = SolidMap();
	Block.Pixels[0].bSolid = false; // one transparent corner seeds the erosion
	ErodeMaps.Add(Block);
	ApplyFadeOut(ErodeMaps, Erode);
	TestTrue(TEXT("erode removes the border first"), !ErodeMaps[0].Pixels[1].bSolid || !ErodeMaps[0].Pixels[8].bSolid);
	TestTrue(TEXT("erode keeps the deep interior"), ErodeMaps[0].Pixels[27].bSolid);
	// Looping clips never fade.
	FSettings Looped;
	Looped.bLoop = true;
	Looped.FadeOutFrames = 2;
	Looped.FadeOutMode = TEXT("Dissolve");
	TArray<FFrameMap> LoopedMaps;
	LoopedMaps.Add(SolidMap());
	LoopedMaps.Add(SolidMap());
	ApplyFadeOut(LoopedMaps, Looped);
	TestEqual(TEXT("looping clips do not fade"), LoopedMaps[0].SolidCount(), 64);
	TestEqual(TEXT("looping clips do not fade (last)"), LoopedMaps[1].SolidCount(), 64);
	return true;
}

IMPLEMENT_SIMPLE_AUTOMATION_TEST(FGBPixelVfxPercentilePeakTest, "GBPixelVfx.Levels.PercentilePeak",
	EAutomationTestFlags::EditorContext | EAutomationTestFlags::EngineFilter)
bool FGBPixelVfxPercentilePeakTest::RunTest(const FString& Parameters)
{
	using namespace GBPixelVfxConvert;
	// Ten visible pixels with luminance 0..9: the 50th percentile (nearest rank) is 4,
	// so an implementation using the maximum (9) fails the value check below.
	FSettings Settings = GBPixelVfxTestLocal::MakeRampSettings();
	Settings.LevelsPercentile = 0.5f;
	Settings.AlphaMode = TEXT("Opacity");
	Settings.AlphaThreshold = 0.0f;
	FWorkFrame Frame;
	Frame.Width = 10;
	Frame.Height = 1;
	Frame.Color.SetNum(10);
	Frame.Opacity.SetNum(10);
	for (int32 i = 0; i < 10; ++i)
	{
		const float X = static_cast<float>(i);
		Frame.Color[i] = FLinearColor(X, X, X);
		Frame.Opacity[i] = 1.f;
	}
	float Peak = 1.f;
	float Hot = 1.f;
	float Cold = 1.f;
	ComputePeaks({ Frame }, Settings, Peak, Hot, Cold);
	TestTrue(TEXT("the percentile peak is 4, not the max"),
		FMath::IsNearlyEqual(Peak, 4.f, 0.01f));
	return true;
}

IMPLEMENT_SIMPLE_AUTOMATION_TEST(FGBPixelVfxLightTrackTest, "GBPixelVfx.Light.TrackEnergyAnchor",
	EAutomationTestFlags::EditorContext | EAutomationTestFlags::EngineFilter)
bool FGBPixelVfxLightTrackTest::RunTest(const FString& Parameters)
{
	using namespace GBPixelVfxConvert;
	// Two 2x1 frames: frame 0 has one hot pixel (v = 1) at U = 0.25, frame 1 has two
	// hot pixels (v = 1 each) at U = 0.25 and 0.75. Energies are 1 and 2, so the
	// normalized track is [0.5, 1]; the anchor averages the per-frame centroids.
	FSettings Settings = GBPixelVfxTestLocal::MakeRampSettings();
	Settings.bLightEnabled = true;
	FFrameMap F0;
	F0.Width = 2;
	F0.Height = 1;
	F0.Pixels.SetNum(2);
	F0.Pixels[0].bSolid = true;
	F0.Pixels[0].bHot = true;
	F0.Pixels[0].Ramp = 0;
	F0.Pixels[0].Index = 1;
	F0.Pixels[0].Value = 1.f;
	FFrameMap F1;
	F1.Width = 2;
	F1.Height = 1;
	F1.Pixels.SetNum(2);
	F1.Pixels[0].bSolid = true;
	F1.Pixels[0].bHot = true;
	F1.Pixels[0].Ramp = 0;
	F1.Pixels[0].Index = 1;
	F1.Pixels[0].Value = 1.f;
	F1.Pixels[1].bSolid = true;
	F1.Pixels[1].bHot = true;
	F1.Pixels[1].Ramp = 0;
	F1.Pixels[1].Index = 1;
	F1.Pixels[1].Value = 1.f;
	TArray<float> Track;
	float AnchorU = 0.f;
	float AnchorV = 0.f;
	ComputeLightTrack({ F0, F1 }, Settings, Track, AnchorU, AnchorV);
	TestEqual(TEXT("two frames produce two track values"), Track.Num(), 2);
	if (Track.Num() == 2)
	{
		// A constant-lighting implementation returning 1 for both fails here.
		TestTrue(TEXT("dimmer frame has lower energy"), FMath::IsNearlyEqual(Track[0], 0.5f, 0.01f));
		TestTrue(TEXT("brightest frame normalizes to 1"), FMath::IsNearlyEqual(Track[1], 1.f, 0.01f));
	}
	// Frame centroids: 0.25 and 0.5; the anchor averages them to 0.375.
	TestTrue(TEXT("the anchor U averages the frame centroids"), FMath::IsNearlyEqual(AnchorU, 0.375f, 0.01f));
	TestTrue(TEXT("the anchor V is the row middle"), FMath::IsNearlyEqual(AnchorV, 0.5f, 0.01f));
	return true;
}

IMPLEMENT_SIMPLE_AUTOMATION_TEST(FGBPixelVfxFractionalAlphaTest, "GBPixelVfx.Alpha.FractionalUnpremultiply",
	EAutomationTestFlags::EditorContext | EAutomationTestFlags::EngineFilter)
bool FGBPixelVfxFractionalAlphaTest::RunTest(const FString& Parameters)
{
	using namespace GBPixelVfxConvert;
	// Coverage 0.5 over HDR red (2, 0, 0): premultiplied RGB is (1, 0, 0), so the
	// un-premultiplied color is (2, 0, 0) with luminance ~0.43. An implementation that
	// multiplies by alpha again would see (0.5, 0, 0) and map a much darker band.
	FSettings Settings = GBPixelVfxTestLocal::MakeRampSettings();
	Settings.AlphaMode = TEXT("Opacity");
	FWorkFrame Frame;
	Frame.Width = 1;
	Frame.Height = 1;
	Frame.Color.SetNum(1);
	Frame.Opacity.SetNum(1);
	Frame.Color[0] = FLinearColor(1.f, 0.f, 0.f);
	Frame.Opacity[0] = 0.5f;
	float Peak = 1.f;
	float Hot = 1.f;
	float Cold = 1.f;
	ComputePeaks({ Frame }, Settings, Peak, Hot, Cold);
	TestTrue(TEXT("the peak sees the un-premultiplied color"),
		FMath::IsNearlyEqual(Peak, 0.4252f, 0.01f));
	const FFrameMap Map = MapFrame(Frame, Settings, Peak, Hot, Cold);
	TestTrue(TEXT("fractional coverage is solid"), Map.Pixels[0].bSolid);
	TestTrue(TEXT("fractional coverage maps to the top band"),
		Map.Pixels[0].Value > 0.99f && Map.Pixels[0].Index == Settings.Ramps[0].Num() - 1);
	return true;
}

// UGBPixelVfxSubsystem: clip JSON + PNG atlas loading with caching.
#include "GBPixelVfxSubsystem.h"

#include "Engine/Texture2D.h"
#include "HAL/FileManager.h"
#include "ImageCore.h"
#include "ImageUtils.h"
#include "Misc/FileHelper.h"
#include "Misc/Paths.h"
#include "Modules/ModuleManager.h"

FString UGBPixelVfxSubsystem::VfxDataDir()
{
	return FPaths::ProjectContentDir() / TEXT("Data/Vfx");
}

bool UGBPixelVfxSubsystem::LoadClip(FName Id) const
{
	if (Id.IsNone())
	{
		return false;
	}
	if (ClipCache.Contains(Id) || MissingIds.Contains(Id))
	{
		return ClipCache.Contains(Id);
	}
	const FString JsonPath = VfxDataDir() / (Id.ToString() + TEXT(".json"));
	FString JsonText;
	if (!FFileHelper::LoadFileToString(JsonText, *JsonPath))
	{
		UE_LOG(LogGBPixelVfx, Warning, TEXT("PixelVfx clip '%s' is missing (%s)."), *Id.ToString(), *JsonPath);
		MissingIds.Add(Id);
		return false;
	}
	FGBPixelVfxClip Clip;
	if (!Clip.LoadFromJsonString(JsonText))
	{
		UE_LOG(LogGBPixelVfx, Warning, TEXT("PixelVfx clip '%s' is broken (%s)."), *Id.ToString(), *JsonPath);
		MissingIds.Add(Id);
		return false;
	}
	ClipCache.Add(Id, MoveTemp(Clip));
	return true;
}

const FGBPixelVfxClip* UGBPixelVfxSubsystem::FindClip(FName Id) const
{
	if (!LoadClip(Id))
	{
		return nullptr;
	}
	return ClipCache.Find(Id);
}

UTexture2D* UGBPixelVfxSubsystem::GetAtlas(FName Id) const
{
	const FGBPixelVfxClip* Clip = FindClip(Id);
	if (!Clip)
	{
		return nullptr;
	}
	if (TObjectPtr<UTexture2D>* Cached = AtlasCache.Find(Id))
	{
		if (*Cached)
		{
			return *Cached;
		}
	}
	const FString PngPath = VfxDataDir() / Clip->AtlasFile;
	TArray<uint8> PngBytes;
	if (!FFileHelper::LoadFileToArray(PngBytes, *PngPath))
	{
		UE_LOG(LogGBPixelVfx, Warning, TEXT("PixelVfx atlas for '%s' is missing (%s)."), *Id.ToString(), *PngPath);
		MissingIds.Add(Id);
		ClipCache.Remove(Id);
		return nullptr;
	}
	FImage Image;
	if (!FImageUtils::DecompressImage(PngBytes.GetData(), PngBytes.Num(), Image)
		|| Image.Format != ERawImageFormat::BGRA8)
	{
		UE_LOG(LogGBPixelVfx, Warning, TEXT("PixelVfx atlas for '%s' could not be decoded."), *Id.ToString());
		return nullptr;
	}
	const int32 AtlasW = Image.SizeX;
	const int32 AtlasH = Image.SizeY;
	const int32 ExpectW = Clip->Columns * Clip->FrameWidth;
	const int32 ExpectH = Clip->Rows() * Clip->FrameHeight;
	if (AtlasW != ExpectW || AtlasH != ExpectH
		|| Image.RawData.Num() != static_cast<int64>(AtlasW) * AtlasH * 4)
	{
		UE_LOG(LogGBPixelVfx, Warning, TEXT("PixelVfx atlas for '%s' is %dx%d but the clip needs %dx%d."),
			*Id.ToString(), AtlasW, AtlasH, ExpectW, ExpectH);
		return nullptr;
	}
	UTexture2D* Texture = UTexture2D::CreateTransient(AtlasW, AtlasH, PF_B8G8R8A8);
	if (!Texture || !Texture->GetPlatformData() || Texture->GetPlatformData()->Mips.Num() == 0)
	{
		UE_LOG(LogGBPixelVfx, Warning, TEXT("PixelVfx atlas for '%s': could not create a transient texture."), *Id.ToString());
		return nullptr;
	}
	Texture->Filter = TF_Nearest;
	Texture->SRGB = true;
	Texture->NeverStream = true;
	Texture->CompressionSettings = TC_EditorIcon;
	Texture->LODGroup = TEXTUREGROUP_Pixels2D;
	Texture->AddressX = TA_Clamp;
	Texture->AddressY = TA_Clamp;
#if WITH_EDITORONLY_DATA
	Texture->MipGenSettings = TMGS_NoMipmaps;
#endif
	// FColor is B, G, R, A in memory: exactly BGRA8; rows go top to bottom.
	FTexture2DMipMap& Mip = Texture->GetPlatformData()->Mips[0];
	void* Data = Mip.BulkData.Lock(LOCK_READ_WRITE);
	FMemory::Memcpy(Data, Image.RawData.GetData(), Image.RawData.Num());
	Mip.BulkData.Unlock();
	Texture->UpdateResource();
	AtlasCache.Add(Id, Texture);
	return Texture;
}

TArray<FName> UGBPixelVfxSubsystem::ListClipIds() const
{
	TArray<FName> Ids;
	TArray<FString> Files;
	IFileManager::Get().FindFiles(Files, *(VfxDataDir() / TEXT("*.json")), true, false);
	for (const FString& File : Files)
	{
		Ids.Add(FName(*FPaths::GetBaseFilename(File)));
	}
	Ids.Sort([](const FName& A, const FName& B) { return A.LexicalLess(B); });
	return Ids;
}

void UGBPixelVfxSubsystem::ClearCache()
{
	AtlasCache.Reset();
	ClipCache.Reset();
	MissingIds.Reset();
}
// GBPixelVfx runtime module: log category plus the pvfx.Spawn console command (non-shipping).
#include "GBPixelVfxLog.h"

#include "GBPixelVfxActor.h"
#include "GBPixelVfxComponent.h"
#include "HAL/IConsoleManager.h"
#include "Kismet/GameplayStatics.h"
#include "Modules/ModuleManager.h"

DEFINE_LOG_CATEGORY(LogGBPixelVfx);

class FGBPixelVfxModule : public FDefaultGameModuleImpl
{
};

IMPLEMENT_MODULE(FGBPixelVfxModule, GBPixelVfx);

#if !UE_BUILD_SHIPPING
static void PixelVfxSpawnCommand(const TArray<FString>& Args, UWorld* World)
{
	if (!World)
	{
		UE_LOG(LogGBPixelVfx, Warning, TEXT("pvfx.Spawn: no world."));
		return;
	}
	if (Args.Num() < 1)
	{
		UE_LOG(LogGBPixelVfx, Warning, TEXT("pvfx.Spawn usage: pvfx.Spawn <ClipId> [SizeScale]"));
		return;
	}
	const FName ClipId(*Args[0]);
	float SizeScale = 1.f;
	if (Args.Num() > 1)
	{
		SizeScale = FCString::Atof(*Args[1]);
	}
	APlayerCameraManager* Manager = UGameplayStatics::GetPlayerCameraManager(World, 0);
	if (!Manager)
	{
		UE_LOG(LogGBPixelVfx, Warning, TEXT("pvfx.Spawn: no player camera."));
		return;
	}
	const FVector ViewPos = Manager->GetCameraLocation();
	const FVector ViewDir = Manager->GetCameraRotation().Vector();
	const FVector Point = ViewPos + ViewDir * 300.f;
	FVector SpawnAt = Point;
	FHitResult Hit;
	FCollisionQueryParams Params(SCENE_QUERY_STAT(GBPixelVfxSpawn), false);
	if (World->LineTraceSingleByChannel(Hit, Point, Point - FVector(0.f, 0.f, 2000.f), ECC_Visibility, Params))
	{
		SpawnAt = Hit.Location;
	}
	if (!AGBPixelVfxActor::SpawnPixelVfx(World, ClipId, FTransform(SpawnAt), SizeScale))
	{
		UE_LOG(LogGBPixelVfx, Warning, TEXT("pvfx.Spawn: unknown clip '%s'."), *ClipId.ToString());
	}
}

static FAutoConsoleCommandWithWorldAndArgs GBPixelVfxSpawnCmd(
	TEXT("pvfx.Spawn"),
	TEXT("Spawns a baked pixel VFX clip 300 cm in front of the view: pvfx.Spawn <ClipId> [SizeScale]"),
	FConsoleCommandWithWorldAndArgsDelegate::CreateStatic(&PixelVfxSpawnCommand));
#endif
// AGBPixelVfxActor: root component wrapper plus the static spawn helper.
#include "GBPixelVfxActor.h"

#include "GBPixelVfxComponent.h"
#include "GBPixelVfxSubsystem.h"

AGBPixelVfxActor::AGBPixelVfxActor()
{
	PixelVfx = CreateDefaultSubobject<UGBPixelVfxComponent>(TEXT("PixelVfx"));
	SetRootComponent(PixelVfx);
}

AGBPixelVfxActor* AGBPixelVfxActor::SpawnPixelVfx(UObject* WorldContext, FName ClipId,
	const FTransform& Transform, float SizeScale)
{
	UWorld* World = WorldContext ? WorldContext->GetWorld() : nullptr;
	if (!World)
	{
		UE_LOG(LogGBPixelVfx, Warning, TEXT("PixelVfx spawn '%s': no world."), *ClipId.ToString());
		return nullptr;
	}
	FActorSpawnParameters Params;
	Params.SpawnCollisionHandlingOverride = ESpawnActorCollisionHandlingMethod::AlwaysSpawn;
	AGBPixelVfxActor* Actor = World->SpawnActor<AGBPixelVfxActor>(AGBPixelVfxActor::StaticClass(), Transform, Params);
	if (!Actor)
	{
		return nullptr;
	}
	Actor->PixelVfx->SizeScale = SizeScale;
	const UGBPixelVfxSubsystem* Subsystem = World->GetGameInstance()
		? World->GetGameInstance()->GetSubsystem<UGBPixelVfxSubsystem>()
		: nullptr;
	const FGBPixelVfxClip* Clip = Subsystem ? Subsystem->FindClip(ClipId) : nullptr;
	if (Clip && !Clip->bLoop)
	{
		Actor->PixelVfx->bAutoDestroyOwnerWhenFinished = true;
	}
	if (!Actor->PixelVfx->Play(ClipId))
	{
		Actor->Destroy();
		return nullptr;
	}
	return Actor;
}
// Plays one baked pixel VFX clip: a camera-facing quad plus a real point light on a baked track.
#pragma once

#include "Components/SceneComponent.h"
#include "GBPixelVfxTypes.h"

class UPointLightComponent;

UCLASS(ClassGroup = "Glyphbound", meta = (BlueprintSpawnableComponent))
class GBPIXELVFX_API UGBPixelVfxComponent : public USceneComponent
{
	GENERATED_BODY()

public:
	UGBPixelVfxComponent();

	// Starts playing a clip (builds the quad and the light on first use). False when unknown.
	UFUNCTION(BlueprintCallable, Category = "PixelVfx")
	bool Play(FName ClipId, bool bRestart = true);

	UFUNCTION(BlueprintCallable, Category = "PixelVfx")
	void Stop();

	UFUNCTION(BlueprintCallable, Category = "PixelVfx")
	bool IsPlaying() const { return bPlaying && CurrentClip.IsValid(); }

	// Destroy the owner actor when a one-shot clip finishes.
	UPROPERTY(EditAnywhere, BlueprintReadWrite, Category = "PixelVfx")
	bool bAutoDestroyOwnerWhenFinished = false;

	UPROPERTY(EditAnywhere, BlueprintReadWrite, Category = "PixelVfx")
	float PlayRate = 1.f;

	// Multiplier on the clip world size and on the light radius.
	UPROPERTY(EditAnywhere, BlueprintReadWrite, Category = "PixelVfx")
	float SizeScale = 1.f;

	UPROPERTY(EditAnywhere, BlueprintReadWrite, Category = "PixelVfx")
	float LightIntensityScale = 1.f;

	UPROPERTY(EditAnywhere, BlueprintReadWrite, Category = "PixelVfx")
	bool bEnableLight = true;

	// Defaults to the clip's value once Play loads it.
	UPROPERTY(EditAnywhere, BlueprintReadWrite, Category = "PixelVfx")
	bool bLightCastsShadows = true;

	bool bLightCastsShadowsSet = false;

	// When set, replaces the clip facing. "None" (the default) uses the clip value.
	UPROPERTY(EditAnywhere, BlueprintReadWrite, Category = "PixelVfx")
	FName FacingOverride;

	UPROPERTY(EditAnywhere, BlueprintReadWrite, Category = "PixelVfx")
	float StartTimeOffset = 0.f;

	// Quad world origin/extent for diagnostics (bake showcase logging).
	void GetQuadWorldBounds(FVector& OutOrigin, FVector& OutExtent) const;

protected:
	virtual void TickComponent(float DeltaTime, ELevelTick TickType, FActorComponentTickFunction* ThisTickFunction) override;
	virtual void EndPlay(const EEndPlayReason::Type EndPlayReason) override;
	virtual void OnComponentDestroyed(bool bDestroyingHierarchy) override;

private:
	void EnsureBuilt();
	void RefreshClipMaterial();
	void RebuildLight();
	void UpdateFrame(bool bForce);
	void UpdateQuadTransform();
	FVector PivotWorldOffset() const;

	TSharedPtr<FGBPixelVfxClip> CurrentClip;
	FName CurrentClipId = NAME_None;

	UPROPERTY(Transient)
	TObjectPtr<UStaticMeshComponent> Quad = nullptr;

	UPROPERTY(Transient)
	TObjectPtr<UMaterialInstanceDynamic> QuadMaterial = nullptr;

	UPROPERTY(Transient)
	TObjectPtr<UPointLightComponent> ClipLight = nullptr;

	bool bPlaying = false;
	float PlayTime = 0.f;
	int32 CurrentFrame = -1;
	bool bQuadMissingMaterialWarned = false;
};

// AGBPixelVfxBakeGameMode: bake / showcase / compare state machines. See the header for the contract.
#include "GBPixelVfxBakeGameMode.h"

namespace GBPixelVfxBakeLocal
{
	float Saturate(float X) { return FMath::Clamp(X, 0.f, 1.f); }

	bool EnsureDir(const FString& Dir)
	{
		IPlatformFile& Files = FPlatformFileManager::Get().GetPlatformFile();
		return Files.CreateDirectoryTree(*Dir);
	}

	// Bilinear resample of a linear HDR frame to the review cell size, tone-mapped x/(1+x).
	void BlitSourceCell(const FLinearColor* Src, int32 SrcW, int32 SrcH, FColor* Dst, int32 DstW, int32 DstH)
	{
		for (int32 y = 0; y < DstH; ++y)
		{
			const float SrcY = (static_cast<float>(y) + 0.5f) * SrcH / DstH - 0.5f;
			const int32 Y0 = FMath::Clamp(FMath::FloorToInt(SrcY), 0, SrcH - 1);
			const int32 Y1 = FMath::Min(Y0 + 1, SrcH - 1);
			const float TY = FMath::Clamp(SrcY - Y0, 0.f, 1.f);
			for (int32 x = 0; x < DstW; ++x)
			{
				const float SrcX = (static_cast<float>(x) + 0.5f) * SrcW / DstW - 0.5f;
				const int32 X0 = FMath::Clamp(FMath::FloorToInt(SrcX), 0, SrcW - 1);
				const int32 X1 = FMath::Min(X0 + 1, SrcW - 1);
				const float TX = FMath::Clamp(SrcX - X0, 0.f, 1.f);
				const FLinearColor A = Src[Y0 * SrcW + X0] * (1.f - TX) + Src[Y0 * SrcW + X1] * TX;
				const FLinearColor B = Src[Y1 * SrcW + X0] * (1.f - TX) + Src[Y1 * SrcW + X1] * TX;
				const FLinearColor C = A * (1.f - TY) + B * TY;
				Dst[y * DstW + x] = FColor(
					static_cast<uint8>(FMath::Clamp(C.R / (1.f + C.R), 0.f, 1.f) * 255.f + 0.5f),
					static_cast<uint8>(FMath::Clamp(C.G / (1.f + C.G), 0.f, 1.f) * 255.f + 0.5f),
					static_cast<uint8>(FMath::Clamp(C.B / (1.f + C.B), 0.f, 1.f) * 255.f + 0.5f),
					255);
			}
		}
	}

	// Nearest upscale of one baked frame.
	void BlitPixelCell(const FColor* Src, int32 SrcW, int32 SrcH, FColor* Dst, int32 DstW, int32 DstH)
	{
		for (int32 y = 0; y < DstH; ++y)
		{
			const int32 SY = FMath::Min(y * SrcH / DstH, SrcH - 1);
			for (int32 x = 0; x < DstW; ++x)
			{
				const int32 SX = FMath::Min(x * SrcW / DstW, SrcW - 1);
				Dst[y * DstW + x] = Src[SY * SrcW + SX];
			}
		}
	}

	bool SavePng(const FString& Path, int32 W, int32 H, const TArray<FColor>& Pixels)
	{
		if (Pixels.Num() != W * H)
		{
			return false;
		}
		TArray64<uint8> Compressed;
		FImageUtils::PNGCompressImageArray(W, H, TArrayView64<const FColor>(Pixels.GetData(), Pixels.Num()), Compressed);
		if (Compressed.Num() == 0)
		{
			return false;
		}
		TArray<uint8> Bytes;
		Bytes.Append(Compressed.GetData(), static_cast<int32>(FMath::Min<int64>(Compressed.Num(), MAX_int32)));
		return FFileHelper::SaveArrayToFile(Bytes, *Path);
	}
}

AGBPixelVfxBakeGameMode::AGBPixelVfxBakeGameMode()
{
	PrimaryActorTick.bCanEverTick = true;
	PrimaryActorTick.bStartWithTickEnabled = true;
	DefaultPawnClass = nullptr;
	HUDClass = nullptr;
}

void AGBPixelVfxBakeGameMode::ParseCommandLine()
{
	FString BakeArg;
	FString CompareArg;
	if (FParse::Value(FCommandLine::Get(), TEXT("-PvfxBake="), BakeArg))
	{
		RunMode = ERunMode::Bake;
		RunArg = BakeArg;
	}
	else if (FParse::Value(FCommandLine::Get(), TEXT("-PvfxCompare="), CompareArg))
	{
		RunMode = ERunMode::Compare;
		RunArg = CompareArg;
	}
	else if (FString(FCommandLine::Get()).Contains(TEXT("-PvfxShowcase")))
	{
		RunMode = ERunMode::Showcase;
	}
}

void AGBPixelVfxBakeGameMode::BeginPlay()
{
	Super::BeginPlay();
	ParseCommandLine();
	if (RunMode == ERunMode::None)
	{
		return;
	}
	if (RunMode == ERunMode::Bake)
	{
		BakeJobFiles.Reset();
		for (const FString& File : FGBPixelVfxBakeJob::ListJobFiles())
		{
			if (RunArg.Equals(TEXT("All"), ESearchCase::IgnoreCase)
				|| FPaths::GetBaseFilename(File).Equals(RunArg, ESearchCase::IgnoreCase))
			{
				BakeJobFiles.Add(File);
			}
		}
		if (BakeJobFiles.Num() == 0)
		{
			UE_LOG(LogGBPixelVfx, Error, TEXT("PVFX no bake job matches '%s'."), *RunArg);
			FinishRun();
		}
	}
}

void AGBPixelVfxBakeGameMode::Tick(float DeltaSeconds)
{
	Super::Tick(DeltaSeconds);
	switch (RunMode)
	{
	case ERunMode::Bake: TickBake(); break;
	case ERunMode::Showcase: TickShowcase(); break;
	case ERunMode::Compare: TickCompare(); break;
	default: break;
	}
}

void AGBPixelVfxBakeGameMode::FinishRun()
{
	UE_LOG(LogGBPixelVfx, Display, TEXT("PVFX DONE"));
	RunMode = ERunMode::None;
	FPlatformMisc::RequestExit(false);
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------- bake mode ----

FVector AGBPixelVfxBakeGameMode::BakeCameraDirection() const
{
	const float Yaw = FMath::DegreesToRadians(Job.ViewYawDeg);
	const float Pitch = FMath::DegreesToRadians(Job.ViewPitchDeg);
	return FVector(FMath::Cos(Pitch) * FMath::Cos(Yaw), FMath::Cos(Pitch) * FMath::Sin(Yaw),
		FMath::Sin(Pitch));
}

void AGBPixelVfxBakeGameMode::BakeDestroyRig()
{
	if (NiagaraActor)
	{
		NiagaraActor->Destroy();
	}
	NiagaraActor = nullptr;
	NiagaraComp = nullptr;
	NiagaraSystem = nullptr;
	if (CaptureComp)
	{
		if (AActor* CaptureActor = CaptureComp->GetOwner())
		{
			CaptureActor->Destroy();
		}
	}
	CaptureComp = nullptr;
	CaptureRT = nullptr;
	if (ViewCam)
	{
		ViewCam->Destroy();
	}
	ViewCam = nullptr;
	if (KeyLight)
	{
		KeyLight->Destroy();
	}
	KeyLight = nullptr;
}

bool AGBPixelVfxBakeGameMode::BakeSpawnRig(int32 RTWidth)
{
	UWorld* World = GetWorld();
	if (!World)
	{
		return false;
	}
	NiagaraSystem = LoadObject<UNiagaraSystem>(nullptr, *Job.SystemPath);
	if (!NiagaraSystem)
	{
		UE_LOG(LogGBPixelVfx, Error, TEXT("PVFX %s: cannot load Niagara system '%s' (is NiagaraFluids on the command line?)."),
			*Job.Id.ToString(), *Job.SystemPath);
		return false;
	}
	FActorSpawnParameters Params;
	Params.SpawnCollisionHandlingOverride = ESpawnActorCollisionHandlingMethod::AlwaysSpawn;
	NiagaraActor = World->SpawnActor<AActor>(AActor::StaticClass(), FTransform::Identity, Params);
	if (!NiagaraActor)
	{
		return false;
	}
	NiagaraComp = NewObject<UNiagaraComponent>(NiagaraActor, TEXT("Niagara"));
	NiagaraActor->SetRootComponent(NiagaraComp);
	NiagaraComp->RegisterComponent();
	NiagaraComp->SetAsset(NiagaraSystem);
	NiagaraComp->SetRandomSeedOffset(0);
	NiagaraComp->SetAllowScalability(false);
	NiagaraComp->bAutoActivate = false;
	for (const auto& Pair : Job.UserFloats)
	{
		NiagaraComp->SetVariableFloat(Pair.Key, Pair.Value);
	}
	for (const auto& Pair : Job.UserVectors)
	{
		NiagaraComp->SetVariableVec3(Pair.Key, Pair.Value);
	}
	for (const auto& Pair : Job.UserColors)
	{
		NiagaraComp->SetVariableLinearColor(Pair.Key, Pair.Value);
	}
	NiagaraComp->Activate(true);

	const FVector Dir = BakeCameraDirection();
	const FRotator CamRot = FRotationMatrix::MakeFromX(Dir).Rotator();
	const FVector RightV = CamRot.RotateVector(FVector::RightVector);
	const FVector UpV = CamRot.RotateVector(FVector::UpVector);
	const bool bOrtho = Job.Projection.Equals(TEXT("Ortho"), ESearchCase::IgnoreCase);
	const int32 RTW = FMath::Max(16, RTWidth);
	const int32 RTH = FMath::Max(16, FMath::RoundToInt(RTW * Job.FrameHeight / static_cast<float>(FMath::Max(1, Job.FrameWidth))));

	AActor* CaptureActor = World->SpawnActor<AActor>(AActor::StaticClass(), FTransform::Identity, Params);
	CaptureComp = NewObject<USceneCaptureComponent2D>(CaptureActor, TEXT("PvfxCapture"));
	CaptureActor->SetRootComponent(CaptureComp);
	CaptureComp->RegisterComponent();
	CaptureComp->PrimitiveRenderMode = ESceneCapturePrimitiveRenderMode::PRM_UseShowOnlyList;
	if (bLegacyCapture)
	{
		CaptureComp->PrimitiveRenderMode = ESceneCapturePrimitiveRenderMode::PRM_LegacySceneCapture;
	}
	else
	{
		CaptureComp->ShowOnlyComponent(NiagaraComp);
	}
	CaptureComp->CaptureSource = ESceneCaptureSource::SCS_SceneColorHDR;
	CaptureComp->bCaptureEveryFrame = false;
	CaptureComp->bCaptureOnMovement = false;
	CaptureComp->bAlwaysPersistRenderingState = true;
	CaptureComp->bInheritMainViewCameraPostProcessSettings = false;
	CaptureComp->ShowFlags.SetBloom(false);
	CaptureComp->ShowFlags.SetEyeAdaptation(false);
	CaptureComp->ShowFlags.SetMotionBlur(false);
	CaptureComp->ShowFlags.SetTonemapper(false);
	CaptureComp->ShowFlags.SetToneCurve(false);
	CaptureComp->ShowFlags.SetTemporalAA(false);
	CaptureComp->ShowFlags.SetGrain(false);
	CaptureComp->ShowFlags.SetVignette(false);
	CaptureComp->ShowFlags.SetCameraImperfections(false);
	CaptureComp->ShowFlags.SetLensFlares(false);
	CaptureComp->ShowFlags.SetDepthOfField(false);
	if (bOrtho)
	{
		CaptureComp->ProjectionType = ECameraProjectionMode::Orthographic;
		CaptureComp->OrthoWidth = FramingWidthCm;
		CaptureActor->SetActorLocation(FramingCenterCm - Dir * 2000.f);
	}
	else
	{
		CaptureComp->ProjectionType = ECameraProjectionMode::Perspective;
		CaptureComp->FOVAngle = Job.FovDeg;
		const float Dist = (FramingWidthCm * 0.5f) / FMath::Tan(FMath::DegreesToRadians(Job.FovDeg * 0.5f));
		CaptureActor->SetActorLocation(FramingCenterCm - Dir * Dist);
	}
	CaptureActor->SetActorRotation(CamRot);
	CaptureRT = NewObject<UTextureRenderTarget2D>(GetTransientPackage(), NAME_None, RF_Transient);
	CaptureRT->InitCustomFormat(RTW, RTH, PF_FloatRGBA, true);
	CaptureRT->UpdateResourceImmediate(true);
	CaptureComp->TextureTarget = CaptureRT;

	if (Job.bKeyLight)
	{
		KeyLight = World->SpawnActor<ADirectionalLight>(ADirectionalLight::StaticClass(), FTransform::Identity, Params);
		// Style guide: light from the top left as seen by the capture camera.
		const FVector Travel = (Dir - UpV + RightV * 0.8f).GetSafeNormal();
		if (UDirectionalLightComponent* LightComp = Cast<UDirectionalLightComponent>(KeyLight->GetLightComponent()))
		{
			LightComp->SetMobility(EComponentMobility::Movable);
			LightComp->SetIntensity(Job.KeyLightIntensity);
			LightComp->SetLightColor(FLinearColor::White);
		}
		KeyLight->SetActorRotation(Travel.Rotation());
	}

	// The main view must render every frame and must look at the effect (GPU sims do not
	// advance in scene captures): park a camera actor on the capture and make it the view target.
	ViewCam = World->SpawnActor<ACameraActor>(ACameraActor::StaticClass(), FTransform::Identity, Params);
	ViewCam->SetActorLocation(CaptureActor->GetActorLocation());
	ViewCam->SetActorRotation(CamRot);
	if (bOrtho)
	{
		ViewCam->GetCameraComponent()->ProjectionMode = ECameraProjectionMode::Orthographic;
		ViewCam->GetCameraComponent()->OrthoWidth = FramingWidthCm;
	}
	else
	{
		ViewCam->GetCameraComponent()->ProjectionMode = ECameraProjectionMode::Perspective;
		ViewCam->GetCameraComponent()->FieldOfView = Job.FovDeg;
	}
	if (APlayerController* PC = EnsurePlayerController())
	{
		PC->SetViewTarget(ViewCam);
	}
	else
	{
		UE_LOG(LogGBPixelVfx, Warning, TEXT("PVFX %s: no player controller, the main view may not advance the sim."),
			*Job.Id.ToString());
	}
	return true;
}

void AGBPixelVfxBakeGameMode::BakeFillCaptureTicks(bool bPrepassNow)
{
	CaptureAtTicks.Reset();
	CaptureCursor = 0;
	const int32 WarmupFrames = FMath::Max(0, FMath::RoundToInt(Job.WarmupSeconds * Job.SimFps));
	if (bPrepassNow)
	{
		// The framing union covers the warmed-up window only, like the real pass: the
		// transient rise from t = 0 would inflate the box of a looping clip.
		const int32 TotalFrames = WarmupFrames + FMath::Max(1, FMath::RoundToInt(Job.DurationSeconds * Job.SimFps));
		for (int32 Tick = WarmupFrames; Tick < TotalFrames; Tick += 4)
		{
			CaptureAtTicks.Add(Tick);
		}
		CaptureTotalFrames = CaptureAtTicks.Num();
	}
	else
	{
		const int32 Blend = (Job.bLoop && Job.LoopBlendFrames > 0) ? Job.LoopBlendFrames : 0;
		const int32 Total = Job.FrameCount() + Blend;
		CaptureStride = FMath::Max(1, FMath::RoundToInt(static_cast<float>(Job.SimFps) / FMath::Max(1, Job.Fps)));
		for (int32 i = 0; i < Total; ++i)
		{
			// Each sample is scheduled independently so non-divisible rates keep the declared fps.
			CaptureAtTicks.Add(WarmupFrames + FMath::RoundToInt(static_cast<float>(i * Job.SimFps) / FMath::Max(1, Job.Fps)));
		}
		CaptureTotalFrames = Total;
	}
}

bool AGBPixelVfxBakeGameMode::BakeCaptureOne()
{
	if (!CaptureComp || !CaptureRT)
	{
		return false;
	}
	CaptureComp->CaptureScene();
	FTextureRenderTargetResource* Res = CaptureRT->GameThread_GetRenderTargetResource();
	if (!Res)
	{
		return false;
	}
	TArray<FLinearColor> Raw;
	if (!Res->ReadLinearColorPixels(Raw) || Raw.Num() != CaptureRT->SizeX * CaptureRT->SizeY)
	{
		return false;
	}
	FSimFrame Frame;
	Frame.Width = CaptureRT->SizeX;
	Frame.Height = CaptureRT->SizeY;
	Frame.Color.SetNum(Raw.Num());
	Frame.Opacity.SetNum(Raw.Num());
	for (int32 i = 0; i < Raw.Num(); ++i)
	{
		Frame.Color[i] = Raw[i];
		Frame.Opacity[i] = GBPixelVfxBakeLocal::Saturate(1.f - Raw[i].A);
	}
	SimFrames.Add(MoveTemp(Frame));
	return true;
}

void AGBPixelVfxBakeGameMode::BakeAnalysePrepass()
{
	using namespace GBPixelVfxBakeLocal;
	const float TestLevel = Job.AlphaThreshold * 0.5f;
	// The visibility test uses the job's alpha rule: additive effects (fire) stay near A = 1
	// in the capture, so opacity alone would call them invisible.
	const bool bLuminance = Job.AlphaMode.Equals(TEXT("Luminance"), ESearchCase::IgnoreCase);
	const bool bMax = !bLuminance && !Job.AlphaMode.Equals(TEXT("Opacity"), ESearchCase::IgnoreCase);
	const int32 RTW = SimFrames.Num() > 0 ? SimFrames[0].Width : 0;
	const int32 RTH = SimFrames.Num() > 0 ? SimFrames[0].Height : 0;
	int32 MinX = RTW;
	int32 MaxX = -1;
	int32 MinY = RTH;
	int32 MaxY = -1;
	float MaxOpacity = 0.f;
	float MaxLuminance = 0.f;
	for (const FSimFrame& Frame : SimFrames)
	{
		for (int32 y = 0; y < Frame.Height; ++y)
		{
			for (int32 x = 0; x < Frame.Width; ++x)
			{
				const int32 At = y * Frame.Width + x;
				const float Opacity = Frame.Opacity[At];
				const FLinearColor& RGB = Frame.Color[At];
				// Conversion evaluates alpha after Capture.Exposure: the prepass must too,
				// or a raised exposure can make outer pixels visible only in the final pass.
				const float Lum = (RGB.R * 0.2126f + RGB.G * 0.7152f + RGB.B * 0.0722f) * Job.Exposure;
				MaxOpacity = FMath::Max(MaxOpacity, Opacity);
				MaxLuminance = FMath::Max(MaxLuminance, Lum);
				float A = Opacity;
				if (bLuminance)
				{
					A = Saturate(Lum);
				}
				else if (bMax)
				{
					A = FMath::Max(Opacity, Saturate(Lum));
				}
				if (A > TestLevel)
				{
					MinX = FMath::Min(MinX, x);
					MaxX = FMath::Max(MaxX, x);
					MinY = FMath::Min(MinY, y);
					MaxY = FMath::Max(MaxY, y);
				}
			}
		}
	}
	if (MaxX < MinX)
	{
		if (!bLegacyCapture)
		{
			UE_LOG(LogGBPixelVfx, Warning, TEXT("PVFX %s: nothing visible with the show-only list (max opacity %.2f, max luminance %.2f), retrying with the full scene."),
				*Job.Id.ToString(), MaxOpacity, MaxLuminance);
			bLegacyCapture = true;
			BakeDestroyRig();
			BakeSpawnRig(256);
			BakeStep = EBakeStep::WaitReady;
			StepTick = 0;
			return;
		}
		UE_LOG(LogGBPixelVfx, Error, TEXT("PVFX %s: nothing visible (max opacity %.2f, max luminance %.2f), failing the job."),
			*Job.Id.ToString(), MaxOpacity, MaxLuminance);
		BakeLogAndCleanup(false);
		return;
	}
	// The stored pivot must stay valid without clamping: include the projected source
	// origin (the job actor at world 0,0,0) in the framing box before fitting the aspect.
	{
		const FRotator CamRotKeys = CaptureComp ? CaptureComp->GetComponentRotation() : FRotator::ZeroRotator;
		const FVector RightKeys = CamRotKeys.RotateVector(FVector::RightVector);
		const FVector UpKeys = CamRotKeys.RotateVector(FVector::UpVector);
		const float FramingHeightKeys = FramingWidthCm * Job.FrameHeight / static_cast<float>(FMath::Max(1, Job.FrameWidth));
		const FVector RelO = FVector::ZeroVector - FramingCenterCm;
		const float AO = FVector::DotProduct(RelO, RightKeys) / FMath::Max(1.f, FramingWidthCm) + 0.5f;
		const float BO = 0.5f - FVector::DotProduct(RelO, UpKeys) / FMath::Max(1.f, FramingHeightKeys);
		const int32 OPX = FMath::FloorToInt(AO * RTW - 0.5f);
		const int32 OPY = FMath::FloorToInt(BO * RTH - 0.5f);
		MinX = FMath::Min(MinX, OPX);
		MaxX = FMath::Max(MaxX, OPX);
		MinY = FMath::Min(MinY, OPY);
		MaxY = FMath::Max(MaxY, OPY);
	}
	const bool bTouchesEdge = (MinX <= 1 || MinY <= 1 || MaxX >= RTW - 2 || MaxY >= RTH - 2);
	if (bTouchesEdge && !bRetriedWide)
	{
		UE_LOG(LogGBPixelVfx, Warning, TEXT("PVFX %s: the first framing guess is too tight, doubling the width once and retrying."),
			*Job.Id.ToString());
		bRetriedWide = true;
		FramingWidthCm *= 2.f;
		BakeDestroyRig();
		BakeSpawnRig(256);
		BakeStep = EBakeStep::WaitReady;
		StepTick = 0;
		return;
	}
	if (bTouchesEdge)
	{
		UE_LOG(LogGBPixelVfx, Warning, TEXT("PVFX %s: the effect still touches the capture edge after doubling."), *Job.Id.ToString());
	}
	// Pixels to world offsets on the capture plane (center-plane approximation for perspective).
	const FRotator CamRot = CaptureComp->GetComponentRotation();
	const FVector RightV = CamRot.RotateVector(FVector::RightVector);
	const FVector UpV = CamRot.RotateVector(FVector::UpVector);
	const float FramingHeightCm = FramingWidthCm * Job.FrameHeight / static_cast<float>(FMath::Max(1, Job.FrameWidth));
	const FVector OldCenter = FramingCenterCm;
	float MinA = FLT_MAX;
	float MaxA = -FLT_MAX;
	float MinB = FLT_MAX;
	float MaxB = -FLT_MAX;
	for (int32 Corner = 0; Corner < 4; ++Corner)
	{
		const int32 PX = (Corner % 2 == 0) ? MinX : MaxX;
		const int32 PY = (Corner < 2) ? MinY : MaxY;
		const float A = ((static_cast<float>(PX) + 0.5f) / RTW - 0.5f) * FramingWidthCm;
		const float B = (0.5f - (static_cast<float>(PY) + 0.5f) / RTH) * FramingHeightCm;
		MinA = FMath::Min(MinA, A);
		MaxA = FMath::Max(MaxA, A);
		MinB = FMath::Min(MinB, B);
		MaxB = FMath::Max(MaxB, B);
	}
	float BoxW = (MaxA - MinA) * (1.f + 2.f * Job.AutoFramePadding);
	float BoxH = (MaxB - MinB) * (1.f + 2.f * Job.AutoFramePadding);
	// Keep the frame aspect: grow the short side.
	const float Aspect = Job.FrameWidth / static_cast<float>(FMath::Max(1, Job.FrameHeight));
	if (BoxW / FMath::Max(1.f, BoxH) < Aspect)
	{
		BoxW = BoxH * Aspect;
	}
	else
	{
		BoxH = BoxW / Aspect;
	}
	FramingCenterCm = OldCenter + RightV * ((MinA + MaxA) * 0.5f) + UpV * ((MinB + MaxB) * 0.5f);
	FramingWidthCm = BoxW;
	UE_LOG(LogGBPixelVfx, Display, TEXT("PVFX %s: auto-frame %.0f cm at (%.0f, %.0f, %.0f)."),
		*Job.Id.ToString(), FramingWidthCm, FramingCenterCm.X, FramingCenterCm.Y, FramingCenterCm.Z);
	// Real pass with the new framing: destroy and re-spawn the system.
	BakeDestroyRig();
	bRespawned = true;
	BakeSpawnRig(Job.Resolution);
	BakeStep = EBakeStep::WaitReady;
	StepTick = 0;
}

void AGBPixelVfxBakeGameMode::BakeConvertAndWrite()
{
	using namespace GBPixelVfxBakeLocal;
	using namespace GBPixelVfxConvert;

	FSettings Settings;
	Settings.FrameWidth = Job.FrameWidth;
	Settings.FrameHeight = Job.FrameHeight;
	Settings.Fps = Job.Fps;
	Settings.bLoop = Job.bLoop;
	Settings.LoopBlendFrames = Job.LoopBlendFrames;
	Settings.AlphaMode = Job.AlphaMode;
	Settings.AlphaThreshold = Job.AlphaThreshold;
	Settings.ColorMode = Job.ColorMode;
	Settings.HotLuminance = Job.HotLuminance;
	Settings.HotSaturation = Job.HotSaturation;
	Settings.Ramps = Job.Ramps;
	Settings.LevelsPercentile = Job.LevelsPercentile;
	Settings.Gamma = Job.Gamma;
	Settings.Hysteresis = Job.Hysteresis;
	Settings.MinIslandPixels = Job.MinIslandPixels;
	Settings.bFillHoles = Job.bFillHoles;
	Settings.MinColorClusterPixels = Job.MinColorClusterPixels;
	Settings.FadeOutFrames = Job.FadeOutFrames;
	Settings.FadeOutMode = Job.FadeOutMode;
	Settings.Outline = Job.Outline;
	Settings.bLightEnabled = Job.bLightEnabled;

	if (Settings.Ramps.Num() == 0)
	{
		// A job without Ramps uses one default ramp (the FluidFire fire ramp).
		UE_LOG(LogGBPixelVfx, Warning, TEXT("PVFX %s: the job has no palette ramps, using the default fire ramp."),
			*Job.Id.ToString());
		TArray<FColor> DefaultRamp;
		const TCHAR* Hex[] = { TEXT("#4B0F0F"), TEXT("#BC2A12"), TEXT("#F03416"),
			TEXT("#F3610B"), TEXT("#E2DE9C"), TEXT("#F5F3CF") };
		for (const TCHAR* H : Hex)
		{
			FColor Srgb;
			if (GBPixelVfxConvert::ParseHexColor(FString(H), Srgb))
			{
				DefaultRamp.Add(Srgb);
			}
		}
		Settings.Ramps.Add(MoveTemp(DefaultRamp));
	}
	TArray<FWorkFrame> Frames;
	Frames.Reserve(SimFrames.Num());
	for (const FSimFrame& Captured : SimFrames)
	{
		FWorkFrame Frame;
		Frame.Width = Captured.Width;
		Frame.Height = Captured.Height;
		Frame.Color = Captured.Color;
		Frame.Opacity = Captured.Opacity;
		Frames.Add(MoveTemp(Frame));
	}
	ApplyExposure(Frames, Job.Exposure);
	ApplyLoopBlend(Frames, Job.FrameCount(), Settings.LoopBlendFrames, Settings.bLoop);
	TArray<FWorkFrame> Small;
	Small.Reserve(Frames.Num());
	for (const FWorkFrame& Frame : Frames)
	{
		Small.Add(DownsampleFrame(Frame, Settings.FrameWidth, Settings.FrameHeight));
	}
	float Peak = 1.f;
	float HotPeak = 1.f;
	float ColdPeak = 1.f;
	ComputePeaks(Small, Settings, Peak, HotPeak, ColdPeak);
	TArray<FFrameMap> Maps;
	Maps.Reserve(Small.Num());
	for (const FWorkFrame& Frame : Small)
	{
		Maps.Add(MapFrame(Frame, Settings, Peak, HotPeak, ColdPeak));
		if (Maps.Num() > 1)
		{
			ApplyHysteresis(Maps.Last(), Maps[Maps.Num() - 2], Settings);
		}
	}
	if (Settings.bLoop && Maps.Num() > 1)
	{
		ApplyHysteresis(Maps[0], Maps.Last(), Settings);
	}
	for (FFrameMap& Map : Maps)
	{
		RemoveSmallIslands(Map, Settings.MinIslandPixels);
		if (Settings.bFillHoles)
		{
			FillOnePixelHoles(Map);
		}
	}
	// Color clusters, then the one-shot fade-out, both before the outline.
	// The light track is computed after the fade so a faded frame emits less.
	for (FFrameMap& Map : Maps)
	{
		RecolorSmallColorClusters(Map, Settings, Settings.MinColorClusterPixels);
	}
	ApplyFadeOut(Maps, Settings);
	TArray<float> Track;
	float AnchorU = 0.5f;
	float AnchorV = 0.6f;
	ComputeLightTrack(Maps, Settings, Track, AnchorU, AnchorV);
	TArray<TArray<FColor>> Pixels;
	ResolveFrames(Maps, Settings, Pixels);

	// Pivot: project the system origin on the capture plane.
	const FRotator CamRot = CaptureComp->GetComponentRotation();
	const FVector RightV = CamRot.RotateVector(FVector::RightVector);
	const FVector UpV = CamRot.RotateVector(FVector::UpVector);
	const float FramingHeightCm = FramingWidthCm * Job.FrameHeight / static_cast<float>(FMath::Max(1, Job.FrameWidth));
	const FVector Rel = FVector::ZeroVector - FramingCenterCm;
	const float PivotU = 0.5f + FVector::DotProduct(Rel, RightV) / FMath::Max(1.f, FramingWidthCm);
	const float PivotV = 0.5f - FVector::DotProduct(Rel, UpV) / FMath::Max(1.f, FramingHeightCm);

	FGBPixelVfxClip Clip;
	Clip.Id = Job.Id;
	Clip.AtlasFile = Job.Id.ToString() + TEXT(".png");
	Clip.FrameWidth = Job.FrameWidth;
	Clip.FrameHeight = Job.FrameHeight;
	int32 Columns = FMath::Max(1, FMath::CeilToInt(FMath::Sqrt(static_cast<float>(Maps.Num()))));
	while (Columns > 1 && Columns * Job.FrameWidth > 1024)
	{
		--Columns;
	}
	Clip.Columns = Columns;
	Clip.FrameCount = Maps.Num();
	Clip.Fps = Job.Fps;
	Clip.bLoop = Job.bLoop;
	Clip.WorldWidthCm = FramingWidthCm;
	Clip.WorldHeightCm = FramingHeightCm;
	Clip.PivotU = PivotU;
	Clip.PivotV = PivotV;
	if (Job.Facing.Equals(TEXT("Billboard"), ESearchCase::IgnoreCase)) Clip.Facing = EGBPixelVfxFacing::Billboard;
	else if (Job.Facing.Equals(TEXT("Ground"), ESearchCase::IgnoreCase)) Clip.Facing = EGBPixelVfxFacing::Ground;
	else if (Job.Facing.Equals(TEXT("Fixed"), ESearchCase::IgnoreCase)) Clip.Facing = EGBPixelVfxFacing::Fixed;
	else Clip.Facing = EGBPixelVfxFacing::Upright;
	Clip.Glow = Job.Glow;
	Clip.bLit = Job.bLit;
	Clip.Light.bEnabled = Job.bLightEnabled;
	Clip.Light.ColorHex = Job.LightColorHex;
	Clip.Light.Color = Job.LightColor;
	Clip.Light.Intensity = Job.LightIntensity;
	Clip.Light.RadiusCm = Job.LightRadiusCm;
	Clip.Light.bCastShadows = Job.bLightCastShadows;
	Clip.Light.TrackMode = Job.LightTrackMode;
	Clip.Light.AnchorU = AnchorU;
	Clip.Light.AnchorV = AnchorV;
	if (Job.LightTrackMode.Equals(TEXT("Constant"), ESearchCase::IgnoreCase))
	{
		Clip.Light.Track.SetNum(Maps.Num());
		for (float& Entry : Clip.Light.Track)
		{
			Entry = 1.f;
		}
	}
	else
	{
		Clip.Light.Track = Track;
	}
	Clip.SourceSystem = Job.SystemPath;
	Clip.SourceDurationSeconds = Job.DurationSeconds;
	Clip.SourceSimFps = Job.SimFps;
	Clip.EngineVersion = FEngineVersion::Current().ToString();
	Clip.BakedUtc = FDateTime::UtcNow().ToIso8601();

	const FString DataDir = FPaths::ProjectContentDir() / TEXT("Data/Vfx");
	EnsureDir(DataDir);
	const FString PngPath = DataDir / Clip.AtlasFile;
	const FString JsonPath = DataDir / (Job.Id.ToString() + TEXT(".json"));

	const int32 Rows = Clip.Rows();
	TArray<FColor> Atlas;
	Atlas.SetNumZeroed(Columns * Job.FrameWidth * Rows * Job.FrameHeight);
	for (int32 f = 0; f < Pixels.Num(); ++f)
	{
		const int32 Col = f % Columns;
		const int32 Row = f / Columns;
		for (int32 y = 0; y < Job.FrameHeight; ++y)
		{
			for (int32 x = 0; x < Job.FrameWidth; ++x)
			{
				Atlas[(Row * Job.FrameHeight + y) * Columns * Job.FrameWidth + Col * Job.FrameWidth + x] =
					Pixels[f][y * Job.FrameWidth + x];
			}
		}
	}
	if (!SavePng(PngPath, Columns * Job.FrameWidth, Rows * Job.FrameHeight, Atlas))
	{
		UE_LOG(LogGBPixelVfx, Error, TEXT("PVFX %s: could not write %s."), *Job.Id.ToString(), *PngPath);
		BakeLogAndCleanup(false);
		return;
	}
	FString JsonText;
	if (!Clip.SaveToJsonString(JsonText) || !FFileHelper::SaveStringToFile(JsonText, *JsonPath))
	{
		UE_LOG(LogGBPixelVfx, Error, TEXT("PVFX %s: could not write %s."), *Job.Id.ToString(), *JsonPath);
		BakeLogAndCleanup(false);
		return;
	}

	// Review files.
	const FString ReviewDir = FPaths::ProjectSavedDir() / TEXT("PixelVfx") / Job.Id.ToString();
	EnsureDir(ReviewDir);
	const int32 CellW = Job.FrameWidth * 6;
	const int32 CellH = Job.FrameHeight * 6;
	const int32 Gutter = 6;
	const int32 SheetCols = 8;
	const int32 SheetRows = (Pixels.Num() + SheetCols - 1) / SheetCols;
	const int32 SheetW = SheetCols * CellW + (SheetCols + 1) * Gutter;
	const int32 SheetH = SheetRows * CellH + (SheetRows + 1) * Gutter;
	const FColor Bg(0x3A, 0x3A, 0x3A, 255);
	const FColor CellBg(0x16, 0x16, 0x1C, 255);
	TArray<FColor> Sheet;
	Sheet.SetNum(SheetW * SheetH);
	for (FColor& Cell : Sheet)
	{
		Cell = Bg;
	}
	TArray<FColor> Source;
	Source.SetNum(SheetW * SheetH);
	for (FColor& Cell : Source)
	{
		Cell = FColor::Black;
	}
	TArray<FColor> CellBuf;
	CellBuf.SetNum(CellW * CellH);
	for (int32 f = 0; f < Pixels.Num(); ++f)
	{
		const int32 SX = Gutter + (f % SheetCols) * (CellW + Gutter);
		const int32 SY = Gutter + (f / SheetCols) * (CellH + Gutter);
		BlitPixelCell(Pixels[f].GetData(), Job.FrameWidth, Job.FrameHeight, CellBuf.GetData(), CellW, CellH);
		// Baked cells are opaque on a dark background so viewers never show them as white.
		for (int32 i = 0; i < CellBuf.Num(); ++i)
		{
			if (CellBuf[i].A == 0)
			{
				CellBuf[i] = CellBg;
			}
		}
		for (int32 y = 0; y < CellH; ++y)
		{
			for (int32 x = 0; x < CellW; ++x)
			{
				Sheet[(SY + y) * SheetW + SX + x] = CellBuf[y * CellW + x];
			}
		}
		// The matching source frame before conversion (post exposure, tone-mapped, smooth).
		if (SimFrames.IsValidIndex(f))
		{
			BlitSourceCell(SimFrames[f].Color.GetData(), SimFrames[f].Width, SimFrames[f].Height,
				CellBuf.GetData(), CellW, CellH);
			for (int32 y = 0; y < CellH; ++y)
			{
				for (int32 x = 0; x < CellW; ++x)
				{
					Source[(SY + y) * SheetW + SX + x] = CellBuf[y * CellW + x];
				}
			}
		}
	}
	SavePng(ReviewDir / TEXT("sheet.png"), SheetW, SheetH, Sheet);
	SavePng(ReviewDir / TEXT("source.png"), SheetW, SheetH, Source);
	int32 BigFrame = 0;
	for (int32 f = 0; f < Clip.Light.Track.Num(); ++f)
	{
		if (Clip.Light.Track[f] >= Clip.Light.Track[BigFrame])
		{
			BigFrame = f;
		}
	}
	if (Pixels.IsValidIndex(BigFrame))
	{
		const int32 BigW = Job.FrameWidth * 12;
		const int32 BigH = Job.FrameHeight * 12;
		TArray<FColor> Big;
		Big.SetNum(BigW * BigH);
		BlitPixelCell(Pixels[BigFrame].GetData(), Job.FrameWidth, Job.FrameHeight, Big.GetData(), BigW, BigH);
		for (FColor& Pixel : Big)
		{
			if (Pixel.A == 0)
			{
				Pixel = CellBg;
			}
		}
		SavePng(ReviewDir / TEXT("frame_big.png"), BigW, BigH, Big);
	}

	int32 SolidTotal = 0;
	for (const FFrameMap& Map : Maps)
	{
		SolidTotal += Map.SolidCount();
	}
	const float SolidAvg = Maps.Num() > 0 && Job.FrameWidth > 0 && Job.FrameHeight > 0
		? 100.f * SolidTotal / (Maps.Num() * Job.FrameWidth * Job.FrameHeight)
		: 0.f;
	if (SolidAvg < 1.f || SolidAvg > 95.f)
	{
		UE_LOG(LogGBPixelVfx, Warning, TEXT("PVFX %s: average solid coverage is %.1f%% (framing or threshold may be wrong)."),
			*Job.Id.ToString(), SolidAvg);
	}
	UE_LOG(LogGBPixelVfx, Display,
		TEXT("PVFX BAKE %s: frames=%d frame=%dx%d world=%.0fx%.0f cm peak=%.2f solid=%.1f%% colors=%d -> %s"),
		*Job.Id.ToString(), Maps.Num(), Job.FrameWidth, Job.FrameHeight,
		FramingWidthCm, FramingHeightCm, Peak, SolidAvg, CountDistinctColors(Pixels), *PngPath);
	BakeLogAndCleanup(true);
}

void AGBPixelVfxBakeGameMode::BakeLogAndCleanup(bool bOk)
{
	if (!bOk)
	{
		UE_LOG(LogGBPixelVfx, Error, TEXT("PVFX %s: job failed."), *Job.Id.ToString());
	}
	BakeDestroyRig();
	FApp::SetUseFixedTimeStep(false);
	++BakeJobIndex;
	BakeStep = EBakeStep::NextJob;
	StepTick = 0;
}

void AGBPixelVfxBakeGameMode::TickBake()
{
	switch (BakeStep)
	{
	case EBakeStep::StartJob:
	{
		if (BakeJobIndex >= BakeJobFiles.Num())
		{
			FinishRun();
			return;
		}
		if (!Job.LoadFromFile(BakeJobFiles[BakeJobIndex]))
		{
			UE_LOG(LogGBPixelVfx, Error, TEXT("PVFX cannot read job file '%s'."),
				*BakeJobFiles[BakeJobIndex]);
			++BakeJobIndex;
			return;
		}
		UE_LOG(LogGBPixelVfx, Display, TEXT("PVFX baking %s (%s)."), *Job.Id.ToString(), *Job.SystemPath);
		FApp::SetUseFixedTimeStep(true);
		FApp::SetFixedDeltaTime(1.0 / FMath::Max(1, Job.SimFps));
		FramingWidthCm = Job.WidthCm;
		FramingCenterCm = Job.CenterCm;
		bLegacyCapture = false;
		bRetriedWide = false;
		bRespawned = false;
		SimFrames.Reset();
		if (!BakeSpawnRig(Job.bAutoFrame ? 256 : Job.Resolution))
		{
			BakeLogAndCleanup(false);
			return;
		}
		SimTick = 0;
		BakeStep = EBakeStep::WaitReady;
		StepTick = 0;
		break;
	}
	case EBakeStep::WaitReady:
	{
		++StepTick;
		if (NiagaraSystem && NiagaraSystem->IsReadyToRun())
		{
			UE_LOG(LogGBPixelVfx, Display, TEXT("PVFX %s: system ready, running the sim."),
				*Job.Id.ToString());
			// Fresh pass: the prepass and the real pass each simulate from t = 0.
			SimTick = 0;
			CaptureCursor = 0;
			SimFrames.Reset();
			bRunningPrepass = Job.bAutoFrame && !bRespawned;
			BakeFillCaptureTicks(bRunningPrepass);
			BakeStep = EBakeStep::RunSim;
			StepTick = 0;
		}
		else if (StepTick == 1 || StepTick % 600 == 0)
		{
			UE_LOG(LogGBPixelVfx, Display, TEXT("PVFX %s: waiting for the system to compile (%d)."),
				*Job.Id.ToString(), StepTick);
		}
		else if (StepTick > 3600)
		{
			UE_LOG(LogGBPixelVfx, Error, TEXT("PVFX %s: the system never became ready to run."),
				*Job.Id.ToString());
			BakeLogAndCleanup(false);
		}
		break;
	}
	case EBakeStep::RunSim:
	{
		++SimTick;
		if (CaptureCursor < CaptureAtTicks.Num() && SimTick >= CaptureAtTicks[CaptureCursor])
		{
			if (!BakeCaptureOne())
			{
				UE_LOG(LogGBPixelVfx, Error, TEXT("PVFX %s: capture readback failed at sim tick %d."),
					*Job.Id.ToString(), SimTick);
				BakeLogAndCleanup(false);
				break;
			}
			++CaptureCursor;
		}
		if (CaptureCursor >= CaptureTotalFrames && CaptureTotalFrames > 0)
		{
			if (bRunningPrepass)
			{
				bRunningPrepass = false;
				BakeAnalysePrepass();
			}
			else
			{
				BakeStep = EBakeStep::ConvertWrite;
			}
		}
		break;
	}
	case EBakeStep::ConvertWrite:
		BakeConvertAndWrite();
		BakeStep = EBakeStep::NextJob;
		StepTick = 0;
		break;
	case EBakeStep::NextJob:
		BakeStep = EBakeStep::StartJob;
		break;
	}
}
