358: void AGBPixelVfxBakeGameMode::BakeFillCaptureTicks(bool bPrepassNow)
359: {
360: 	CaptureAtTicks.Reset();
361: 	CaptureCursor = 0;
362: 	const int32 WarmupFrames = FMath::Max(0, FMath::RoundToInt(Job.WarmupSeconds * Job.SimFps));
363: 	if (bPrepassNow)
364: 	{
365: 		// The framing union covers the warmed-up window only, like the real pass: the
366: 		// transient rise from t = 0 would inflate the box of a looping clip.
367: 		const int32 TotalFrames = WarmupFrames + FMath::Max(1, FMath::RoundToInt(Job.DurationSeconds * Job.SimFps));
368: 		for (int32 Tick = WarmupFrames; Tick < TotalFrames; Tick += 4)
369: 		{
370: 			CaptureAtTicks.Add(Tick);
371: 		}
372: 		CaptureTotalFrames = CaptureAtTicks.Num();
373: 	}
374: 	else
375: 	{
376: 		const int32 Blend = (Job.bLoop && Job.LoopBlendFrames > 0) ? Job.LoopBlendFrames : 0;
377: 		const int32 Total = Job.FrameCount() + Blend;
378: 		CaptureStride = FMath::Max(1, FMath::RoundToInt(static_cast<float>(Job.SimFps) / FMath::Max(1, Job.Fps)));
379: 		for (int32 i = 0; i < Total; ++i)
380: 		{
381: 			// Each sample is scheduled independently so non-divisible rates keep the declared fps.
382: 			CaptureAtTicks.Add(WarmupFrames + FMath::RoundToInt(static_cast<float>(i * Job.SimFps) / FMath::Max(1, Job.Fps)));
383: 		}
384: 		CaptureTotalFrames = Total;
385: 	}
386: }
387: 
388: bool AGBPixelVfxBakeGameMode::BakeCaptureOne()
389: {
390: 	if (!CaptureComp || !CaptureRT)
391: 	{
392: 		return false;
393: 	}
394: 	CaptureComp->CaptureScene();
395: 	FTextureRenderTargetResource* Res = CaptureRT->GameThread_GetRenderTargetResource();
396: 	if (!Res)
397: 	{
398: 		return false;
399: 	}
400: 	TArray<FLinearColor> Raw;
401: 	if (!Res->ReadLinearColorPixels(Raw) || Raw.Num() != CaptureRT->SizeX * CaptureRT->SizeY)
402: 	{
403: 		return false;
404: 	}
405: 	FSimFrame Frame;
406: 	Frame.Width = CaptureRT->SizeX;
407: 	Frame.Height = CaptureRT->SizeY;
408: 	Frame.Color.SetNum(Raw.Num());
409: 	Frame.Opacity.SetNum(Raw.Num());
410: 	for (int32 i = 0; i < Raw.Num(); ++i)
411: 	{
412: 		Frame.Color[i] = Raw[i];
413: 		Frame.Opacity[i] = GBPixelVfxBakeLocal::Saturate(1.f - Raw[i].A);
414: 	}
415: 	SimFrames.Add(MoveTemp(Frame));
416: 	return true;
417: }
418: 
419: void AGBPixelVfxBakeGameMode::BakeAnalysePrepass()
420: {
421: 	using namespace GBPixelVfxBakeLocal;
422: 	const float TestLevel = Job.AlphaThreshold * 0.5f;
423: 	// The visibility test uses the job's alpha rule: additive effects (fire) stay near A = 1
424: 	// in the capture, so opacity alone would call them invisible.
425: 	const bool bLuminance = Job.AlphaMode.Equals(TEXT("Luminance"), ESearchCase::IgnoreCase);
426: 	const bool bMax = !bLuminance && !Job.AlphaMode.Equals(TEXT("Opacity"), ESearchCase::IgnoreCase);
427: 	const int32 RTW = SimFrames.Num() > 0 ? SimFrames[0].Width : 0;
428: 	const int32 RTH = SimFrames.Num() > 0 ? SimFrames[0].Height : 0;
429: 	int32 MinX = RTW;
430: 	int32 MaxX = -1;
431: 	int32 MinY = RTH;
432: 	int32 MaxY = -1;
433: 	float MaxOpacity = 0.f;
434: 	float MaxLuminance = 0.f;
435: 	for (const FSimFrame& Frame : SimFrames)
436: 	{
437: 		for (int32 y = 0; y < Frame.Height; ++y)
438: 		{
439: 			for (int32 x = 0; x < Frame.Width; ++x)
440: 			{
441: 				const int32 At = y * Frame.Width + x;
442: 				const float Opacity = Frame.Opacity[At];
443: 				const FLinearColor& RGB = Frame.Color[At];
444: 				// Conversion evaluates alpha after Capture.Exposure: the prepass must too,
445: 				// or a raised exposure can make outer pixels visible only in the final pass.
446: 				const float Lum = (RGB.R * 0.2126f + RGB.G * 0.7152f + RGB.B * 0.0722f) * Job.Exposure;
447: 				MaxOpacity = FMath::Max(MaxOpacity, Opacity);
448: 				MaxLuminance = FMath::Max(MaxLuminance, Lum);
449: 				float A = Opacity;
450: 				if (bLuminance)
451: 				{
452: 					A = Saturate(Lum);
453: 				}
454: 				else if (bMax)
455: 				{
456: 					A = FMath::Max(Opacity, Saturate(Lum));
457: 				}
458: 				if (A > TestLevel)
459: 				{
460: 					MinX = FMath::Min(MinX, x);
461: 					MaxX = FMath::Max(MaxX, x);
462: 					MinY = FMath::Min(MinY, y);
463: 					MaxY = FMath::Max(MaxY, y);
464: 				}
465: 			}
466: 		}
467: 	}
468: 	if (MaxX < MinX)
469: 	{
470: 		if (!bLegacyCapture)
471: 		{
472: 			UE_LOG(LogGBPixelVfx, Warning, TEXT("PVFX %s: nothing visible with the show-only list (max opacity %.2f, max luminance %.2f), retrying with the full scene."),
473: 				*Job.Id.ToString(), MaxOpacity, MaxLuminance);
474: 			bLegacyCapture = true;
475: 			BakeDestroyRig();
476: 			BakeSpawnRig(256);
477: 			BakeStep = EBakeStep::WaitReady;
478: 			StepTick = 0;
479: 			return;
480: 		}
481: 		UE_LOG(LogGBPixelVfx, Error, TEXT("PVFX %s: nothing visible (max opacity %.2f, max luminance %.2f), failing the job."),
482: 			*Job.Id.ToString(), MaxOpacity, MaxLuminance);
483: 		BakeLogAndCleanup(false);
484: 		return;
485: 	}
486: 	// The stored pivot must stay valid without clamping: include the projected source
487: 	// origin (the job actor at world 0,0,0) in the framing box before fitting the aspect.
488: 	{
489: 		const FRotator CamRotKeys = CaptureComp ? CaptureComp->GetComponentRotation() : FRotator::ZeroRotator;
490: 		const FVector RightKeys = CamRotKeys.RotateVector(FVector::RightVector);
491: 		const FVector UpKeys = CamRotKeys.RotateVector(FVector::UpVector);
492: 		const float FramingHeightKeys = FramingWidthCm * Job.FrameHeight / static_cast<float>(FMath::Max(1, Job.FrameWidth));
493: 		const FVector RelO = FVector::ZeroVector - FramingCenterCm;
494: 		const float AO = FVector::DotProduct(RelO, RightKeys) / FMath::Max(1.f, FramingWidthCm) + 0.5f;
495: 		const float BO = 0.5f - FVector::DotProduct(RelO, UpKeys) / FMath::Max(1.f, FramingHeightKeys);
496: 		const int32 OPX = FMath::FloorToInt(AO * RTW - 0.5f);
497: 		const int32 OPY = FMath::FloorToInt(BO * RTH - 0.5f);
498: 		MinX = FMath::Min(MinX, OPX);
499: 		MaxX = FMath::Max(MaxX, OPX);
500: 		MinY = FMath::Min(MinY, OPY);
501: 		MaxY = FMath::Max(MaxY, OPY);
502: 	}
503: 	const bool bTouchesEdge = (MinX <= 1 || MinY <= 1 || MaxX >= RTW - 2 || MaxY >= RTH - 2);
504: 	if (bTouchesEdge && !bRetriedWide)
505: 	{
506: 		UE_LOG(LogGBPixelVfx, Warning, TEXT("PVFX %s: the first framing guess is too tight, doubling the width once and retrying."),
507: 			*Job.Id.ToString());
508: 		bRetriedWide = true;
509: 		FramingWidthCm *= 2.f;
510: 		BakeDestroyRig();
511: 		BakeSpawnRig(256);
512: 		BakeStep = EBakeStep::WaitReady;
513: 		StepTick = 0;
514: 		return;
515: 	}
516: 	if (bTouchesEdge)
517: 	{
518: 		UE_LOG(LogGBPixelVfx, Warning, TEXT("PVFX %s: the effect still touches the capture edge after doubling."), *Job.Id.ToString());
519: 	}
520: 	// Pixels to world offsets on the capture plane (center-plane approximation for perspective).
521: 	const FRotator CamRot = CaptureComp->GetComponentRotation();
522: 	const FVector RightV = CamRot.RotateVector(FVector::RightVector);
523: 	const FVector UpV = CamRot.RotateVector(FVector::UpVector);
524: 	const float FramingHeightCm = FramingWidthCm * Job.FrameHeight / static_cast<float>(FMath::Max(1, Job.FrameWidth));
525: 	const FVector OldCenter = FramingCenterCm;
526: 	float MinA = FLT_MAX;
527: 	float MaxA = -FLT_MAX;
528: 	float MinB = FLT_MAX;
529: 	float MaxB = -FLT_MAX;
530: 	for (int32 Corner = 0; Corner < 4; ++Corner)
531: 	{
532: 		const int32 PX = (Corner % 2 == 0) ? MinX : MaxX;
533: 		const int32 PY = (Corner < 2) ? MinY : MaxY;
534: 		const float A = ((static_cast<float>(PX) + 0.5f) / RTW - 0.5f) * FramingWidthCm;
535: 		const float B = (0.5f - (static_cast<float>(PY) + 0.5f) / RTH) * FramingHeightCm;
536: 		MinA = FMath::Min(MinA, A);
537: 		MaxA = FMath::Max(MaxA, A);
538: 		MinB = FMath::Min(MinB, B);
539: 		MaxB = FMath::Max(MaxB, B);
540: 	}
541: 	float BoxW = (MaxA - MinA) * (1.f + 2.f * Job.AutoFramePadding);
542: 	float BoxH = (MaxB - MinB) * (1.f + 2.f * Job.AutoFramePadding);
543: 	// Keep the frame aspect: grow the short side.
544: 	const float Aspect = Job.FrameWidth / static_cast<float>(FMath::Max(1, Job.FrameHeight));
545: 	if (BoxW / FMath::Max(1.f, BoxH) < Aspect)
546: 	{
547: 		BoxW = BoxH * Aspect;
548: 	}
549: 	else
550: 	{
551: 		BoxH = BoxW / Aspect;
552: 	}
553: 	FramingCenterCm = OldCenter + RightV * ((MinA + MaxA) * 0.5f) + UpV * ((MinB + MaxB) * 0.5f);
554: 	FramingWidthCm = BoxW;
555: 	UE_LOG(LogGBPixelVfx, Display, TEXT("PVFX %s: auto-frame %.0f cm at (%.0f, %.0f, %.0f)."),
556: 		*Job.Id.ToString(), FramingWidthCm, FramingCenterCm.X, FramingCenterCm.Y, FramingCenterCm.Z);
557: 	// Real pass with the new framing: destroy and re-spawn the system.
558: 	BakeDestroyRig();
559: 	bRespawned = true;
560: 	BakeSpawnRig(Job.Resolution);
561: 	BakeStep = EBakeStep::WaitReady;
562: 	StepTick = 0;
563: }
564: 
565: void AGBPixelVfxBakeGameMode::BakeConvertAndWrite()
566: {

exec
"C:\\Windows\\System32\\WindowsPowerShell\\v1.0\\powershell.exe" -Command 'Get-Content Glyphbound/Plugins/GBPixelVfx/Source/GBPixelVfxBaker/Private/GBPixelVfxBakeJob.cpp; Get-Content Glyphbound/Plugins/GBPixelVfx/Source/GBPixelVfxBaker/Public/GBPixelVfxConvert.h; Get-Content Glyphbound/Plugins/GBPixelVfx/Bake/Jobs/FluidFire.json' in C:\GameJam
 succeeded in 235ms:
// FGBPixelVfxBakeJob: JSON loader. Reads exactly the keys of the Bake/Jobs files.
#include "GBPixelVfxBakeJob.h"

#include "Dom/JsonObject.h"
#include "Dom/JsonValue.h"
#include "HAL/FileManager.h"
#include "Misc/FileHelper.h"
#include "Misc/Paths.h"
#include "Serialization/JsonReader.h"
#include "Serialization/JsonSerializer.h"
#include "GBPixelVfxConvert.h"

namespace GBPixelVfxJobLocal
{
	bool ParseHex(const FString& Text, FLinearColor& OutColor)
	{
		FColor Srgb;
		if (!GBPixelVfxConvert::ParseHexColor(Text, Srgb))
		{
			return false;
		}
		OutColor = FLinearColor(Srgb);
		return true;
	}

	void ReadFloats(const TSharedPtr<FJsonObject>& Obj, TMap<FName, float>& Out)
	{
		if (!Obj.IsValid())
		{
			return;
		}
		for (const auto& Pair : Obj->Values)
		{
			Out.Add(FName(*Pair.Key), static_cast<float>(Pair.Value->AsNumber()));
		}
	}

	void ReadVectors(const TSharedPtr<FJsonObject>& Obj, TMap<FName, FVector>& Out)
	{
		if (!Obj.IsValid())
		{
			return;
		}
		for (const auto& Pair : Obj->Values)
		{
			const TArray<TSharedPtr<FJsonValue>>* Arr = nullptr;
			if (Pair.Value->TryGetArray(Arr) && Arr && Arr->Num() == 3)
			{
				Out.Add(FName(*Pair.Key), FVector(
					(*Arr)[0]->AsNumber(), (*Arr)[1]->AsNumber(), (*Arr)[2]->AsNumber()));
			}
		}
	}

	void ReadColors(const TSharedPtr<FJsonObject>& Obj, TMap<FName, FLinearColor>& Out)
	{
		if (!Obj.IsValid())
		{
			return;
		}
		for (const auto& Pair : Obj->Values)
		{
			FString Text;
			if (Pair.Value->TryGetString(Text))
			{
				FLinearColor Color;
				if (ParseHex(Text, Color))
				{
					Out.Add(FName(*Pair.Key), Color);
				}
			}
		}
	}

	FVector ReadVector3(const TSharedPtr<FJsonObject>& Obj, const TCHAR* Key, const FVector& Default)
	{
		const TArray<TSharedPtr<FJsonValue>>* Arr = nullptr;
		if (!Obj.IsValid() || !Obj->TryGetArrayField(Key, Arr) || !Arr || Arr->Num() != 3)
		{
			return Default;
		}
		return FVector((*Arr)[0]->AsNumber(), (*Arr)[1]->AsNumber(), (*Arr)[2]->AsNumber());
	}

	double ReadNumber(const TSharedPtr<FJsonObject>& Obj, const TCHAR* Key, double Default)
	{
		double Value = Default;
		if (Obj.IsValid())
		{
			Obj->TryGetNumberField(Key, Value);
		}
		return Value;
	}

	bool ReadBool(const TSharedPtr<FJsonObject>& Obj, const TCHAR* Key, bool Default)
	{
		bool Value = Default;
		if (Obj.IsValid())
		{
			Obj->TryGetBoolField(Key, Value);
		}
		return Value;
	}

	FString ReadString(const TSharedPtr<FJsonObject>& Obj, const TCHAR* Key, const FString& Default)
	{
		FString Value = Default;
		if (Obj.IsValid())
		{
			Obj->TryGetStringField(Key, Value);
		}
		return Value;
	}

	// The FluidFire fire ramp: used when a job has no Ramps array at all.
	TArray<FColor> DefaultFireRamp()
	{
		TArray<FColor> Ramp;
		const TCHAR* Hex[] = { TEXT("#4B0F0F"), TEXT("#BC2A12"), TEXT("#F03416"),
			TEXT("#F3610B"), TEXT("#E2DE9C"), TEXT("#F5F3CF") };
		for (const TCHAR* H : Hex)
		{
			FColor Srgb;
			if (GBPixelVfxConvert::ParseHexColor(FString(H), Srgb))
			{
				Ramp.Add(Srgb);
			}
		}
		return Ramp;
	}

	void ReadRamps(const TSharedPtr<FJsonObject>& Pixel, TArray<TArray<FColor>>& Out)
	{
		const TArray<TSharedPtr<FJsonValue>>* Ramps = nullptr;
		if (!Pixel.IsValid() || !Pixel->TryGetArrayField(TEXT("Ramps"), Ramps) || !Ramps)
		{
			// Absent: keep the caller's defaults.
			return;
		}
		Out.Reset();
		for (const TSharedPtr<FJsonValue>& Ramp : *Ramps)
		{
			const TArray<TSharedPtr<FJsonValue>>* Colors = nullptr;
			if (!Ramp->TryGetArray(Colors) || !Colors)
			{
				continue;
			}
			TArray<FColor> RampColors;
			for (const TSharedPtr<FJsonValue>& Entry : *Colors)
			{
				FString Text;
				if (Entry->TryGetString(Text))
				{
					FColor Srgb;
					if (GBPixelVfxConvert::ParseHexColor(Text, Srgb))
					{
						RampColors.Add(Srgb);
					}
				}
			}
			if (RampColors.Num() > 0)
			{
				Out.Add(MoveTemp(RampColors));
			}
		}
	}
}

TArray<FString> FGBPixelVfxBakeJob::ListJobFiles()
{
	TArray<FString> Files;
	const FString Dir = FPaths::ProjectPluginsDir() / TEXT("GBPixelVfx/Bake/Jobs");
	IFileManager::Get().FindFiles(Files, *(Dir / TEXT("*.json")), true, false);
	Files.Sort();
	for (FString& File : Files)
	{
		File = Dir / File;
	}
	return Files;
}

bool FGBPixelVfxBakeJob::LoadFromFile(const FString& Path)
{
	FString Text;
	if (!FFileHelper::LoadFileToString(Text, *Path))
	{
		return false;
	}
	TSharedPtr<FJsonObject> Root;
	const TSharedRef<TJsonReader<>> Reader = TJsonReaderFactory<>::Create(Text);
	if (!FJsonSerializer::Deserialize(Reader, Root) || !Root.IsValid())
	{
		return false;
	}

	FGBPixelVfxBakeJob Fresh;
	FString IdText;
	if (!Root->TryGetStringField(TEXT("Id"), IdText) || IdText.IsEmpty())
	{
		return false;
	}
	Fresh.Id = FName(*IdText);
	Fresh.Ramps.Add(GBPixelVfxJobLocal::DefaultFireRamp());
	Root->TryGetStringField(TEXT("System"), Fresh.SystemPath);

	const TSharedPtr<FJsonObject>* Sub = nullptr;
	if (Root->TryGetObjectField(TEXT("UserFloats"), Sub) && Sub)
	{
		GBPixelVfxJobLocal::ReadFloats(*Sub, Fresh.UserFloats);
	}
	if (Root->TryGetObjectField(TEXT("UserVectors"), Sub) && Sub)
	{
		GBPixelVfxJobLocal::ReadVectors(*Sub, Fresh.UserVectors);
	}
	if (Root->TryGetObjectField(TEXT("UserColors"), Sub) && Sub)
	{
		GBPixelVfxJobLocal::ReadColors(*Sub, Fresh.UserColors);
	}

	const TSharedPtr<FJsonObject> Capture = Root->GetObjectField(TEXT("Capture"));
	Fresh.Resolution = static_cast<int32>(GBPixelVfxJobLocal::ReadNumber(Capture, TEXT("Resolution"), Fresh.Resolution));
	Fresh.bAutoFrame = GBPixelVfxJobLocal::ReadBool(Capture, TEXT("AutoFrame"), Fresh.bAutoFrame);
	Fresh.AutoFramePadding = static_cast<float>(GBPixelVfxJobLocal::ReadNumber(Capture, TEXT("AutoFramePadding"), Fresh.AutoFramePadding));
	Fresh.WidthCm = static_cast<float>(GBPixelVfxJobLocal::ReadNumber(Capture, TEXT("WidthCm"), Fresh.WidthCm));
	Fresh.CenterCm = GBPixelVfxJobLocal::ReadVector3(Capture, TEXT("CenterCm"), Fresh.CenterCm);
	Fresh.ViewYawDeg = static_cast<float>(GBPixelVfxJobLocal::ReadNumber(Capture, TEXT("ViewYawDeg"), Fresh.ViewYawDeg));
	Fresh.ViewPitchDeg = static_cast<float>(GBPixelVfxJobLocal::ReadNumber(Capture, TEXT("ViewPitchDeg"), Fresh.ViewPitchDeg));
	Fresh.Projection = GBPixelVfxJobLocal::ReadString(Capture, TEXT("Projection"), Fresh.Projection);
	Fresh.FovDeg = static_cast<float>(GBPixelVfxJobLocal::ReadNumber(Capture, TEXT("FovDeg"), Fresh.FovDeg));
	Fresh.WarmupSeconds = static_cast<float>(GBPixelVfxJobLocal::ReadNumber(Capture, TEXT("WarmupSeconds"), Fresh.WarmupSeconds));
	Fresh.DurationSeconds = static_cast<float>(GBPixelVfxJobLocal::ReadNumber(Capture, TEXT("DurationSeconds"), Fresh.DurationSeconds));
	Fresh.SimFps = static_cast<int32>(GBPixelVfxJobLocal::ReadNumber(Capture, TEXT("SimFps"), Fresh.SimFps));
	Fresh.Exposure = static_cast<float>(GBPixelVfxJobLocal::ReadNumber(Capture, TEXT("Exposure"), Fresh.Exposure));
	Fresh.bKeyLight = GBPixelVfxJobLocal::ReadBool(Capture, TEXT("KeyLight"), Fresh.bKeyLight);
	Fresh.KeyLightIntensity = static_cast<float>(GBPixelVfxJobLocal::ReadNumber(Capture, TEXT("KeyLightIntensity"), Fresh.KeyLightIntensity));

	const TSharedPtr<FJsonObject> Pixel = Root->GetObjectField(TEXT("Pixel"));
	Fresh.FrameWidth = static_cast<int32>(GBPixelVfxJobLocal::ReadNumber(Pixel, TEXT("FrameWidth"), Fresh.FrameWidth));
	Fresh.FrameHeight = static_cast<int32>(GBPixelVfxJobLocal::ReadNumber(Pixel, TEXT("FrameHeight"), Fresh.FrameHeight));
	Fresh.Fps = static_cast<int32>(GBPixelVfxJobLocal::ReadNumber(Pixel, TEXT("Fps"), Fresh.Fps));
	Fresh.bLoop = GBPixelVfxJobLocal::ReadBool(Pixel, TEXT("Loop"), Fresh.bLoop);
	Fresh.LoopBlendFrames = static_cast<int32>(GBPixelVfxJobLocal::ReadNumber(Pixel, TEXT("LoopBlendFrames"), Fresh.LoopBlendFrames));
	Fresh.AlphaMode = GBPixelVfxJobLocal::ReadString(Pixel, TEXT("AlphaMode"), Fresh.AlphaMode);
	Fresh.AlphaThreshold = static_cast<float>(GBPixelVfxJobLocal::ReadNumber(Pixel, TEXT("AlphaThreshold"), Fresh.AlphaThreshold));
	Fresh.ColorMode = GBPixelVfxJobLocal::ReadString(Pixel, TEXT("ColorMode"), Fresh.ColorMode);
	Fresh.HotLuminance = static_cast<float>(GBPixelVfxJobLocal::ReadNumber(Pixel, TEXT("HotLuminance"), Fresh.HotLuminance));
	Fresh.HotSaturation = static_cast<float>(GBPixelVfxJobLocal::ReadNumber(Pixel, TEXT("HotSaturation"), Fresh.HotSaturation));
	GBPixelVfxJobLocal::ReadRamps(Pixel, Fresh.Ramps);
	Fresh.LevelsPercentile = static_cast<float>(GBPixelVfxJobLocal::ReadNumber(Pixel, TEXT("LevelsPercentile"), Fresh.LevelsPercentile));
	Fresh.Gamma = static_cast<float>(GBPixelVfxJobLocal::ReadNumber(Pixel, TEXT("Gamma"), Fresh.Gamma));
	Fresh.Hysteresis = static_cast<float>(GBPixelVfxJobLocal::ReadNumber(Pixel, TEXT("Hysteresis"), Fresh.Hysteresis));
	Fresh.MinIslandPixels = static_cast<int32>(GBPixelVfxJobLocal::ReadNumber(Pixel, TEXT("MinIslandPixels"), Fresh.MinIslandPixels));
	Fresh.bFillHoles = GBPixelVfxJobLocal::ReadBool(Pixel, TEXT("FillHoles"), Fresh.bFillHoles);
	Fresh.MinColorClusterPixels = static_cast<int32>(GBPixelVfxJobLocal::ReadNumber(Pixel, TEXT("MinColorClusterPixels"), Fresh.MinColorClusterPixels));
	Fresh.FadeOutFrames = static_cast<int32>(GBPixelVfxJobLocal::ReadNumber(Pixel, TEXT("FadeOutFrames"), Fresh.FadeOutFrames));
	Fresh.FadeOutMode = GBPixelVfxJobLocal::ReadString(Pixel, TEXT("FadeOutMode"), Fresh.FadeOutMode);
	Fresh.Outline = GBPixelVfxJobLocal::ReadString(Pixel, TEXT("Outline"), Fresh.Outline);

	const TSharedPtr<FJsonObject> Light = Root->GetObjectField(TEXT("Light"));
	Fresh.bLightEnabled = GBPixelVfxJobLocal::ReadBool(Light, TEXT("Enabled"), Fresh.bLightEnabled);
	const FString LightHex = GBPixelVfxJobLocal::ReadString(Light, TEXT("Color"), Fresh.LightColorHex);
	FLinearColor ParsedColor;
	if (GBPixelVfxJobLocal::ParseHex(LightHex, ParsedColor))
	{
		Fresh.LightColorHex = LightHex;
		Fresh.LightColor = ParsedColor;
	}
	Fresh.LightIntensity = static_cast<float>(GBPixelVfxJobLocal::ReadNumber(Light, TEXT("Intensity"), Fresh.LightIntensity));
	Fresh.LightRadiusCm = static_cast<float>(GBPixelVfxJobLocal::ReadNumber(Light, TEXT("RadiusCm"), Fresh.LightRadiusCm));
	Fresh.bLightCastShadows = GBPixelVfxJobLocal::ReadBool(Light, TEXT("CastShadows"), Fresh.bLightCastShadows);
	Fresh.LightTrackMode = GBPixelVfxJobLocal::ReadString(Light, TEXT("TrackMode"), Fresh.LightTrackMode);

	const TSharedPtr<FJsonObject> World = Root->GetObjectField(TEXT("World"));
	Fresh.Facing = GBPixelVfxJobLocal::ReadString(World, TEXT("Facing"), Fresh.Facing);
	Fresh.Glow = static_cast<float>(GBPixelVfxJobLocal::ReadNumber(World, TEXT("Glow"), Fresh.Glow));
	Fresh.bLit = GBPixelVfxJobLocal::ReadBool(World, TEXT("Lit"), Fresh.bLit);

	*this = Fresh;
	return true;
}
// Pure pixel-art conversion (namespace GBPixelVfxConvert): no UObject, no engine objects.
// Operates on arrays of full resolution linear frames; every step is unit-testable.
#pragma once

#include "CoreMinimal.h"

namespace GBPixelVfxConvert
{
// Bake settings that drive the conversion. Code defaults are conservative (cleanup passes off);
// the bake jobs pick the real values.
struct FSettings
{
	int32 FrameWidth = 48;
	int32 FrameHeight = 64;
	int32 Fps = 12;
	bool bLoop = true;
	int32 LoopBlendFrames = 6;
	FString AlphaMode = TEXT("Max"); // Opacity, Luminance, Max
	float AlphaThreshold = 0.3f;
	FString ColorMode = TEXT("Split"); // Ramp, Split, Nearest
	float HotLuminance = 0.55f;
	float HotSaturation = 0.35f;
	// Palette ramps, dark to light, as sRGB bytes. Output colors are always one of these.
	TArray<TArray<FColor>> Ramps;
	float LevelsPercentile = 0.99f;
	float Gamma = 1.f;
	float Hysteresis = 0.03f;
	int32 MinIslandPixels = 0;
	bool bFillHoles = false;
	int32 MinColorClusterPixels = 0;
	int32 FadeOutFrames = 0;
	FString FadeOutMode = TEXT("Dissolve"); // Dissolve, Erode
	FString Outline = TEXT("None"); // None, Black, Darkest
	bool bLightEnabled = true;
};

// One working frame: RGB is premultiplied by coverage against black (linear HDR),
// Opacity is the coverage (0..1) before any threshold.
struct FWorkFrame
{
	int32 Width = 0;
	int32 Height = 0;
	TArray<FLinearColor> Color;
	TArray<float> Opacity;
};

struct FMappedPixel
{
	bool bSolid = false;
	uint8 Ramp = 0;
	uint8 Index = 0;
	float Value = 0.f; // levels value v (0..1)
	bool bHot = false;
};

struct FFrameMap
{
	int32 Width = 0;
	int32 Height = 0;
	TArray<FMappedPixel> Pixels;

	int32 SolidCount() const
	{
		int32 Count = 0;
		for (const FMappedPixel& Pixel : Pixels)
		{
			if (Pixel.bSolid)
			{
				++Count;
			}
		}
		return Count;
	}
};

GBPIXELVFXBAKER_API bool ParseHexColor(const FString& Hex, FColor& OutColor);
GBPIXELVFXBAKER_API float PixelLuminance(const FLinearColor& Color);
GBPIXELVFXBAKER_API float PixelSaturation(const FLinearColor& Color);
GBPIXELVFXBAKER_API FVector LabFromLinear(const FLinearColor& Color);
GBPIXELVFXBAKER_API bool IsHotPixel(const FLinearColor& Unpremultiplied, const FSettings& Settings);

// Exposure: RGB *= Exposure (in place).
GBPIXELVFXBAKER_API void ApplyExposure(TArray<FWorkFrame>& Frames, float Exposure);

// Loop cross-blend on the full resolution frames (Frames holds KeepCount + BlendFrames entries).
// After blending the extra entries are dropped. No-op unless bLoop and BlendFrames > 0.
GBPIXELVFXBAKER_API void ApplyLoopBlend(TArray<FWorkFrame>& Frames, int32 KeepCount, int32 BlendFrames, bool bLoop);

// Box average downsample of RGB and Opacity.
GBPIXELVFXBAKER_API FWorkFrame DownsampleFrame(const FWorkFrame& In, int32 OutWidth, int32 OutHeight);

// One levels peak for the whole clip plus the hot/cold re-normalization peaks (Split mode).
GBPIXELVFXBAKER_API void ComputePeaks(const TArray<FWorkFrame>& Frames, const FSettings& Settings,
	float& OutPeak, float& OutHotPeak, float& OutColdPeak);

// Per pixel alpha, levels, hard threshold and palette mapping.
GBPIXELVFXBAKER_API FFrameMap MapFrame(const FWorkFrame& Frame, const FSettings& Settings,
	float Peak, float HotPeak, float ColdPeak);

// Temporal hysteresis of the current frame against the previous mapped frame.
// Skipped for a frame whose solid count differs from the previous frame's by more than 30%.
GBPIXELVFXBAKER_API void ApplyHysteresis(FFrameMap& Current, const FFrameMap& Prev, const FSettings& Settings);

// 4-connected solid islands smaller than MinIslandPixels become transparent. No-op when <= 1.
GBPIXELVFXBAKER_API void RemoveSmallIslands(FFrameMap& Map, int32 MinIslandPixels);

// A transparent pixel whose 4 neighbors are all solid becomes solid with the most common neighbor color.
GBPIXELVFXBAKER_API void FillOnePixelHoles(FFrameMap& Map);

// Every 4-connected region of solid pixels with the same ramp and index that is
// smaller than MinPixels is recolored with the most common color among the solid
// pixels 4-adjacent to the region (ties: the darker one). No-op when MinPixels <= 1.
// Regions are found on the state before the pass (one pass).
GBPIXELVFXBAKER_API void RecolorSmallColorClusters(FFrameMap& Map, const FSettings& Settings, int32 MinPixels);

// Fade-out for one-shot clips (no-op when bLoop or FadeOutFrames <= 0). Over the last
// FadeOutFrames frames progress t goes from 1/(N+1) to N/(N+1). Dissolve removes a solid
// pixel when Bayer4x4(x,y) < t; Erode removes solid pixels within ceil(t*MaxDepth)
// 4-connected steps of a transparent pixel or the frame edge.
GBPIXELVFXBAKER_API void ApplyFadeOut(TArray<FFrameMap>& Maps, const FSettings& Settings);

// Transparent pixels 4-adjacent to a solid pixel become the outline color. Returns false for "None".
GBPIXELVFXBAKER_API bool ResolveOutlineColor(const FSettings& Settings, FColor& OutColor);

// Resolve every frame to final sRGB bytes (palette colors exactly, alpha 0 or 255), with outline.
GBPIXELVFXBAKER_API void ResolveFrames(const TArray<FFrameMap>& Maps, const FSettings& Settings,
	TArray<TArray<FColor>>& OutPixels);

// Per frame energy E = sum of v over solid hot pixels (all solid pixels when not Split);
// track = E / max(E). Anchor = average over frames of the v-weighted centroid (frame UV, V down).
GBPIXELVFXBAKER_API void ComputeLightTrack(const TArray<FFrameMap>& Maps, const FSettings& Settings,
	TArray<float>& OutTrack, float& OutAnchorU, float& OutAnchorV);

GBPIXELVFXBAKER_API int32 CountDistinctColors(const TArray<TArray<FColor>>& Frames);
}

		private static void StageAdditionalDirectoriesFromConfig(DeploymentContext SC, DirectoryReference ProjectContentRoot, StagedDirectoryReference StageContentRoot, ConfigHierarchy PlatformGameConfig, bool bUFS, string ConfigKeyName)
		{
			List<string> ExtraDirs;
			if (PlatformGameConfig.GetArray("/Script/UnrealEd.ProjectPackagingSettings", ConfigKeyName, out ExtraDirs))
			{
				// Each string has the format '(Path="TheDirToStage")'
				foreach (var PathStr in ExtraDirs)
				{
					string RelativePath = null;
					var PathParts = PathStr.Split('"');
					if (PathParts.Length == 3)
					{
						RelativePath = PathParts[1];
					}
					else if (PathParts.Length == 1)
					{
						RelativePath = PathParts[0];
					}
					if (RelativePath != null)
					{
						DirectoryReference InputDir = DirectoryReference.Combine(ProjectContentRoot, RelativePath);
						if (Directory.Exists(InputDir.FullName)==false)
						{
							Logger.LogWarning("Unable to find directory \"{Arg0}\" for staging, retrieved from \"/Script/UnrealEd.ProjectPackagingSettings\" \"{ConfigKeyName}\"", InputDir.FullName, ConfigKeyName);
							continue;
						}
						StagedDirectoryReference OutputDir = StagedDirectoryReference.Combine(StageContentRoot, RelativePath);
						if (bUFS)
						{
							List<FileReference> Files = SC.FindFilesToStage(InputDir, StageFilesSearch.AllDirectories);
							Files.RemoveAll(x => x.HasExtension(".uasset") || x.HasExtension(".umap") || x.HasExtension(".po") || x.HasExtension(".archive") || (SC.DedicatedServer && x.HasExtension(".mp4")));
							SC.StageFiles(StagedFileType.UFS, InputDir, Files, OutputDir);
						}
						else
						{
							SC.StageFiles(StagedFileType.NonUFS, InputDir, StageFilesSearch.AllDirectories, OutputDir);
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}

void USceneComponent::SetWorldScale3D(FVector NewScale)
{
	FVector NewRelScale = NewScale;

	// If attached to something, transform into local space
	if(GetAttachParent() != nullptr && !IsUsingAbsoluteScale())
	{
		FTransform ParentToWorld = GetAttachParent()->GetSocketTransform(GetAttachSocketName());
		NewRelScale = NewScale * ParentToWorld.GetSafeScaleReciprocal(ParentToWorld.GetScale3D());
	}

	SetRelativeScale3D(NewRelScale);
}

APlayerController* AGBPixelVfxBakeGameMode::EnsurePlayerController()
{
	if (UWorld* World = GetWorld())
	{
		if (APlayerController* PC = World->GetFirstPlayerController())
		{
			return PC;
		}
		return UGameplayStatics::CreatePlayer(World, 0);
	}
	return nullptr;
}

void AGBPixelVfxBakeGameMode::BakeDestroyRig()
{
	if (NiagaraActor)
	{
		NiagaraActor->Destroy();
	}
	NiagaraActor = nullptr;
	NiagaraComp = nullptr;
	NiagaraSystem = nullptr;
	if (CaptureComp)
	{
		if (AActor* CaptureActor = CaptureComp->GetOwner())
		{
			CaptureActor->Destroy();
		}
	}
	CaptureComp = nullptr;
	CaptureRT = nullptr;
	if (ViewCam)
	{
		ViewCam->Destroy();
	}
	ViewCam = nullptr;
	if (KeyLight)
	{
		KeyLight->Destroy();
	}
	KeyLight = nullptr;
}

+void UGBPixelVfxComponent::RefreshClipMaterial()
+{
+	// Clip-dependent material state is refreshed on every successful Play so that
+	// switching clips without Stop() picks up the new atlas, variant and glow.
+	if (!Quad || !CurrentClip.IsValid())
+	{
+		return;
+	}
+	const TCHAR* MaterialPath = CurrentClip->bLit
+		? GBPixelVfxComponentLocal::LitMaterialPath
+		: GBPixelVfxComponentLocal::SpriteMaterialPath;
+	UMaterialInterface* Base = Cast<UMaterialInterface>(StaticLoadObject(UMaterialInterface::StaticClass(),
+		nullptr, MaterialPath));
+	if (!Base && !bQuadMissingMaterialWarned)
+	{
+		bQuadMissingMaterialWarned = true;
+		UE_LOG(LogGBPixelVfx, Warning, TEXT("PixelVfx: material %s is missing, falling back to %s."),
+			MaterialPath, GBPixelVfxComponentLocal::FallbackMaterialPath);
+		Base = Cast<UMaterialInterface>(StaticLoadObject(UMaterialInterface::StaticClass(),
+			nullptr, GBPixelVfxComponentLocal::FallbackMaterialPath));
+	}
+	if (!Base)
+	{
+		return;
+	}
+	QuadMaterial = UMaterialInstanceDynamic::Create(Base, this);
+	UGBPixelVfxSubsystem* Subsystem = nullptr;
+	if (UWorld* World = GetWorld())
+	{
+		if (UGameInstance* GameInstance = World->GetGameInstance())
 		{
-			Quad->SetMaterial(0, QuadMaterial);
+			Subsystem = GameInstance->GetSubsystem<UGBPixelVfxSubsystem>();
 		}
 	}
-	RebuildLight();
+	UTexture2D* Atlas = Subsystem ? Subsystem->GetAtlas(CurrentClipId) : nullptr;
+	if (Atlas)
+	{
+		QuadMaterial->SetTextureParameterValue(TEXT("Atlas"), Atlas);
+	}
+	QuadMaterial->SetVectorParameterValue(TEXT("Tint"), FLinearColor::White);
+	QuadMaterial->SetScalarParameterValue(TEXT("Glow"), CurrentClip->Glow);
+	if (Quad)
+	{
+		Quad->SetMaterial(0, QuadMaterial);
+	}

codex
1. **MAJOR — GBPixelVfxComponent.cpp:268 — Original #1 remains broken under inherited scale.** The mesh inherits component scale, but placement uses only clip dimensions × `SizeScale`. A 400 cm bottom-pivot sprite on a scale-2 actor extends 200 cm below its component; set matching world scale or include the mesh’s actual world scale in pivot/light calculations.

2. **MAJOR — GBPixelVfxConvert.cpp:692,785 — Fade-out does not guarantee an empty last frame.** With two fade frames, Dissolve leaves 20 pixels of a solid 8×8 frame; Erode leaves four. This follows the brief’s `N/(N+1)` endpoint but produces a visible pop at Stop; explicitly clear the final frame or append an empty frame before computing lighting.

3. **MINOR — GBPixelVfxConvert.cpp:617 — Color-cluster voting counts shared boundary pixels repeatedly.** For an L-shaped region, one dark pixel touching two members ties two distinct bright neighbors and wins, although bright is the pixel majority. Deduplicate boundary indices before counting votes.

4. **MINOR — GBPixelVfxBakeGameMode_Bake.cpp:382,953 — Original #11 remains wrong when Fps exceeds SimFps.** At SimFps=30/Fps=60, duplicate scheduled ticks are consumed one per simulation tick, stretching the captured interval. Consume all samples due at the same tick, or reject Fps > SimFps.

5. **MINOR — GBPixelVfxBakeJob.cpp:135 — Original #12’s required missing-Ramps warning is absent.** The loader silently preserves its default fire ramp, so the later empty-ramp warning never runs; accidentally omitting Ramps changes the appearance without the requested diagnostic. Warn where the absent array is detected.

6. **MINOR — GBPixelVfxConvertTests.cpp:207 — SplitRouting still lacks independent peak expectations (original #14).** Returning arbitrary unequal hot/cold peaks, then consistently using them in MapFrame, passes because expected values divide by those same returned peaks. Assert hand-calculated peaks—1.2887 hot and 0.2 cold—and resulting levels.

7. **MINOR — GBPixelVfxConvertTests.cpp:465 — LoopBlend’s non-loop case remains ineffective (original #15).** Removing the `bLoop` guard still passes: the fixture has three frames where KeepCount + BlendFrames requires four, triggering the insufficient-input guard. Supply enough extra frames and check that every retained RGB/opacity value stays unchanged.

8. **MINOR — GBPixelVfxConvertTests.cpp:221 — Original #18’s disappearance/coverage regression coverage is still missing.** HysteresisBand uses one always-solid pixel, so restoring disappeared pixels or omitting the rapid-coverage-change bypass passes. Extend it with disappearing pixels and a >30% coverage change whose near-boundary color must change.

9. **MINOR — GBPixelVfxConvertTests.cpp:588 — ColorClusters does not verify region-wide recoloring or voting.** Recoloring only pixel 7, or always choosing the first neighbor, passes its single-surrounding-color fixture. Check both region members and add competing neighbor colors, a darkness tie, and adjacent small regions requiring the original snapshot.

10. **MINOR — GBPixelVfxConvertTests.cpp:623 — FadeOut accepts substantially wrong masks.** Fixed nested thinning masks satisfy Dissolve’s counts/monotonicity, while removing only pixel 1 satisfies Erode’s border assertion. Assert exact Bayer masks and erosion distances, including transparent holes, full-frame edges, and the empty final-frame behavior from finding 2.

11. **MINOR — GBPixelVfxConvertTests.cpp:722 — TrackEnergyAnchor accepts pixel-count lighting.** Every contributing Value equals 1, so counting hot pixels instead of summing their values passes; constant V=0.5 also passes the one-row fixture. Use unequal values, multiple rows, a solid cold pixel, and a transparent hot pixel with independently calculated energy and centroids.

12. **MINOR — GBPixelVfxConvertTests.cpp:533,560 — QuadPivotPlacement and LightAnchorPlacement omit horizontal offsets and scaling.** Implementations that discard both U terms or ignore `SizeScale` pass because both U values are centered and scale is 1. Add asymmetric U coordinates and non-unit scale, plus a component-level inherited-scale regression for finding 1.

GC/destruction and clip switching: no additional finding; replaced MIDs are collectible, and loop wraps allocate none—repeated Play calls cause allocation churn.

Packaging: UE 5.9 source confirms the material always-cook directory and recursive NonUFS Data staging are sufficient; no editor-only runtime API issue found, and `pvfx.Spawn` is compiled out of Shipping.

Opaque review cells, legacy capture fallback, failure-index advancement, and prepass exposure: no additional finding.

Before enabling the plugin in `.uproject`: fix findings **1–2**, with regression coverage; fix **3** before using cluster cleanup on production bakes.
